使用机器学习间接预测和筛选受阻磁性材料#

原题：Indirect prediction and screening of frustrated magnetic materials using machine learning
作者：Regina Burganova、Irina Piyanzina、Ruslan Akhmerov、Alexander Sigov、Maxim Fedorov。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-10-14
⚡ 磁性/铁电🤖 AI/机器学习

📄 摘要（中文翻译）：磁挫败通常通过挫败指数来量化，挫败指数是根据居里-维斯和尼尔温度计算得出的。由于大多数材料无法同时获得这两个量，因此直接预测磁挫败指数仍然具有挑战性。在这里，我们提出了一种机器学习工作流程，用于间接预测挫败指数，并通过独立预测居里-维斯和尼尔温度来筛选潜在的挫败磁性化合物。在我们的工作中，通过 CatBoost 算法实现的基于成分的模型，在实验数据上进行训练，并使用化学信息数据集细分进行优化。稀土氧化物模型在尼尔预测中和稀土模型在居里-…

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：magnetic materials。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

磁挫败通常通过挫败指数来量化，挫败指数是根据居里-维斯和尼尔温度计算得出的。由于大多数材料无法同时获得这两个量，因此直接预测磁挫败指数仍然具有挑战性。在这里，我们提出了一种机器学习工作流程，用于间接预测挫败指数，并通过独立预测居里-维斯和尼尔温度来筛选潜在的挫败磁性化合物。在我们的工作中，通过 CatBoost 算法实现的基于成分的模型，在实验数据上进行训练，并使用化学信息数据集细分进行优化。稀土氧化物模型在尼尔预测中和稀土模型在居里-魏斯预测中获得了最高的预测精度。通过保守的挫败指数纳入整体不确定性，以提高候选选择的可靠性。对大约 67000 种材料项目化合物的筛选确定了 20 种高可信度的受挫磁性候选化合物，其中包括几种实验上已知的受挫磁体和之前未开发的有待进一步研究的氧化物系统。

English Abstract

Magnetic frustration is commonly quantified by the frustration index, calculated from the Curie-Weiss and Neel temperatures. Because both quantities are not simultaneously available for most materials, direct prediction of the magnetic frustration index remains challenging. Here, we propose a machine learning workflow for indirect prediction of the frustration index and consequent screening of potential frustrated magnetic compounds through independent prediction of Curie-Weiss and Neel temperatures. In our work, composition-based models implemented via the CatBoost algorithm, trained on experimental data were optimized using chemically informed dataset subdivision. The highest predictive accuracy was achieved for the rare earth-oxide model in predicting Neel and for the rare earth model in predicting Curie-Weiss. Ensemble uncertainty was incorporated through a conservative frustration index to improve the reliability of candidate selection. Screening of approximately 67000 Materials Project compounds identified 20 high-confidence frustrated magnetic candidates, including several experimentally known frustrated magnets and previously unexplored oxide systems potential for further investigation.

利用机器学习力场对 Ni2MnGa 中马氏体相变的动力学进行建模#

原题：Modelling the dynamics of the martensitic transformation in Ni₂MnGa with machine-learning force-fields
作者：Markus E. Gruner、Yulia Klunnikova、Mike J. Bruckhoff、Konstatin P. Skokov、Olga N. Miroshkina。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-10-14
⚡ 磁性/铁电🤖 AI/机器学习

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：density functional theory、electronic structure、molecular dynamics、magnetism。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

化学计量 Ni2MnGa 和相关 Heusler 合金中的马氏体转变受到电子结构、磁性和晶格动力学之间微妙的相互作用的控制。虽然密度泛函理论（DFT）已经成功地绘制了调制马氏体的能量图，但它在计算上仍然无法捕获相关长度和时间尺度的有限温度动态。在这里，我们通过构建从头开始分子动力学插值的 DFT 训练的机器学习力场 (ML-FF) 来弥补这一差距。我们证明，在 DFT 数据上训练的 ML-FF 可以重现 Ni2MnGa 的关键特征，包括软 TA2 声子和对纳米孪晶马氏体的偏好。使用经典的分子动力学模拟，我们探索了自适应纳米孪晶对奥氏体-马氏体转变的影响，这促进了向前和向后转变的动力学。我们证明，在 T=0 时，DFT 预测的整个转变路径上的负孪晶边界能支持在有限温度下形成有序调制马氏体。我们的结果为具有接近 DFT 精度的磁性形状记忆合金的大规模模拟铺平了道路。

English Abstract

The martensitic transformations in stoichiometric Ni₂MnGa and related Heusler alloys are governed by a delicate interplay between electronic structure, magnetism, and lattice dynamics. While density functional theory (DFT) has successfully mapped the energy landscape of modulated martensites, it remains computationally prohibitive for capturing finite-temperature dynamics across relevant length and time scales. Here, we bridge this gap by constructing DFT-trained machine-learning force-fields (ML-FF) interpolated from ab initio molecular dynamics. We demonstrate that ML-FF trained on DFT data can reproduce key features of Ni₂MnGa, including the soft TA₂ phonon and a preference for nanotwinned martensites. Using classical molecular dynamics simulations, we explore the impact of adaptive nanotwinning on the austenite-martensite transition, which facilitates the dynamics of both, forward and backward transformation. We demonstrate, that the negative twin boundary energy along the entire transformations path predicted by DFT for T=0, supports the formation of ordered modulated martensites at finite temperatures. Our results pave the way for large-scale simulations of magnetic shape memory alloys with near-DFT accuracy.

04

二原子相互作用势数据库支持用于机器学习和材料特性预测的物理信息特征#

原题：Two-atom interaction potential database supporting physics-informed features for machine learning and material property prediction
The Journal of Chemical Physics，2026-10-14
⚡ 磁性/铁电🤖 AI/机器学习
AI×科学交叉8方向匹配8

📄 摘要（中文翻译）：准确预测分子和复杂晶体系统的能量和相关特性仍然是计算材料科学的基石。虽然密度泛函理论 (DFT) 提供了高精度，但其计算成本对于大规模材料而言却令人望而却步。在这项工作中，我们提出了一个全面的、基于第一原理的两原子相互作用势和原子间力数据库，以通过基于物理的机器学习加速属性预测。该数据库通过 GPAW 软件包使用自旋极化 DFT 进行计算，包含由 68 个独特元素生成的所有 2346 个可能的原子对，作为原子间距离的函数。该数据库的实用…

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：density functional theory。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

准确预测分子和复杂晶体系统的能量和相关特性仍然是计算材料科学的基石。虽然密度泛函理论 (DFT) 提供了高精度，但其计算成本对于大规模材料而言却令人望而却步。在这项工作中，我们提出了一个全面的、基于第一原理的两原子相互作用势和原子间力数据库，以通过基于物理的机器学习加速属性预测。该数据库通过 GPAW 软件包使用自旋极化 DFT 进行计算，包含由 68 个独特元素生成的所有 2346 个可能的原子对，作为原子间距离的函数。该数据库的实用性在多体扩展框架中得到了证明，通过设计高度描述性的特征来预测三种不同类型的能源以进行基准测试。首先，该框架用于预测 G2/97 数据库中分子的总能量。其次，对于二维材料的缺陷能量学（IMP2D 数据库），引入局部同心壳势和空间梯度来捕获吸附物缺陷的形成能。最后，对于 QM9 数据库中的分子能量学，我们开发了高级描述符，包括基于势的矩阵、二体键袋和三体射线不对称参数直方图来评估原子化能量。使用核岭回归进行评估，组合的二体和三体特征集在 10 000 个样本子集上产生了出色的测试结果，平均绝对误差为 0.186 eV (R 2 = 0.9994)。该框架展示了利用显式的、数据库导出的势作为能量和相关属性的高保真预测的稳健描述符的力量。

English Abstract

Accurately predicting the energy and relevant properties of molecules and complex crystalline systems remains a cornerstone of computational materials science. While density functional theory (DFT) offers high accuracy, its computational cost scales prohibitively for large-scale materials. In this work, we present a comprehensive, first-principles-derived database of two-atom interaction potentials and interatomic forces to accelerate the property prediction via physics-informed machine learning. Computed using spin-polarized DFT via the GPAW package, the database encompasses all 2346 possible atomic pairs generated from 68 unique elements as a function of interatomic distance. The practical utility of this database was demonstrated within the many-body expansion framework by engineering highly descriptive features to predict three distinct types of energy for benchmarking. First, the framework was applied to predict the total energy of molecules within the G2/97 database. Second, for defect energetics on 2D materials (the IMP2D database), localized concentric shell potentials and spatial gradients were introduced to capture the formation energy of adsorbate defects. Finally, for molecular energetics within the QM9 database, we developed advanced descriptors, including a potential-based matrix, a two-body Bag of Bonds, and a three-body Ray’s asymmetric parameter histogram to evaluate atomization energy. Evaluated using kernel ridge regression, the combined two-body and three-body feature set yielded an outstanding test result with the mean absolute error of 0.186 eV ( R 2 = 0.9994) on a 10 000-samples subset. This framework showcases the power of utilizing explicit, database-derived potentials as robust descriptors for high-fidelity predictions of energy and relevant properties.

用于评估钙钛矿光伏中机器学习的来源分组协议#

原题：Provenance-grouped protocol for evaluating machine learning in perovskite photovoltaics
作者：Qiwei Han、Chi Zhou。arXiv · cs.LG，2026-10-12
⚡ 磁性/铁电🤖 AI/机器学习
AI×科学交叉8方向匹配8

📄 摘要（中文翻译）：钙钛矿太阳能电池的机器学习模型通常使用随机数据分割进行评估，我们通过大量内部重复系统地显示了信息泄漏。由于近三分之二的精选设备与其他设备共享其确切的配方，因此随机分割主要奖励仅仅回顾历史数据，而不是测试真正的物理概括。为了解决这个问题，我们绘制了钙钛矿数据库的机构出处，并建立了严格的分组划分层次结构，逐步隔离重复的设备、出版物和机构。对 13 个模型进行基准测试在所有分裂中建立了新的最先进的性能，并揭示了一个关键的政权转变：虽然树集成通…

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：perovskite。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

钙钛矿太阳能电池的机器学习模型通常使用随机数据分割进行评估，我们通过大量内部重复系统地显示了信息泄漏。由于近三分之二的精选设备与其他设备共享其确切的配方，因此随机分割主要奖励仅仅回顾历史数据，而不是测试真正的物理概括。为了解决这个问题，我们绘制了钙钛矿数据库的机构出处，并建立了严格的分组划分层次结构，逐步隔离重复的设备、出版物和机构。对 13 个模型进行基准测试在所有分裂中建立了新的最先进的性能，并揭示了一个关键的政权转变：虽然树集成通过识别熟悉的配方来主导随机分裂，但它们的排名在真正看不见的机构和未来的发现上崩溃了，而组合感知的神经架构在这些方面表现出色。此外，特征重要性表明，器件架构和制造方法与吸收剂化学物质一样强烈地决定最终效率。这种标准化协议使社区能够将记忆与真正的物理发现分开。

English Abstract

Machine-learning models for perovskite solar cells are typically evaluated using random data splits, which we show systematically leak information through massive internal duplication. Because nearly two-thirds of curated devices share their exact recipe with another, random splits primarily reward the mere recall of historical data rather than testing true physical generalisation. To solve this, we mapped the institutional provenance of the Perovskite Database and established a rigorous hierarchy of grouped splits that progressively isolate duplicate devices, publications, and institutions. Benchmarking thirteen models establishes new state-of-the-art performance across all splits and reveals a critical regime shift: while tree ensembles dominate random splits by recognising familiar recipes, their ranking collapses on genuinely unseen institutions and future discoveries, where composition-aware neural architectures excel. Furthermore, feature importance demonstrates that the device architecture and fabrication methodology dictate final efficiency just as strongly as the absorber chemistry. This standardised protocol empowers the community to separate memorisation from true physical discovery.

06

来自机器学习跳跃轨迹的非绝热极化子动力学#

原题：Nonadiabatic polaron dynamics from machine-learned hopping trajectories
作者：Daniela Mangano、Luca Leoni、Zhongfei Xu、Li-Min Liu、Cesare Franchini。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-10-15
⚡ 磁性/铁电🤖 AI/机器学习
AI×科学交叉8方向匹配8

📄 摘要（中文翻译）：小极化子的形成和运动在能源材料中普遍存在，并影响光诱导过程。极化子传输受热激活晶格动力学和罕见的跳跃事件控制，需要只能通过机器学习分子动力学才能实现的空间和时间尺度，这使得能够在纳秒内对原子轨迹和极化子配置进行广泛采样。此外，标准从头算分子动力学基础的玻恩-奥本海默近似无法描述核运动介导的电子态之间的非绝热耦合，核运动控制着能量交换和激发态弛豫。在这里，我们开发了一个模拟框架，将机器学习辅助的极化子动力学与非绝热分子动力学相结合，以桥接…

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：polaron、molecular dynamics。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

小极化子的形成和运动在能源材料中普遍存在，并影响光诱导过程。极化子传输受热激活晶格动力学和罕见的跳跃事件控制，需要只能通过机器学习分子动力学才能实现的空间和时间尺度，这使得能够在纳秒内对原子轨迹和极化子配置进行广泛采样。此外，标准从头算分子动力学基础的玻恩-奥本海默近似无法描述核运动介导的电子态之间的非绝热耦合，核运动控制着能量交换和激发态弛豫。在这里，我们开发了一个模拟框架，将机器学习辅助的极化子动力学与非绝热分子动力学相结合，以桥接极化子物理的这些互补方面，并确定不同的极化子跳跃机制如何影响激发态动力学。扩大的轨迹空间使我们能够对最初基于静态马库斯理论论证提出的极化子跳跃机制的绝热或非绝热分类提供第一个直接的动力学确认，并将单个跳跃事件直接与非绝热电子跃迁联系起来。在块体 TiO2 中，当极化子引起的带隙态的能量接近导带流形时，会优先发生非绝热跳跃，从而促进电子去激发，并通过朗道-齐纳机制从基态激发。在氧化镁中，空穴极化子跳跃的主要绝热特性抑制了这些效应，从而导致载流子寿命更长并且不存在热激活激发。在金红石 TiO2(110) 表面，跳跃事件比本体中少见，因为多余的电子在次表面区域高度稳定，导致相应更长的去激发时间。

English Abstract

The formation and motion of small polarons are ubiquitous in energy materials and influence photoinduced processes. Polaron transport is governed by thermally activated lattice dynamics and rare hopping events, requiring spatial and temporal scales only attainable with machine-learning molecular dynamics, which enables extensive sampling of atomic trajectories and polaron configurations over nanoseconds. Moreover, the Born-Oppenheimer approximation underlying standard ab initio molecular dynamics cannot describe the nonadiabatic coupling between electronic states mediated by nuclear motion, which controls energy exchange and excited-state relaxation. Here, we develop a simulation framework that combines machine-learning-assisted polaron dynamics with nonadiabatic molecular dynamics to bridge these complementary aspects of polaron physics, and determine how different polaron-hopping mechanisms affect excited-state dynamics. The enlarged trajectory space allows us to provide the first direct dynamical confirmation of the adiabatic or nonadiabatic classification of polaron hopping regimes originally proposed on the basis of static Marcus-theory arguments, and to connect individual hopping events directly to nonadiabatic electronic transitions. In bulk TiO₂, nonadiabatic hoppings occur preferentially when the energy of the polaron-induced gap state approaches the conduction-band manifold, promoting both electronic de-excitation and, via a Landau-Zener mechanism, excitation from the ground state. In MgO, the predominantly adiabatic character of hole-polaron hopping suppresses these effects, leading to longer charge-carrier lifetimes and an absence of thermally activated excitations. At the rutile TiO₂(110) surface, hopping events are rarer than in bulk because the excess electron is strongly stabilized in the subsurface region, leading to correspondingly longer de-excitation times.

中文摘要

小极化子跳跃的微移弹性带 (NEB) 计算要求很高，因为核运动与自洽的局域电子态耦合。在电荷转移区域附近，缓慢的自洽场收敛、局域化切换和竞争性电子解决方案使替代辅助优化变得复杂。我们为此方案开发了高斯过程辅助 NEB 工作流程，并使用自旋极化密度泛函理论以及 SIESTA 的 Hubbard U 校正 (DFT+U ) 演示了金红石 TiO2 中的电子小极化子跳跃。联合能量-力高斯过程 (GP) 与 Matérn 协方差模型推进了弹性带，而活性原子均方根和最大原子位移限制则约束相对于评估配置的替代运动。达到这些限制会触发第一原理采样，而不需要完全代理收敛。使用 11、13 和 15 个图像以及不同的替代设置和 GP 参数化进行的三个独立 DFT 验证的 96 原子 [001] 计算分别产生 0.1645、0.1637 和 0.1640 eV 的势垒，跨越 0.793 meV。在 15 个图像计算中，重复的中心图像采样会在代理优化期间激活单独校准的局部 GP。最终的全频带 DFT 验证得出最大预计 NEB 力为 0.0346 eV/Å。 324 个原子的计算产生了 0.1302 eV 的较低势垒，表明了超胞敏感性并证明了在更大 GP 观察维度下的可行性。该工作流程结合了受控代理外推、目标电荷转移区域插值和独立的 DFT 验证，以获得极化子迁移路径，强调可重复的障碍和全带力收敛。

English Abstract

Nudged elastic band (NEB) calculations of small-polaron hopping are demanding because nuclear motion is coupled to a self-consistent localized electronic state. Near the charge-transfer region, slow self-consistent-field convergence, localization switching, and competing electronic solutions complicate surrogate-assisted optimization. We develop a Gaussian-process-assisted NEB workflow for this regime and demonstrate electron small-polaron hopping in rutile TiO2 using spin-polarized density functional theory with a Hubbard U correction (DFT+U ) with SIESTA. A joint energy-force Gaussian process (GP) with a Matérn covariance model advances the elastic band, while active-atom root-mean-square and maximum-atom displacement limits constrain surrogate motion relative to evaluated configurations. Reaching these limits triggers first-principles sampling without requiring full surrogate convergence. Three independently DFT-verified 96-atom [001] calculations using 11, 13, and 15 images and different surrogate settings and GP parameterizations yield barriers of 0.1645, 0.1637, and 0.1640 eV, respectively, spanning 0.793 meV. In the 15-image calculation, repeated central-image sampling activates a separately calibrated local GP during surrogate optimization. Final full-band DFT verification gives a maximum projected NEB force of 0.0346 eV/Å. A 324-atom calculation yields a lower barrier of 0.1302 eV, indicating supercell sensitivity and demonstrating feasibility at larger GP observation dimension. The workflow combines controlled surrogate extrapolation, targeted charge-transfer-region interpolation, and independent DFT verification to obtain polaron migration pathways, emphasizing reproducible barriers and full-band force convergence.

中文摘要

具有内置电场的二维（2D）极性氧化物为纳米级电子学、光电子学和表面电荷工程提供了有前景的机会。然而，由于非层状氧化物网络结构的巨大复杂性，他们的发现仍然具有挑战性。在这里，我们将逆向结构设计与机器学习原子间势和第一原理计算相结合，以探索二维氧化物构型。使用 Bi2O3 作为代表性系统，我们识别出两种先前未报道的具有四方和斜方对称性的极性单层。两种结构都表现出垂直分离的 Bi 和 O 子层，产生明显的固有极性，贝里相 2D 极化分别为 34.12 和 37.15 pC/m。值得注意的是，o-Bi2O3 单层表现出二维氧化物中最大的面外极化之一。两个单层均表现出动态稳定性、有限温度结构完整性和宽带隙（~3.8 eV）。大极化源于非层状 Bi-O 网络的维数诱导重构，打破了局域配位对称性并产生面外电荷重新分布，而立体化学活性的 Bi 6s2 孤对电子进一步有利于不对称 Bi-O 键合。这些发现表明，维度引起的局部配位对称性破缺是高极化二维氧化物的一种有前途的设计主题，并证明了将逆向设计与机器学习辅助筛选相结合以探索非层状氧化物网络中非常规极性相的效用。

English Abstract

Two-dimensional (2D) polar oxides with built-in electric fields offer promising opportunities for nanoscale electronics, optoelectronics, and surface charge engineering. However, their discovery remains challenging due to the enormous structural complexity of nonlayered oxide networks. Here, we combine inverse structural design with a machine-learning interatomic potential and first-principles calculations to explore 2D oxide configurations. Using Bi2O3 as a representative system, we identify two previously unreported polar monolayers with tetragonal and orthorhombic symmetries. Both structures exhibit vertically separated Bi and O sublayers, giving rise to pronounced intrinsic polarity with Berry-phase 2D polarizations of 34.12 and 37.15 pC/m, respectively. Notably, the o-Bi2O3 monolayer exhibits one of the largest reported out-of-plane polarizations among 2D oxides. Both monolayers exhibit dynamical stability, finite-temperature structural integrity, and wide band gaps (~3.8 eV). The large polarization originates from dimensionality-induced reconstruction of the nonlayered Bi-O network, which breaks local coordination symmetry and produces out-of-plane charge redistribution, while the stereochemically active Bi 6s2 lone pair further favors asymmetric Bi-O bonding. These findings suggest dimensionality-induced local-coordination symmetry breaking as a promising design motif for high-polarization 2D oxides, and demonstrate the utility of combining inverse design with machine-learning-assisted screening to explore unconventional polar phases in nonlayered oxide networks.

页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

我们报告了六方6H-钙钛矿Ba3LnRu2O9 （ Ln = Gd ， Ho ）的综合介子自旋旋转和弛豫（ muSR ）研究，以研究稀土各向异性在4d-4f耦合系统磁基态中的作用。Ba3HoRu2O9在TN1 = 46 K和TN2 = 10 K时表现出两个连续的磁有序，导致复杂的磁基态，而Ba3GdRu2O9在Gd3 + (L = 0) S态离子下，在TN = 14.5 K时主要表现出单一的磁有序。零场（ ZF ）和纵场（ LF ） muSR测量显示，尽管它们的结构相似，但磁性行为却明显不同。Ba3HoRu2O9呈现多尺度不均匀磁态，其特征为双分量指数弛豫、广域分布、不完全LF解耦和持续自旋动力学。MuSR结果确定了46和10 K附近的两个特征磁能量尺度，分别与相关的Ru-Ho相互作用和准静态磁有序性相关。雷德菲尔德分析进一步证明了强大的局部磁场和分布式自旋波动时间尺度，证实了磁不均匀性。机器学习辅助μ子位点和偶极场计算表明，宽场分布自然源于μ子在不同结构位点感应到不同的共存磁畴。相比之下， Ba3GdRu2O9在14.5 K以下表现出相对均匀的反铁磁基态，高斯弛豫与狭窄的静态场分布一致。这些结果表明， Ba3LnRu2O9中的稀土离子是一个强大的调谐参数，通过竞争性的4d-4f磁相互作用，将基态从均匀驱动到复杂、多尺度的不均匀行为。

English Abstract

We report a comprehensive muon spin rotation and relaxation (muSR) study of the hexagonal 6H-perovskites Ba3LnRu2O9 (Ln = Gd, Ho) to investigate the role of rare-earth anisotropy in the magnetic ground state of 4d-4f coupled systems. Ba3HoRu2O9 exhibits two successive magnetic orderings at TN1 = 46 K and TN2 = 10 K, leading to a complex magnetic ground state, whereas Ba3GdRu2O9, with Gd3+ (L = 0) S-state ions, shows primarily single magnetic ordering at TN = 14.5 K. Zero-field (ZF) and longitudinal-field (LF) muSR measurements reveal markedly different magnetic behavior despite their structural similarity. Ba3HoRu2O9 displays a multi-scale inhomogeneous magnetic state characterized by two-component exponential relaxation, broad local-field distributions, incomplete LF decoupling, and persistent spin dynamics. The muSR results identify two characteristic magnetic energy scales near 46 and 10 K, associated with correlated Ru-Ho interactions and quasistatic magnetic ordering, respectively. Redfield analysis further demonstrates strong local magnetic fields and distributed spin fluctuation timescales, confirming magnetic inhomogeneity. Machine-learning-assisted muon-site and dipolar-field calculations show that the broad field distribution naturally arises from muons sensing distinct coexisting magnetic domains at different structural sites. In contrast, Ba3GdRu2O9 exhibits a comparatively homogeneous antiferromagnetic ground state below 14.5 K, with Gaussian relaxation consistent with a narrow static field distribution. These results establish that the rare-earth ion in Ba3LnRu2O9 is a powerful tuning parameter, driving the ground state from homogeneous to complex, multi-scale inhomogeneous behavior through competing 4d-4f magnetic interactions.

页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

我们使用我们之前开发的无标签机器学习管道 citeMing21、Ming23 研究了不同相关函数在重建 spin-1 双线性双二次链相图中的作用，通过双线性和双二次耦合之间的角度 θ 进行参数化，其中自动编码器将 iDMRG 相关矩阵压缩为潜在特征，并通过轮廓分析进行 K 均值聚类将它们分组为相。在没有任何预先标记的情况下，该方案揭示了四个自然相，Haldane相、二聚相、临界三聚相和铁磁相，并准确定位了Takhtajan-Babujian (θ=-π/4)、SU(3)-对称Uimin-Lai-Sutherland (θ=π/4)和Ferro-Dimer (θ=5π/4)转变点；浅层监督细化进一步锐化了相边界。我们发现不同的相关函数在相位识别中发挥着根本不同的作用。 SU(3)-对称相关性单独选择可与四个相相关的四簇分区，而自旋-自旋和二聚体-二聚体相关性必须组合起来才能恢复完整的相图。相反，三聚体-三聚体相关性表现为边际相关性：它本身不足以进行相识别，但与其他相关性结合时可以提高预测相边界的精度。此外，潜在表示和轮廓值揭示了我们与每个相内相关性的 θ 依赖性相关的特征，并且暂时与底层重整化群结构相关。这些结果表明，机器学习不仅可以识别量子相，还可以揭示不同相关函数中编码的物理信息。

English Abstract

We investigate the role of different correlation functions in reconstructing the phase diagram of the spin-1 bilinear-biquadratic chain, parametrized by the angle θ between the bilinear and biquadratic couplings, using our previously developed label-free machine-learning pipeline citeMing21, Ming23, in which an autoencoder compresses iDMRG correlation matrices into latent features and K-means clustering with silhouette analysis groups them into phases. Without any prior labels, the scheme reveals four natural phases, the Haldane, dimerized, critical trimerized, and ferromagnetic phases, and accurately locates the Takhtajan-Babujian (θ=-π/4), SU(3)-symmetric Uimin-Lai-Sutherland (θ=π/4), and Ferro-Dimer (θ=5π/4) transition points; a shallow supervised refinement further sharpens the phase boundaries. We find that different correlation functions play fundamentally different roles in phase identification. The SU(3)-symmetric correlation alone selects a four-cluster partition that can be associated with the four phases, whereas the spin-spin and dimer-dimer correlations must be combined to recover the complete phase diagram. In contrast, the trimer-trimer correlation behaves as a marginal correlation: it is insufficient for phase identification by itself but improves the precision of the predicted phase boundaries when combined with the other correlations. Furthermore, the latent representations and silhouette values reveal signatures that we relate to the θ dependence of the correlations within each phase and, tentatively, to the underlying renormalization-group structure. These results demonstrate that machine learning can not only identify quantum phases but also uncover the physical information encoded in different correlation functions.

TorchSpin：PyTorch 中的可微电子顺磁共振模拟#

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：spin hamiltonian。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

解释电子顺磁共振 (EPR) 谱通常需要重复正向模拟和自旋哈密顿参数的优化。成熟的软件提供了成熟的模拟和拟合方法，但这些计算通常不能作为机器学习工作流程中的可微分组件。我们推出了 TorchSpin，这是一个开源 PyTorch 框架，它将 EasySpin 的组织和数值约定移植到基于张量的计算环境中。涵盖连续波 EPR、ENDOR、脉冲 EPR、自旋动力学、轨迹模拟和磁力测量的代表性计算密切再现了相应的 EasySpin 结果。在匹配的基准测试中，对于九个工作负载中的六个，TorchSpin CPU 执行速度比 EasySpin 更快，尽管性能很大程度上取决于计算。因此，我们重点关注 PyTorch 中自动微分所支持的功能。在 Cu(II) 粉末光谱演示中，通过 TorchSpin 传播的反馈提高了监督卷积神经网络 (CNN) 的质量，而无需在细化过程中使用参数标签。仅使用噪声谱和 TorchSpin 重建损失从随机初始化的 CNN 训练的模型实现了 ≥ 0.99 的中值相似度。在 TorchSpin 生成的库上训练的 CNN 也无需适应即可转移到独立生成的 EasySpin 谱，其性能与 EasySpin 训练的对照相当。经过训练后，CNN 在每个频谱大约 1 毫秒内生成初始参数估计，而使用 GPU 加速在大约 1.3 毫秒内处理一批 512 个频谱。因此，TorchSpin 既提供了已建立的 EPR 计算的经过测试的 Python 实现，也提供了可微分的物理层，用于快速学习推理、谱细化和无参数标签训练。

English Abstract

Interpreting electron paramagnetic resonance (EPR) spectra commonly requires repeated forward simulation and optimization of spin Hamiltonian parameters. Established software provides mature simulation and fitting methods, but these calculations are not generally available as differentiable components within machine-learning workflows. We present TorchSpin, an open-source PyTorch framework that ports the organization and numerical conventions of EasySpin into a tensor-based computational environment. Representative calculations spanning continuous-wave EPR, ENDOR, pulse EPR, spin dynamics, trajectory simulation, and magnetometry closely reproduced corresponding EasySpin results. In matched benchmarks, TorchSpin CPU execution was faster than EasySpin for six of nine workloads although performance depended strongly on the calculation. We therefore focused on the capabilities enabled by automatic differentiation in PyTorch. In a Cu(II) powder-spectrum demonstration, feedback propagated through TorchSpin improved the quality of a supervised convolutional neural network (CNN) without using parameter labels during refinement. Models trained from randomly initialized CNNs using only noisy spectra and TorchSpin reconstruction loss achieved median similarities of ≥ 0.99. CNNs trained on TorchSpin-generated libraries also transferred without adaptation to independently generated EasySpin spectra with performance comparable to EasySpin-trained controls. Once trained, the CNN produced initial parameter estimates in approximately 1 ms per spectrum while a batch of 512 was processed in approximately 1.3 ms with GPU acceleration. TorchSpin therefore provides both a tested Python implementation of established EPR calculations and a differentiable physical layer for rapid learned inference, spectral refinement, and parameter-label-free training.

使用复值变分自动编码器从光谱诱发偏振数据学习弛豫时间分布#

原题：Learning relaxation time distributions from spectral induced polarization data with a complex-valued variational autoencoder
作者：Charles L. Bérubé、Sébastien Gagnon、Lahiru M. A. Nagasingha、Jean-Luc Gagnon、E. Rachel Kenko、Reza Ghanati 等 7 人。arXiv · physics，2026-10-16

页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

光谱诱发极化 (SIP) 是一种用于表征地下材料的地球物理方法。它通过施加交流电来测量岩石和土壤的频率相关复电阻率。 Debye 和 Warburg 分解是分析和解释 SIP 数据的标准方法，因为它们可以估计岩土材料的弛豫时间分布 (RTD)。然而，传统的分解方法独立地处理测量，尽管 SIP 数据具有复值性质，但仍然在实值空间中工作，并且提供有限的不确定性量化。这些限制降低了异构数据集上传统分解的有效性。我们将分解方法重新表述为无监督机器学习问题，并引入条件变分自动编码器 (CVAE)，该编码器学习从 SIP 数据集到连续 RTD 的共享映射。该模型在包含 140 个颗粒混合物、沉积岩和火成岩以及胶结材料的实验室和现场 SIP 测量的数据集上进行了验证。 CVAE 在复值数据空间中运行，电阻率虚部和相位分量的重建误差分别为 0.53% 和 0.27%，在相应的 p 值为 0.007 和 0.06 时，有证据表明比传统方法有所改进。推断的 RTD 稳定且物理一致，其总荷电率和平均弛豫时间分别与可极化晶粒含量和晶粒尺寸相关，决定系数高达 0.98。该方法的另一个贡献是学习的潜在表示，它将 SIP 频谱组织到结构化空间中。相对于传统 RTD 参数，该空间二维投影中的无监督聚类将轮廓得分提高了近两倍。

English Abstract

Spectral induced polarization (SIP) is a geophysical method used to characterize subsurface materials. It measures the frequency-dependent complex electrical resistivity of rocks and soils through the application of an alternating current. Debye and Warburg decompositions are standard methods for analyzing and interpreting SIP data, as they allow estimation of the relaxation time distribution (RTD) of geomaterials. However, conventional decomposition approaches treat measurements independently, work in real-valued spaces despite the complex-valued nature of SIP data, and provide limited uncertainty quantification. These limitations reduce the effectiveness of conventional decomposition on heterogeneous datasets. We reformulate the decomposition approach as an unsupervised machine learning problem and introduce a conditional variational autoencoder (CVAE) that learns a shared mapping from SIP datasets to continuous RTDs. The model is validated on a dataset comprising 140 laboratory and field SIP measurements of granular mixtures, sedimentary and igneous rock, and cementitious materials. The CVAE operates in complex-valued data space and achieves reconstruction errors of 0.53 % and 0.27 % for the imaginary and phase components of resistivity, respectively, with evidence of improvement over conventional methods at corresponding p-values of 0.007 and 0.06. The inferred RTDs are stable and physically consistent, and their total chargeability and mean relaxation time correlate with polarizable grain content and grain size, respectively, with coefficients of determination up to 0.98. An additional contribution of the proposed method is the learned latent representation, which organizes SIP spectra into a structured space. Unsupervised clustering in a two-dimensional projection of this space improves the silhouette score by nearly a factor of two relative to conventional RTD parameters.

SIEVE：克服等变自监督学习中的拓扑障碍#

原题：SIEVE: Overcoming Topological Obstruction in Equivariant Self-Supervised Learning
作者：Jongann Lee、Sun Woo Park、Yun Young Choi。arXiv · cs.LG，2026-10-16
⚡ 磁性/铁电🤖 AI/机器学习
AI×科学交叉8方向匹配0

📄 摘要（中文翻译）：我们发现 3D 点云掩码自动编码和 SE(3) 等变网络之间理论上不兼容。我们证明标准掩蔽会触发拓扑崩溃，有效地迫使模型在没有参考系的情况下产生幻觉方向。为了解决这个问题，我们引入了 SIEVE（通过等方差进行标量不变提取），这是一种 SE(3) 等变自监督框架，可将点云几何压缩为不变标量嵌入。其核心是球形投影掩模，它保留全局姿态，同时混淆局部语义，避开构造造成的障碍。实验证实，SIEVE 避免了常数掩模和噪声掩模失败时发生的预测崩溃。…

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

我们发现 3D 点云掩码自动编码和 SE(3) 等变网络之间理论上不兼容。我们证明标准掩蔽会触发拓扑崩溃，有效地迫使模型在没有参考系的情况下产生幻觉方向。为了解决这个问题，我们引入了 SIEVE（通过等方差进行标量不变提取），这是一种 SE(3) 等变自监督框架，可将点云几何压缩为不变标量嵌入。其核心是球形投影掩模，它保留全局姿态，同时混淆局部语义，避开构造造成的障碍。实验证实，SIEVE 避免了常数掩模和噪声掩模失败时发生的预测崩溃。在下游点云任务中，生成的嵌入优于基于坐标的基线和手工制作的几何描述符，在泛化到不可见的形状类别方面获得了最大的收益。

English Abstract

We identify a theoretical incompatibility between 3D point cloud masked autoencoding and SE(3)-equivariant networks. We prove that standard masking triggers a topological collapse, effectively forcing the model to hallucinate orientation without a reference frame. To resolve this, we introduce SIEVE (Scalar Invariant Extraction Via Equivariance), an SE(3)-equivariant self-supervised framework that compresses point cloud geometry into invariant scalar embeddings. At its core is the spherical projection mask, which preserves global pose while obfuscating local semantics, sidestepping the obstruction by construction. Experiments confirm that SIEVE circumvents the predicted collapse where constant and noise masks fail. On downstream point cloud tasks, the resulting embeddings improve over coordinate-based baselines and hand-crafted geometric descriptors, with the largest gains on generalization to unseen shape categories.

磁性胶体的机器学习拓扑传输#

原题：Machine-learned topological transport of magnetic colloids
作者：Sebastian Wohlrab、Aneena Rinu Perayil、Piotr Kuświk、Maciej Urbaniak、Feliks Stobiecki、Arne J. Vereijken 等 9 人。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-10-14
⚡ 磁性/铁电🤖 AI/机器学习
AI×科学交叉8方向匹配0

📄 摘要（中文翻译）：通过调节均匀磁场的方向，可以在磁性图案上方传输磁性微粒。在周期性图案中，驱动控制回路缠绕在由图案的对称性决定的特殊方向上，从而诱导一个晶胞的传输。传输受到拓扑保护，其轨迹由围绕特殊方向的控制环的缠绕数控制。在非周期性模式中，特殊方向是位置相关的，排除了使用一小组缠绕数来对传输进行全局表征。我们在这里使用经过布朗动力学模拟数据训练的强化学习代理，该代理使用磁性模式的局部环境来决定控制回路上的动作。该代理直接从粒子响应中自主发现拓扑传输协议…

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

通过调节均匀磁场的方向，可以在磁性图案上方传输磁性微粒。在周期性图案中，驱动控制回路缠绕在由图案的对称性决定的特殊方向上，从而诱导一个晶胞的传输。传输受到拓扑保护，其轨迹由围绕特殊方向的控制环的缠绕数控制。在非周期性模式中，特殊方向是位置相关的，排除了使用一小组缠绕数来对传输进行全局表征。我们在这里使用经过布朗动力学模拟数据训练的强化学习代理，该代理使用磁性模式的局部环境来决定控制回路上的动作。该代理直接从粒子响应中自主发现拓扑传输协议，而无需事先表征磁势景观。仅在复杂的非周期性模式上训练的代理可以直接推广到完全看不见的周期性、准晶体和无序基质。此外，该代理动态调整控制环路，以跨晶格缺陷传输粒子，并平衡可能导致静态拓扑环路失效的外部漂移力。通过在模型中添加寻路算法，我们还实现了多个相同胶体沿着不同的按需轨迹同时、独立的传输。我们证明，控制回路可以直接转移到实验装置中，以成功驱动磁性微粒穿过非均匀磁性薄膜。

English Abstract

By modulating the orientation of a uniform magnetic field it is possible to transport magnetic microparticles above magnetic patterns. In periodic patterns, driving control loops that wind around special orientations, which are dictated by the symmetry of the pattern, induce transport by one unit cell. The transport is topologically protected with trajectories controlled by the winding numbers of the control loop around the special orientations. In non-periodic patterns, the special orientations are position dependent, precluding a global characterization of the transport using a small set of winding numbers. We use here a reinforcement learning agent trained with Brownian dynamics simulation data that uses the local environment of the magnetic pattern to decide an action over the control loop. The agent autonomously discovers topological transport protocols directly from the particle response without prior characterization of the magnetic potential landscape. An agent trained solely on a complex non-periodic pattern directly generalizes to completely unseen periodic, quasicrystalline, and disordered substrates. Moreover, the agent dynamically adjusts the control loop to transport particles across lattice defects and to counterbalance external drift forces that would cause static topological loops to fail. By adding a pathfinding algorithm to the model, we additionally achieve simultaneous, independent transport of multiple identical colloids along different on demand trajectories. We demonstrate that the control loops can be directly transferred to an experimental setup to successfully drive magnetic microparticles across a non-uniform magnetic thin film.

来自多值机器学习偶极模型的电场驱动液体和固体核动力学#

原题：Electric-Field Driven Nuclear Dynamics of Liquids and Solids from a Multi-Valued Machine-Learned Dipolar Model
作者：Elia Stocco、Christian Carbogno、Mariana Rossi。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-10-12

📄 摘要（中文翻译）：由于可以评估具有所需特性的新的或亚稳态的材料相，因此外部电场驱动振动运动是人们持续关注的话题。在这里，我们将电偶极近似中的从头算分子动力学与机器学习神经网络 (NN) 相结合，开发出一种通用、高效且准确的方法来对分子、固体和液体执行电场驱动的核动力学。我们针对原子间势和偶极子训练等变和自微神经网络，修改模型基础设施以考虑后者在周期系统中的多值性质。我们通过具有量子力学精度的纳秒长分子动力学模拟来解决极性液体和极性固体中电场相互作用引起的…

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：interatomic potential、molecular dynamics、phase transition、ferroelectric。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

由于可以评估具有所需特性的新的或亚稳态的材料相，因此外部电场驱动振动运动是人们持续关注的话题。在这里，我们将电偶极近似中的从头算分子动力学与机器学习神经网络 (NN) 相结合，开发出一种通用、高效且准确的方法来对分子、固体和液体执行电场驱动的核动力学。我们针对原子间势和偶极子训练等变和自微神经网络，修改模型基础设施以考虑后者在周期系统中的多值性质。我们通过具有量子力学精度的纳秒长分子动力学模拟来解决极性液体和极性固体中电场相互作用引起的性质变化，从而展示了该方法。对于液态水，我们对 GHz 至 THz 范围内的介电函数和电冻结转变进行了计算，表明核量子效应增强了这种现象。对于铁电钙钛矿LiNbO₃，我们模拟了铁电到顺电的相变以及与偏振切换机制相关的驱动声子模式的非平衡动力学，表明在模拟中没有实现完全的偏振切换。

English Abstract

The driving of vibrational motion by external electric fields is a topic of continued interest, due to the possibility of assessing new or metastable material phases with desirable properties. Here, we combine ab initio molecular dynamics within the electric-dipole approximation with machine-learning neural networks (NNs) to develop a general, efficient and accurate method to perform electric-field-driven nuclear dynamics for molecules, solids, and liquids. We train equivariant and autodifferentiable NNs for the interatomic potential and the dipole, modifying the model infrastructure to account for the multi-valued nature of the latter in periodic systems. We showcase the method by addressing property modifications induced by electric field interactions in a polar liquid and a polar solid from nanosecond-long molecular dynamics simulations with quantum-mechanical accuracy. For liquid water, we present a calculation of the dielectric function in the GHz to THz range and the electrofreezing transition, showing that nuclear quantum effects enhance this phenomenon. For the ferroelectric perovskite LiNbO₃, we simulate the ferroelectric to paraelectric phase transition and the non-equilibrium dynamics of driven phonon modes related to the polarization switching mechanisms, showing that a full polarization switch is not achieved in the simulations.

使用神经网络势理论研究 SrTiO₃ 中的氧空位扩散#

原题：Theoretical Investigation of Oxygen-Vacancy Diffusion in SrTiO₃ using Neural Network Potentials
作者：Kazutaka Nishiguchi、Ryota Yamamoto、Seiichiro Ten-no。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-10-14

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：neural network potential、density functional theory、molecular dynamics、point defects。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

氧空位的形成和扩散在决定 SrTiO₃ (STO) 的电子和原子传输特性方面发挥着重要作用。对此类点缺陷的精确分析需要足够大规模和长时间的分子动力学（MD）模拟；然而，由于计算成本较高，使用基于密度泛函理论 (DFT) 的从头MD (AIMD) 直接研究氧空位动力学仅限于相对较小的系统和较短的时间尺度。在这项研究中，我们使用 AIMD 模拟生成的参考数据集开发了用于原始缺氧 STO 的神经网络潜力 (NNP)。经过训练的 NNP 模型准确地再现了原始 STO 和包含氧空位的 STO 的 DFT 参考能量、原子力和维里尔。 NNP 还再现了总能量和氧空位形成能的超晶胞尺寸依赖性，包括外推到稀极限的形成能。然后使用 NNP (NNP-MD) 进行大规模和长时间的 MD 模拟，以研究氧空位扩散。与传统经典 MD 模拟获得的氧空位扩散系数相比，所得氧空位扩散系数与实验数据的吻合程度要好得多。阿伦尼乌斯分析得出氧空位迁移焓为 0.64 eV，与实验值 0.67 eV 非常一致。这些结果表明，基于第一原理数据训练的 NNP 为研究钙钛矿氧化物中的缺陷形成和长期空位传输提供了准确且计算高效的框架。

English Abstract

Oxygen-vacancy formation and diffusion play important roles in determining the electronic and atomic transport properties of SrTiO₃ (STO). Accurate analysis of such point defects requires sufficiently large-scale and long-time molecular dynamics (MD) simulations; however, direct investigations of oxygen-vacancy dynamics using it ab initio MD (AIMD) based on density functional theory (DFT) are limited to relatively small systems and short time scales because of their high computational cost. In this study, we develop a neural network potential (NNP) for pristine and oxygen-deficient STO using a reference dataset generated from AIMD simulations. The trained NNP model accurately reproduces the DFT reference energies, atomic forces, and virials for both pristine STO and STO containing an oxygen vacancy. The NNP also reproduces the supercell-size dependence of the total energies and oxygen-vacancy formation energies, including the formation energy extrapolated to the dilute limit. Large-scale and long-time MD simulations using the NNP (NNP-MD) are then performed to investigate oxygen-vacancy diffusion. The resulting oxygen-vacancy diffusion coefficients show substantially better agreement with experimental data than those obtained from conventional classical MD simulations. An Arrhenius analysis yields an oxygen-vacancy migration enthalpy of 0.64 eV, in close agreement with the experimental value of 0.67 eV. These results demonstrate that NNPs trained on first-principles data provide an accurate and computationally efficient framework for investigating defect formation and long-time vacancy transport in perovskite oxides.

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

TiSe ₂中电荷密度波（ CDW ）相的确切微观起源、对称性和热熔融机制仍然存在激烈争论，特别是关于手性结构顺序和多步相变。利用由精确的机器学习原子间势驱动的大规模分子动力学，我们解决了单层TiSe ₂的有限温度结构动力学。我们证明， CDW熔化偏离了传统的均值场二阶行为，经历了两步过程，其特征是具有增殖畴壁的扩展波动区（ 200-250 K ）、相位退相干、组分选择性熔化和过阻尼的软光学声子。此外，各向异性长波长热波动自发稳定具有P1对称性的不对称3Q CDW级。这些发现为2D量子材料中的波动驱动跃迁提供了一个统一的微观框架，表明TiSe ₂的复杂CDW物理完全通过非扰动电子-声子和声子-声子相互作用捕获。

English Abstract

The exact microscopic origin, symmetry, and thermal melting mechanism of the charge density wave (CDW) phase in TiSe₂ remain intensely debated, particularly regarding chiral structural order and multi-step phase transitions. Using large-scale molecular dynamics driven by an accurate machine-learning interatomic potential, we resolve the finite-temperature structural dynamics of the monolayer TiSe₂. We demonstrate that CDW melting deviates from conventional mean-field second-order behavior, undergoing a two-step process characterized by an extended fluctuation region (200-250 K) with proliferating domain walls, phase decoherence, component-selective melting, and an overdamped soft optical phonon. Furthermore, anisotropic long-wavelength thermal fluctuations spontaneously stabilize an asymmetric 3Q CDW order with P1 symmetry. These findings provide a unified microscopic framework for fluctuation-driven transitions in 2D quantum materials, demonstrating that the complex CDW physics of TiSe₂ is captured entirely via non-perturbative electron-phonon and phonon-phonon interactions.

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

现有的钙钛矿太阳能电池机器学习模型难以预测由新材料制成的设备。大多数人将器件层视为任意分类标签，并通过无特征的组合矢量或通过需要预先解决的单元格的刚性晶体图来表示钙钛矿吸收剂。因此，它们无法推广到未记录的合金或不熟悉的电池架构。我们提出了虚拟合金物理感知语义系统（ V-PaSS ） ，这是一种围绕钙钛矿固体解决方案的物理连续性构建的架构。V-PaSS代表任意混合阳离子、混合卤化物吸收剂，通过Vegard定律通过学习带隙弯曲插值23种纯ABX ”端点化合物的弛豫晶体图。对于器件堆栈，它将层序列和细胞极性（ n-i-p或p-i-n ）与界面带偏移相结合，并用科学语言模型编码化学描述。根据机构分组方案进行评估，其中测试设备完全来自看不见的实验室，未经调整的V-PaSS默认配置在14个测试模型中实现了最低的误差（ 2.861% MAE ，相对于梯度提升的基线3.017%降低了5.2% ，优于最佳调谐基准2.912% ）。V-PaSS在测试设备与培训最不同的地方遥遥领先：在看不见的吸收体合金上减少了15.7-19.0%的误差，在新实验室的混合吸收体上减少了16.1%的误差，在未观察到传输层的设备上扩大了3.6倍的领先优势。结合树形集成， V-PaSS在所有五个基准分割中设置了新的先进水平。

English Abstract

Existing machine-learning models for perovskite solar cells struggle to predict devices made from new materials. Most treat device layers as arbitrary categorical labels and represent perovskite absorbers either through featureless composition vectors or through rigid crystal graphs that require a pre-solved unit cell. As a result, they cannot generalize to unrecorded alloys or unfamiliar cell architectures. We present the Virtual-Alloy Physics-Aware Semantic System (V-PaSS), an architecture built around the physical continuity of perovskite solid solutions. V-PaSS represents arbitrary mixed-cation, mixed-halide absorbers by interpolating relaxed crystal graphs of 23 pure ABX₃ endpoint compounds via Vegard's law with learned bandgap bowing. For the device stack, it couples layer sequence and cell polarity (n-i-p or p-i-n) with interfacial band offsets and encodes chemical descriptions with a scientific language model. Evaluated under an institution-grouped protocol where test devices come exclusively from unseen laboratories, an untuned default configuration of V-PaSS achieves the lowest error among fourteen tested models (2.861% MAE, a 5.2% relative reduction over a gradient-boosted baseline at 3.017%, and outperforming the best tuned benchmark at 2.912%). V-PaSS pulls furthest ahead where test devices differ most from training: reducing error by 15.7-19.0% on unseen absorber alloys, by 16.1% on mixed absorbers from new laboratories, and widening its lead over the baseline by 3.6× on devices with unobserved transport layers. Combined with tree ensembles, V-PaSS sets a new state of the art across all five benchmark splits.

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：phase transition。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

拓扑边缘状态是拓扑相的标志，它们的存在通过体边缘对应与体带拓扑相关。在光子赝自旋霍尔系统中，这些状态表现出赝自旋动量锁定，晶格相互作用和界面几何形状决定每个赝自旋的传播方向。在不改变晶格配置或激发旋向性的情况下反转赝自旋相关的传播方向仍然是控制拓扑边缘传输的基本挑战。在这里，我们从理论上和实验上证明了嵌入金属腔内的微波超表面中的腔可调拓扑边缘传输。改变腔高度可以重塑谐振器相互作用，驱动能带反转和拓扑相变，同时保留晶格几何形状。对于固定的畴壁结构，这种转变反转与每个赝自旋相关的传播方向，同时保留赝自旋动量锁定。观察到具有相同旋向的激励会发射沿相反方向传播的边缘波，其各自的边缘带频率由腔高度决定。这些结果将腔电动力学确立为控制体拓扑和重新配置拓扑界面的方向响应的外部手段，为固定光子晶格结构中的拓扑波路由创造了新的机会。

English Abstract

Topological edge states are a hallmark of topological phases, with their existence tied to bulk band topology through bulk-edge correspondence. In photonic pseudospin-Hall systems, these states exhibit pseudospin-momentum locking, with lattice interactions and interface geometry determining each pseudospin's propagation direction. Reversing the pseudospin-dependent propagation direction without altering the lattice configuration or excitation handedness remains a fundamental challenge in controlling topological edge transport. Here, we theoretically and experimentally demonstrate cavity-tunable topological edge transport in a microwave metasurface embedded within a metallic cavity. Varying the cavity height reshapes resonator interactions, driving band inversion and topological phase transition while preserving the lattice geometry. For a fixed domain-wall configuration, this transition reverses the propagation direction associated with each pseudospin while preserving pseudospin-momentum locking. Excitations with the same handedness are observed to launch edge waves propagating in opposite directions at their respective edge-band frequencies determined by the cavity height. These results establish cavity electrodynamics as an external means of controlling the bulk topology and reconfiguring the directional response of topological interfaces, creating new opportunities for topological wave routing in fixed photonic lattice structures.

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

木星的微弱环系统包含微米和亚微米大小的尘埃，其动力学受到行星快速旋转磁场的影响。光学观测已经确定了主环和光环的形态，但木星附近的细小尘埃仍然难以表征。我们使用两级卷积神经网络分析了近十年的现场朱诺波电场测量，以识别和分类灰尘影响、孤立波和其他等离子体波。这些原位尘埃撞击探测揭示了一种径向演化的结构，该结构在传统观察到的光晕内部延伸得很远。在最内部区域，尘埃集中在一个致密的赤道对称层中，这表明光学内部边界并不标志着尘埃群的末端。再向外，该层垂直扩展成宽阔的光环，然后向主环收缩，直接揭示了木星光环的环形形态。尘埃数密度在晕内和主环-晕过渡附近增强。垂直分布还产生随径向距离变化的半球不对称性，这与木星磁性几何形状对带电尘埃动力学的影响一致。相比之下，孤立波主要出现在高纬度地区，它们绘制的磁足迹显示出与木星极光结构（尤其是主极光椭圆形）强烈的空间对应关系。这些现场测量共同扩展了木星环的遥感图像，并揭示了其最内部磁层中先前未解决的尘埃和等离子体结构。

English Abstract

Jupiter's faint ring system contains micron- and submicron-sized dust whose dynamics are influenced by the planet's rapidly rotating magnetic field. Optical observations have established the morphology of the main ring and halo, but tenuous dust close to Jupiter remains difficult to characterize. We analyze nearly a decade of in-situ Juno Waves electric-field measurements using a two-stage convolutional neural network to identify and classify dust impacts, solitary waves, and other plasma waves. These in-situ dust-impact detections reveal a radially evolving structure extending well inside the traditionally observed halo. In the innermost region, dust is concentrated in a compact, equatorially symmetric layer, indicating that the optical inner boundary does not mark the end of the dust population. Farther outward, the layer expands vertically into a broad halo before contracting toward the main ring, directly revealing the toroidal morphology of the Jovian halo. Dust number density is enhanced within the halo and near the main-ring--halo transition. The vertical distribution also develops hemispheric asymmetries that vary with radial distance, consistent with an influence of Jupiter's magnetic geometry on charged-dust dynamics. In contrast, solitary waves occur predominantly at high latitudes, and their mapped magnetic footprints show a strong spatial correspondence with Jovian auroral structures, particularly the main auroral oval. Together, these in-situ measurements extend the remote-sensing picture of Jupiter's rings and reveal previously unresolved dust and plasma structures in its innermost magnetosphere.

重要的原子：最大的材料数据集有 99% 的冗余，本地化学可以指导所需的 1%#

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

材料的通用机器学习力场 (MLFF) 现在已接受约 1 亿个结构的训练，假设可转移性需要如此规模。我们在最大的可用材料数据集 OMat24 上测试这个前提，通过在 650 万个原子（不到 OMat24 的 1%）的子集上训练 MACE 模型，以三种互补的方式选择：随机、通过预训练 MLFF 的潜在空间聚类（DIRECT），以及通过一种新的无模型方法最大化离散、可解释局部环境的多样性（EnvTok）。所有三个模型均接近全数据参考 MACE-OMAT-0 模型，力值在几十 meV/mathringA 以内，并且在形成能、弹性模量、热容、Li⁺ 迁移势垒以及高达 3000 K 和 500 GPa 的分子动力学稳定性方面保持接近或超过它。这种近乎独立于选择策略的情况表明，化学环境的多样性（而不是大小）定义了 OMat24 的信息内容：其余 99% 的结构很大程度上是冗余的。因此，化学空间的广泛采样仍然至关重要，但仅对于结构和化学上不同的配置才需要参考标签。 EnvTok 使用原子位置和物种（可选择通过磁矩增强）在任何标记或训练之前识别这些位置和物种，从而生成紧凑的、独立于体系结构的数据集。这种数百倍的减少使得混合 DFT 或更高级的通用训练集重新标记变得可行，并释放计算预算以扩展环境覆盖范围和多保真度学习。

English Abstract

General-purpose machine-learning force fields (MLFFs) for materials are now trained on the order of 100 million structures, assuming transferability requires such scale. We test this premise on OMat24, the largest available materials dataset, by training MACE models on subsets of 6.5 million atoms, less than 1% of OMat24, selected in three complementary ways: at random, by latent-space clustering of a pretrained MLFF (DIRECT), and by a new model-free method maximizing the diversity of discrete, interpretable local environments (EnvTok). All three models approach the full-data reference MACE-OMAT-0 model to within a few tens of meV/mathringA in forces, and remain close to or exceed it on formation energies, elastic moduli, heat capacities, Li⁺ migration barriers, and molecular-dynamics stability up to 3000,K and 500,GPa. This near-independence from the selection strategy shows that chemical environment diversity, not size, defines OMat24's information content: the remaining 99% of structures are largely redundant. Broad sampling of chemical space thus remains essential, but reference labels are needed only for structurally and chemically distinct configurations. Using atomic positions and species, optionally augmented by magnetic moments, EnvTok identifies these before any labelling or training, yielding compact, architecture-independent datasets. This hundredfold reduction makes hybrid-DFT or higher-level relabelling of general-purpose training sets feasible, and frees computational budget for extending environmental coverage and multi-fidelity learning.

页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

我们通过共面波导 (sim50 mK, 15.4--18 T) 的宽带微波传输来探测莫尔排列单层石墨烯器件的最低朗道能级的电导率，通过低负高频带差 D(ν) 隔离纵向通道。我们解决了 D(ν) 中的一系列最小值，这些最小值沿着固定载流子密度线而不是固定朗道填充线随场漂移，这是莫尔相当电子晶体的标志。最强的最小值存在于每个场中，并且不匹配分数量子霍尔或整数霍夫施塔特状态。它位于能带填充 s=1/3 处，每三个莫尔晶胞一个电子——DaSilva、Jung 和 MacDonald 预测的广义维格纳晶体是最有利的固定密度 (t=0) 公度。较弱的最小值落在邻近相应填充物附近。

English Abstract

We probe the conductivity of the lowest Landau level of a moiré-aligned monolayer graphene device by broadband microwave transmission through a coplanar waveguide (sim50 mK, 15.4--18 T), isolating the longitudinal channel through a low-minus-high frequency band difference D(ν). We resolve a series of minima in D(ν) that drift with field along lines of fixed carrier density rather than fixed Landau filling, the hallmark of moiré-commensurate electron crystals. The strongest minimum is present at every field and matches no fractional quantum Hall or integer Hofstadter state. It lies at band filling s=1/3, one electron for every three moiré cells---the generalized Wigner crystal that DaSilva, Jung, and MacDonald predict as the most favorable fixed-density (t=0) commensuration. The weaker minima fall near neighboring commensurate fillings.

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

非阿贝尔任意子，即构成容错拓扑量子计算基础的奇异粒子，预计将在偶分母填充因子下以分数量子霍尔态出现。确定实现了哪种拓扑顺序已被证明是一个具有挑战性的问题，需要对逼真的朗道水平混合进行准确的、非扰动的处理，迄今为止理论研究中一直缺少这种处理。在这里，我们提出了非阿贝尔量子霍尔问题的第一个完整解，其中包括所有电子和所有朗道能级，用于填充因子ν = 3/2的自旋极化电子。我们开发了一个动量分辨的神经网络变分波函数，仅在微观Hamiltonian上进行优化，从而直接访问拓扑退化的基态。在没有任何关于预期结果的物理输入的情况下，它发现了一个退化的基态流形，其纠缠揭示了具有量子维度sqrt2的任何子，量子维度sqrt2是伊辛型非阿贝尔阶的特征。通过直接计算与试验波函数的重叠，我们进一步表明， Landau水平混合比其他候选状态更有利于反Pfaffian。我们的结果证明了神经网络波函数在寻找和表征非阿贝尔任意子的能力，从第一原理，在强朗道水平混合的方案中，与许多最近的实验相关。

English Abstract

Non-Abelian anyons, strange particles forming the basis of fault-tolerant topological quantum computing, are predicted to emerge in fractional quantum Hall states at even-denominator filling factors. Identifying which topological order is realized has proven to be a challenging problem that requires an accurate, non-perturbative treatment of realistic Landau level mixing, which has so far been missing from theoretical studies. Here we present the first complete solution of non-Abelian quantum Hall problem that includes all electrons and all Landau levels, for spin-polarized electrons at filling factor ν = 3/2. We develop a momentum-resolved neural-network variational wave function, optimized solely on the microscopic Hamiltonian, which gives direct access to the topologically degenerate ground states. Without any physics input about the expected outcome, it discovers a degenerate ground-state manifold whose entanglement reveals anyons with quantum dimension sqrt2, the hallmark of Ising-type non-Abelian order. By directly computing the overlap with trial wave functions, we further show that Landau level mixing favors the anti-Pfaffian over other candidate states. Our results demonstrate the power of neural-network wave functions in finding and characterizing non-Abelian anyons from first principles, in regimes of strong Landau level mixing, relevant to many recent experiments.

页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

跨材料磁芯损耗预测需要通过有限的测量来适应新材料的响应。全局特征对齐忽略了源和目标激励覆盖范围的差异。这促使选择可比较的样本并控制每个源测量的对齐贡献。本文提出了T-PCOT，一种基于条件支持部分最优传输的Transformer传输方法。频率、磁通偏移、温度和波形描述符定义了可比较操作条件的稀疏图。容量受限的运输计划在源测量之间分配对齐，并使昂贵的目标质量无法匹配。该计划是根据训练前测量的输入构建的，并指导局部波形特征的一致性。重要性加权采样方案在小批量优化期间保留了统一源回归和传输目标。对三个材料对和三个目标标签预算的实验表明，在所有九个比较中，相对于基于 Transformer 的多内核最大平均差异 (T-MKMMD)，T-PCOT 降低了平均相对误差 (MRE) 和第 95 个百分位误差 (P95)。宏观MRE从12.60%下降到9.65%，宏观P95从30.94%下降到25.06%，相对下降23.41%和19.01%。受控比较和消融检查输入处理、条件对应和源容量的作用。结果表明，通过操作条件支持组织跨材料对准比比较的全局对准方法具有预测优势。所提出的方法在磁芯损耗转移学习中实现了最先进的预测性能。

English Abstract

Cross-material magnetic core loss prediction requires adapting to new material responses with limited measurements. Global feature alignment overlooks differences in source and target excitation coverage. This motivates selecting comparable samples and controlling each source measurement's alignment contribution. This paper proposes T-PCOT, a Transformer transfer method based on condition-supported partial optimal transport. Frequency, flux excursion, temperature, and waveform descriptors define a sparse graph of comparable operating conditions. A capacity-constrained transport plan distributes alignment across source measurements and leaves costly target mass unmatched. The plan is constructed from measured inputs before training and guides local waveform-feature consistency. An importance-weighted sampling scheme preserves both uniform source regression and the transport objective during minibatch optimization. Experiments on three material pairs and three target label budgets show that T-PCOT reduces mean relative error (MRE) and 95th-percentile error (P95) relative to Transformer-based multiple-kernel maximum mean discrepancy (T-MKMMD) in all nine comparisons. Macro MRE decreases from 12.60% to 9.65%, and macro P95 from 30.94% to 25.06%, corresponding to relative reductions of 23.41% and 19.01%. Controlled comparisons and ablations examine the roles of input processing, condition correspondence, and source capacity. The results demonstrate the predictive advantage of organizing cross-material alignment through operating-condition support over the compared global alignment method. The proposed method achieves state-of-the-art predictive performance in magnetic core loss transfer learning.

页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

序列分解已成为深度时间序列预测模型中广泛认可的范例。然而，现有方法主要对所有子序列采用均匀建模策略，从而忽略了不同分量固有的独特归纳偏差。我们发现，趋势成分表现出跨渠道的主导空间耦合，但具有显著的时间冗余，而季节成分的特征是局部时间演变，但仍然容易引起注意力分散。为了解决这个问题，我们提出了一种新的解开的架构，该架构可定制特定的建模策略，以与每个组件的内在属性保持一致。对于趋势分支，我们设计了一个潜在图块，使用潜在图建模来捕获全局拓扑依赖关系和全系统范围的共同运动。相反，对于季节性分支，我们引入了高斯多尺度注意机制，以减轻注意力分散。通过注入可学习的高斯先验，我们施加了结构约束，迫使模型专注于多粒度局部环境，有效地过滤掉全局噪声并准确捕获局部进化阶段。在多个真实世界基准上的广泛实验证明了我们提出的模型的有效性。

English Abstract

Series decomposition has emerged as a widely acknowledged paradigm in deep time series forecasting models. However, existing approaches predominantly employ a homogeneous modeling strategy for all subsequences, thereby overlooking the distinct inductive biases inherent in different components. We identify that the trend component exhibits dominant spatial coupling across channels but carries significant temporal redundancy, whereas the seasonal component is characterized by local temporal evolution but remains susceptible to attention distraction. To address this, we propose a novel disentangled architecture that tailors specific modeling strategies to align with the intrinsic properties of each component. For the trend branch, we design a Latent Graph Block, employing latent graph modeling to capture global topological dependencies and system-wide co-movements. Conversely, for the seasonal branch, we introduce a Gaussian Multi-scale Attention mechanism to mitigate attention distraction. By injecting learnable Gaussian priors, we impose structural constraints that compel the model to focus on multi-granular local contexts, effectively filtering out global noise and accurately capturing local evolutionary phases. Extensive experiments on multiple real-world benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of our proposed model.

HGRP: Heterogeneous Graph Neural Network for Routability Prediction#

作者：Yule Sheng、Yue Yuan、Shan Shen、Daying Sun。arXiv · cs.LG，2026-10-15

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：graph neural network。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
English Abstract

Routability prediction enables rapid evaluation of placement quality without routing, providing effective feedback for routability-driven placement optimization. Recent machine learning methods have achieved efficient routability prediction through data-driven approach. However, their prediction performance highly depends on the capability of input representations to capture essential circuit information. To address the limitations of existing circuit representations in simultaneously preserving logical topology, physical layout and routing resource information, this paper proposes HGRP, a heterogeneous graph neural network for routability prediction. HGRP models the cells, nets, and routing grids as distinct types of nodes and explicitly represents topological connectivity, spatial mapping, and grid adjacency, thereby constructing a heterogeneous graph representation that preserves multi-level circuit semantics. Based on the representation, a heterogeneous message passing mechanism is developed to enable information interaction and feature fusion among distinct types of nodes, effectively learning the complex correlations between circuit structures and routing behaviors. Experimental results on the CircuitNet dataset show that HGRP improves F1-score by 18.69% relative improvements compared with the state-of-the-art in the congestion prediction task. The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/ShenShan123/HGPR.

页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

基于多层感知器神经网络的机器学习替代模型用于在给定外延层参数作为输入的情况下预测砷化铟镓量子阱激光器的六个激光器性能指标。代理模型根据来自 4999 个设计的模拟数据进行训练，这些设计是通过改变外延层参数并使用 Harold 模拟每个设计来提取性能指标而生成的。该模型在保留的测试集上预测所有 6 个选定的指标，其确定系数 R² 大于 0.91，并且与完整设备模拟相比，模拟速度提高了 10⁶ 倍。该模型用于在参数空间中搜索 100 万种潜在的外延设计，并找到最小化阈值电流和内部损耗的设计。使用替代方案而不是完整的设备模拟，可以将一百万个设计的计算时间从标准笔记本电脑上的 231 天减少到 sim2 分钟。还证明，使用 Swish 激活函数（而不是 ReLU）与工程输入特征（即从基本模型输入计算得出的导出量）一起，无需额外训练数据即可将平均 R² 从 0.88 提高到 0.94，并将平均绝对百分比误差从 1.78 ± 0.08 % 降低到 1.17 ± 0.09 %。

English Abstract

A machine-learning surrogate model based on a multi-layer perceptron neural network is used to predict six laser performance metrics for an indium gallium arsenide quantum well laser given epitaxial layer parameters as inputs. The surrogate model was trained on simulation data from a set of 4999 designs which were generated by varying epitaxial layer parameters and simulating each design with Harold to extract performance metrics. The model predicts all 6 chosen metrics with a coefficient of determination, R², greater than 0.91 on a held-out test set and demonstrates a sim 10⁶ × speed up compared to full device simulations. The model is used to search across 1 million potential epitaxial designs in the parameter space and find designs that minimize threshold current and internal loss. Using the surrogate, rather than full device simulation, reduces computation time for a million designs from 231 days to sim2 minutes on a standard laptop. It is also demonstrated that use of the Swish activation function, rather than ReLU, together with engineered input features - i.e. derived quantities calculated from the base model inputs - raise the mean R² from 0.88 to 0.94 without additional training data, and reduce the mean absolute percentage error from 1.78 ± 0.08 % to 1.17 ± 0.09 %.

Physics-informed random neural architectures on compact boundaryless multiply connected manifolds for random-operator models in machine learning and uncertainty quantification#

作者：Christian Soize。arXiv · stat.ML，2026-10-14
⚡ 磁性/铁电🤖 AI/机器学习
AI×科学交叉0方向匹配6

📄 摘要：We introduce stochastic physics-informed neural networks (SPINNs) for stochastic boundary-value problems (SBVPs) with random operators. The method enforces physics through a structural probabilistic coupling: a common Ga…

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
English Abstract

We introduce stochastic physics-informed neural networks (SPINNs) for stochastic boundary-value problems (SBVPs) with random operators. The method enforces physics through a structural probabilistic coupling: a common Gaussian stochastic germ drives both the SBVP random operator and a manifold-based stochastic neural architecture. The stochastic neural network is non-feedforward and intrinsically random. A latent Gaussian field and an inhomogeneous Poisson point process on a compact, boundaryless, multiply connected manifold generate random neuron locations, geodesically local sparse connectivity, and a sparse random weight matrix, all governed by a low-dimensional hyperparameter. For deterministic inputs, the network output is random and can represent non-Gaussian random operators. Training is performed in observation space: SBVP simulations produce random observables, while the SNN defines a predictive conditional probability density function. The supervised objective combines data fidelity, through negative log-likelihood, with a cross-entropy alignment term relative to the SBVP-induced conditional distribution. The resulting target is a convex mixture of observation-induced and SBVP-induced distributions, with a dimensionless parameter controlling robustness to model--data mismatch. The shared stochastic germ yields common-random-numbers Monte Carlo estimators and reduced-variance gradients. The method uses only evaluations of the SBVP solution mapping, without differentiating or modifying it, and avoids PDE-residual or boundary-penalty losses. A stochastic elliptic problem on a periodic cylindrical surface illustrates the approach in SBVP-consistent and SBVP-misspecified regimes. A generalized-posterior formulation is also outlined for hyperparameter inference and posterior averaging without per-query reoptimization.

VN-GraphGG: von Neumann--Mullins--based graph grain growth simulation#

作者：Yuntong Huang、Zhihao Li、Shuyang Dai、Chang Ma、Yang Xiang。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-10-12
⚡ 磁性/铁电🤖 AI/机器学习
AI×科学交叉0方向匹配5

📄 摘要：Efficient simulations of grain growth are essential for investigating the process-structure-property relationships in polycrystalline materials. We introduce a two-dimensional von Neumann-Mullins-based graph grain growth…

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：graph neural network。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
English Abstract

Efficient simulations of grain growth are essential for investigating the process-structure-property relationships in polycrystalline materials. We introduce a two-dimensional von Neumann-Mullins-based graph grain growth simulation (VN-GraphGG), which couples the grain-scale kinetics with microstructure reconstruction. The polycrystalline microstructure is represented as a graph with grain and junction nodes. The grain-area evolution on the grain graph is governed by the von Neumann--Mullins (VN) law, while a graph neural network is trained to reconstruct the microstructure from the graph. This separation allows kinetic coefficients to be varied without retraining the neural reconstruction model. Topological changes during grain growth such as disappearance of grains and changes in neighbor relations are handled straightforwardly through reconstruction and postprocessing. Simulations demonstrate accurate predictions of VN kinetics and coarsening statistics, and significant speedup over the phase-field simulations. The proposed method provides a foundation for efficient grain growth simulation based on grain-scale kinetics, opening the way to incorporate a broader range of physical mechanisms. An application example is presented for abnormal grain growth, in which kinetic heterogeneity significantly influences the grain microstructure evolution.

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：artificial neural network。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
English Abstract

Bridging the structural gap between biological brains and artificial neural networks presents a pathway to altered computational dynamics. This paper introduces a Reservoir Computing framework that replaces random networks with biologically realistic modularity by applying the Weighted Stochastic Block Model (WSBM) to Echo State Networks (ESNs). This topological parameterization constructs reservoirs reflecting latent functional sub-circuits. Furthermore, the integration of Input Dynamical Similarity Analysis (InputDSA) provides a mechanism to isolate and compare how structural priors modulate intrinsic recurrent dynamics versus external inputs. By evaluating these architectures, we establish that structural diversity does not necessarily predicts behavioral diversity across the selected network subset. Furthermore, our demixed analysis confirms Anna Karenina principle of dynamics, demonstrating that environmental constraints enforce dynamical convergence in successful agents, while failing networks degenerate into idiosyncratic trajectories.

Framework for an AI-Ready ARPES Community Database#

作者：Zhi-Xun Shen、Jin Gene Wong、Karen Lei、Leon Garcia、Felix Baumberger、Sergey Borisenko 等 44 人。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-10-14

AI×科学交叉0方向匹配3

📄 摘要：Condensed matter stands to gain profoundly from widespread adoption of artificial intelligence (AI). However, its current means of data organization impedes this endeavor. As a premier measure of electronic structure und…

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：electronic structure。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
English Abstract

Condensed matter stands to gain profoundly from widespread adoption of artificial intelligence (AI). However, its current means of data organization impedes this endeavor. As a premier measure of electronic structure underlying material properties, Angle-Resolved Photoemission Spectroscopy (ARPES) has accumulated over four decades of spectra, yet the community's publication-centered workflow preserves only a highly selected fraction of its experimental record. A shared data ecosystem could transform these fragmented measurements and their contexts into a persistent, collective scientific memory, creating immediate value for reproducibility and reuse while laying the foundation for AI-enabled discovery. In this Perspective, we report outcomes of a recent community workshop and propose a practical path toward such an ecosystem. Its benefits unfold along three timescales. In the near term, standardized, searchable, and citable datasets strengthen analysis pipelines independently of AI. In the intermediate future, community-validated tools operating across laboratories could enable broader reuse and increasingly closed-loop automated experiments. In the long term, a diverse corpus of measured electronic spectra could support foundation models for quantum matter. Looking forward, a diverse corpus of measured electronic spectra could support foundation models for quantum matter and new discoveries. We discuss lessons from existing scientific databases, the current state of AI in ARPES, as well as areas of community convergence on data organization, access, credit, governance, and a staged pilot program.

The Blume-Capel Model as a φ⁶ Field Theory: A Monte Carlo and Flow Model Study#

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：monte carlo simulation。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
English Abstract

We report Monte Carlo simulations of a continuous spin variant of the Blume-Capel model. We compute the phase diagrams in the crystal-field-temperature plane, as a function of the softness of the spins. We show that normalizing flows, a class of generative models capable of direct sampling without autocorrelation, are effective samplers across the phase diagram, including in the first-order transition region and in the vicinity of the tricritical point. Finite-size scaling analyses of flow samples locate the first- and second-order transitions as well as the tricritical point with accuracy comparable to canonical and extended-ensemble Monte Carlo.

Principles and Techniques for Agentic Research in Experimental Physics#

作者：Niko R. Reed、Declan M. Daly、Andrew J. Beling、Jordan D. Giandrea、Ronald L. Walsworth、John W. Blanchard 等 7 人。arXiv · physics，2026-10-14
⚡ 磁性/铁电🤖 AI/机器学习
AI×科学交叉0方向匹配3

📄 摘要：The use of artificial intelligence (AI) in physics research is increasingly common, yet few guidelines exist for its effective use. Starting with a principles-based approach, we outline a set of ten requirements for coll…

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
English Abstract

The use of artificial intelligence (AI) in physics research is increasingly common, yet few guidelines exist for its effective use. Starting with a principles-based approach, we outline a set of ten requirements for collaborative AI agents in experimental physics, spanning scientific norms, effective agent use, and domain expertise. We take a human-centered approach in which agents act as collaborators that amplify rather than replace the expertise of a human researcher (user) who directs the work and is responsible for its results. We then present a framework designed to enforce these requirements, detailing methods for improving off-the-shelf behavior of large language models (LLMs) with custom scaffolding and describing general-purpose scientific workflows and skills. In particular, we highlight the power of closed-loop campaigns where agents work iteratively toward a well-defined goal. We demonstrate the potential of this mode of research through six case studies in experimental physics, covering device design, hardware control, neural network optimization, physical model identification, and data analysis. All six case studies result in useful work products that add utility to our research, including a neural network architecture and solid-state quantum defect photodynamic model that substantially outperform our published work; a microwave waveguide design that extends a published design concept to a fabrication-ready device meeting our experimental constraints; a quantum control pulse sequence candidate that improves simulated performance by more than an order of magnitude; commissioning of an NMR spectrometer to sim1~Hz frequency precision on live hardware; and finally a data-fitting pipeline that achieves, to our knowledge, the first reconstruction of the Ising microstate of an artificial spin-ice array from diffraction-limited magnetic imaging.

Structured Multi-Level-Set Inversion for 3-D Electromagnetic Inverse Scattering#

作者：Yutong Du、Zicheng Liu、Bo Qi、Yali Zong、Xin Li、Peixian Han。arXiv · physics，2026-10-15
⚡ 磁性/铁电🤖 AI/机器学习
AI×科学交叉0方向匹配3

📄 摘要：Many dielectric targets encountered in 3-D electromagnetic inverse scattering exhibit piecewise-homogeneous material distributions, which provide useful structural information for inversion. To exploit this property, thi…

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
English Abstract

Many dielectric targets encountered in 3-D electromagnetic inverse scattering exhibit piecewise-homogeneous material distributions, which provide useful structural information for inversion. To exploit this property, this paper proposes a structured multi-level-set inversion (SMLSI) method within a physics-driven neural network (PDNN) framework. Instead of directly parameterizing the contrast distribution, SMLSI represents it in terms of object support and material distribution. Multiple level-sets are mapped to soft-support functions and combined through a soft-union operation to construct the object support. The normalized contributions of these soft-support functions, together with trainable complex relative permittivities, determine the material distribution within the support. The level-set residuals are parameterized by a coordinate-based neural network, and the trainable parameters are optimized from scattered field data. Structure-aware regularization terms are introduced to promote binary object support and spatially coherent material regions while reducing material mixing and redundant activation of level-set supports. Adaptive regularization weighting is further employed to reduce the dependence on manual parameter tuning. Numerical experiments demonstrate that SMLSI improves reconstruction accuracy over direct contrast parameterization and enables the recovery of irregular 3-D geometries, closely spaced objects, and multiple material regions. Compared with CSI and L₂/₃-FBE-WCIE, SMLSI achieves more accurate reconstructions on representative synthetic targets. Experimental validation using 3-D Fresnel data further demonstrates its applicability to measured scattering data.

Train to Stitch: Topology-Preserving Regularization for Interoperable Representations#

作者：Akshay Malhotra、Nicolas Zilberstein、Shahab Hamidi-Rad。arXiv · cs.LG，2026-10-15
⚡ 磁性/铁电🤖 AI/机器学习
AI×科学交叉0方向匹配3

📄 摘要：As large, powerful pre-trained models become more prevalent, there is growing interest in developing methods that allow different neural networks to be seamlessly combined with little or no direct communication between t…

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
English Abstract

As large, powerful pre-trained models become more prevalent, there is growing interest in developing methods that allow different neural networks to be seamlessly combined with little or no direct communication between them. Take autoencoder-based data compression as an example: the ability to independently choose architectures and train encoder and decoder models, while still ensuring they work together, could fundamentally transform how such models are built, deployed, and updated. In this work, we introduce a novel approach that leverages topological regularization to align the latent spaces of two independently trained autoencoder models, entirely eliminating the need for coordination during training. We provide a conditional theoretical analysis showing how the structural agreement encouraged by our regularizer can translate into approximate geometric compatibility between independently learned representations. Reconstruction experiments validate this approach empirically, substantially narrowing the interoperability gap across multiple datasets.

页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

等离子体实验通过稀疏、异质和不完整的诊断来观察耦合的物理状态。在实验室重联实验设施（FLARE）的推动下，我们提出了磁重联期间等离子体态重建的多模态基础模型的概念证明。使用固定参考电子 betaₑ₀=0.2 下的 250 次合成 VPIC 模拟，通过 225 次训练运行和 25 次不相交验证运行，我们训练了 550 万参数的残差 3D U-Net，以根据不完整的观测重建三个磁场分量和电子密度。该模型将仅空间池化（保留所有 24 个时间帧）与全局时空自注意力和旋转位置编码相结合。跨随机、网格、块和时间模式独立屏蔽磁性和密度模态，可以使用一组权重实现稀疏传感、跨模态完成、密度超分辨率和时间完成，并且无需特定于任务的再训练。在密度完全隐藏的情况下，仅 0.1% 的磁空间可见度即可将中值密度 NRMSE 从约 0.9 降低至 0.1。相反，当磁数据不存在或极其稀疏时，观测到的密度可以改善电流密度重建。在没有磁观测的情况下，大约 1% 的密度可见度接近通过完全观测磁场实现的密度重建精度，尽管当前结构仍然更依赖于直接磁测量。全磁调节还可以稳定缺失未来密度框架的完成。这些结果证明了该模拟机制内的双向跨模态预测耦合。跨等离子体状态或实验测量的概括尚未经过测试。

English Abstract

Plasma experiments observe coupled physical states through sparse, heterogeneous, and incomplete diagnostics. We present a proof of concept toward a multimodal foundation model for plasma state reconstruction during magnetic reconnection, motivated by the Facility for Laboratory Reconnection Experiments (FLARE). Using 250 synthetic VPIC simulations at fixed reference electron beta βₑ₀=0.2, with 225 training runs and 25 disjoint validation runs, we train a 5.5M-parameter residual 3D U-Net to reconstruct three magnetic-field components and electron density from incomplete observations. The model combines spatial-only pooling, which preserves all 24 temporal frames, with global spatiotemporal self-attention and rotary positional encoding. Independently masking magnetic and density modalities across random, grid, block, and temporal patterns enables sparse sensing, cross-modal completion, density super-resolution, and temporal completion with a single set of weights and no task-specific retraining. With density fully hidden, just 0.1% magnetic spatial visibility reduces median density NRMSE from approximately 0.9 to 0.1. Conversely, observed density improves current-density reconstruction when magnetic data are absent or extremely sparse. With no magnetic observations, roughly 1% density visibility approaches the density reconstruction accuracy achieved with fully observed magnetic fields, although current structure remains more dependent on direct magnetic measurements. Full magnetic conditioning also stabilizes completion of missing future density frames. These results demonstrate bidirectional cross-modal predictive coupling within this simulation regime. Generalization across plasma regimes or to experimental measurements remains untested.

非晶硅中出现的中程有序#

原题：Emergent medium-range order in amorphous silicon
作者：Zhen Zhang、Stephen R. Elliott、Yujie Wang、Walter Kob。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-10-15
⚡ 磁性/铁电🤖 AI/机器学习

📄 摘要（中文翻译）：中程有序（MRO）是非晶材料的一个重要特征，但其原子排列的本质仍然难以捉摸。在这里，我们利用大规模机器学习驱动的非晶硅原子模拟，探索了这种典型网络玻璃的 MRO。通过在三个维度上表征结构，我们揭示了中心原子周围原子排列的对称性显示出作为距离函数的交叉。在短距离处，结构由局部键合约束决定，该约束从低密度非晶硅中以四面体为主的环境演变为极高密度非晶硅中高度协调和强烈无序的局部环境。然而，在较大的距离处，三维原子密度分布中出现了不同的立方和八…

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

中程有序（MRO）是非晶材料的一个重要特征，但其原子排列的本质仍然难以捉摸。在这里，我们利用大规模机器学习驱动的非晶硅原子模拟，探索了这种典型网络玻璃的 MRO。通过在三个维度上表征结构，我们揭示了中心原子周围原子排列的对称性显示出作为距离函数的交叉。在短距离处，结构由局部键合约束决定，该约束从低密度非晶硅中以四面体为主的环境演变为极高密度非晶硅中高度协调和强烈无序的局部环境。然而，在较大的距离处，三维原子密度分布中出现了不同的立方和八面体对称图案，尽管在较短的长度尺度上不存在这些对称性。我们发现这种交叉伴随着结构中拓扑环的准周期空间组织。环中心之间的特征间距与结构因子中第一个锐衍射峰 (FSDP) 的长度尺度相匹配，在 MRO 的倒易空间特征与其真实空间组织之间建立了直接联系。这些结果表明，FSDP、环的空间相关性和出现的方向对称性并不是 MRO 的独立表现，而是反映了共同结构组织的不同方面。我们的研究结果提供了非晶硅中 MRO 的统一真实空间图像，并表明网络玻璃中的中间范围结构顺序是由局部键合约束和有效堆积驱动的扩展原子排列之间的相互作用产生的。

English Abstract

Medium-range order (MRO) is an important feature of amorphous materials, but its nature in terms of atomic arrangement has remained elusive. Here, using large-scale machine-learning-driven atomistic simulations of amorphous silicon, we probe the MRO of this archetypal network glass. By characterizing the structure in three dimensions, we reveal that the symmetry of the atomic arrangement around a central atom shows a crossover as a function of distance. At short distances, the structure is determined by local bonding constraints, which evolve from predominantly tetrahedral environments in low-density amorphous silicon to highly coordinated and strongly disordered local environments in very-high-density amorphous silicon. At larger distances, however, distinct cubic- and octahedral-symmetry patterns emerge in the three-dimensional atomic-density distribution, despite the absence of these symmetries at shorter length scales. We find that this crossover is accompanied by a quasi-periodic spatial organization of the topological rings in the structure. The characteristic spacing between ring centers matches the length scale of the first sharp diffraction peak (FSDP) in the structure factor, establishing a direct connection between this reciprocal-space signature of MRO and its real-space organization. These results demonstrate that the FSDP, the spatial correlation of the rings, and emergent orientational symmetries are not independent manifestations of MRO, but reflect different aspects of a common structural organization. Our findings provide a unified real-space picture of MRO in amorphous silicon and suggest that intermediate-range structural order in network glasses emerges from the interplay between local bonding constraints and extended atomic arrangements driven by efficient packing.

Learn the Dynamics, Enforce the Physics: Generative Latent Operators for High-Dimensional Beam Evolution#

作者：Haohan Zou、Alexander Scheinker、Yuanyuan Shi。arXiv · physics，2026-10-14
⚡ 磁性/铁电🤖 AI/机器学习
AI×科学交叉0方向匹配0

📄 摘要：Particle-in-cell (PIC) simulation is indispensable for modeling beam dynamics in particle accelerators, but its computational cost limits many-query workflows, such as configuration exploration, virtual diagnostics, and…

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
English Abstract

Particle-in-cell (PIC) simulation is indispensable for modeling beam dynamics in particle accelerators, but its computational cost limits many-query workflows, such as configuration exploration, virtual diagnostics, and controller design. We introduce GLOBE, a physics-constrained Generative Latent Operator for Beam Evolution, modeling the nonlinear, control-dependent, multiscale evolution of 3D beam--field dynamics covering 10⁷ spatial cells. GLOBE compresses beam--field states into a compact latent space and factors their evolution into two components. A control-conditioned neural operator learns the dominant dynamics, while an amplitude-normalized generative model captures the residual distribution, recovering fine-scale structures and conditional variations. GLOBE enforces field constraints by regime-specific, parameter-free operators: a Poisson solve reconstructs the electric field in the electrostatic regime, and a spectral projection imposes Gauss's law and a divergence-free magnetic field in the electromagnetic regime. On two high-resolution 3D particle-accelerator benchmarks, bunched-beam transport and plasma-wakefield acceleration, GLOBE lowers relative-L₂ error by 9% and 13%, low-frequency spectral error by 69% and 51%, and mid-frequency spectral error by 54% and 20% relative to the strongest baseline (DPOT), satisfies field constraints, and reduces hours of PIC simulation to a minute per rollout.

MosaicMRI: A Diverse Dataset and Benchmark for Raw Musculoskeletal MRI#

作者：Paula Arguello、Berk Tinaz、Mohammad Shahab Sepehri、Maryam Soltanolkotabi、Mahdi Soltanolkotabi。arXiv · stat.ML，2026-10-14
⚡ 磁性/铁电🤖 AI/机器学习
AI×科学交叉0方向匹配0

📄 摘要：Deep learning underpins a wide range of applications in MRI, including reconstruction, artifact removal, and segmentation. However, progress has been driven largely by public datasets focused on brain and knee imaging, s…

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
English Abstract

Deep learning underpins a wide range of applications in MRI, including reconstruction, artifact removal, and segmentation. However, progress has been driven largely by public datasets focused on brain and knee imaging, shaping how models are trained and evaluated. As a result, careful studies of the reliability of these models across diverse anatomical settings remain limited. In this work, we introduce MosaicMRI, a large and diverse collection of fully sampled raw musculoskeletal (MSK) MR measurements designed for training and evaluating machine-learning--based methods. MosaicMRI is the largest open-source raw MSK MRI dataset to date, comprising 3,150 volumes and 89,830 slices, including a 3,096-volume 1.5~T core dataset and a 54-volume 0.55~T low-field set. The dataset offers substantial diversity in volume orientation (e.g., axial, sagittal), imaging contrasts (e.g., PD, T1, T2), anatomies (e.g., spine, knee, hip, ankle, and others), numbers of acquisition coils, and field strengths. Using accelerated reconstruction as a testbed, we study scaling, robustness, and data selection under realistic MSK distribution shifts. Across E2E-VarNet, U-Net, and ViT baselines, mixed-anatomy training improves over anatomy-specific training, showing that the benefit of anatomical diversity is not architecture-specific. Zero-shot experiments on the 0.55~T low-field set highlight that using more training data improves average reconstruction under field-strength shift. Controlled subset experiments further suggest that these gains come from transferable cross-anatomy structure, rather than anatomy or contrast matching alone.
