阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

从稀疏时间序列观测中恢复复杂随机动力系统的相互作用结构本质上是一个不适定的反问题。网络配置的后验是高维、高度退化的，并且由可用数据的非平衡性质决定。为了解决这个问题，我们提出了一个完全贝叶斯、基于集成的推理框架。我们的方法基于连续时间随机微分方程 (SDE) 似然公式——在数学上充当非平衡路径的 Onsager-Machlup 准势——在非参数最小描述长度 (MDL) 结构先验中。该框架严格惩罚模型的复杂性，并通过将连续参数强制放入离散类别的有限词汇中来避免动力学过度拟合。使用块坐标方案对联合后验进行优化，该方案将次二次马尔可夫链蒙特卡罗 (MCMC) 拓扑采样与确定性 Nelder-Mead 参数优化相结合。使用高度非线性的宏观群体动态作为基准，我们证明推理需要非平稳瞬态数据来解析因果信息。此外，我们证明，通过基于集合的后验分析传播结构不确定性，可以比传统的单点（MAP）估计对下游扰动结果产生更加稳健和可靠的预测。该框架具有高度通用性，即使在严重的生成模型不匹配的情况下也能捕获基本的拓扑约束，为复杂随机系统中的不确定性感知推理提供了强大的工具。

English Abstract

Recovering the interaction structure of complex stochastic dynamical systems from sparse time-series observations is an inherently ill-posed inverse problem. The posterior over network configurations is high-dimensional, highly degenerate, and shaped by the non-equilibrium nature of the available data. To address this, we propose a fully Bayesian, ensemble-based inference framework. Our approach grounds a continuous-time Stochastic Differential Equation (SDE) likelihood formulation---acting mathematically as an Onsager-Machlup quasi-potential for non-equilibrium paths---within a non-parametric Minimum Description Length (MDL) structural prior. This framework rigorously penalizes model complexity and avoids kinetic overfitting by forcing continuous parameters into a finite vocabulary of discrete categories. The joint posterior is optimized using a block-coordinate scheme combining sub-quadratic Markov Chain Monte Carlo (MCMC) topological sampling with deterministic Nelder-Mead parameter optimization. Using highly non-linear macroscopic population dynamics as a benchmark, we demonstrate that inference requires non-stationary transient data to resolve causal information. Furthermore, we prove that propagating structural uncertainty via ensemble-based posterior analysis produces significantly more robust and reliable predictions for downstream perturbation outcomes than traditional single-point (MAP) estimates. The framework is highly generalizable, capturing fundamental topological constraints even under severe generative model mismatches, offering a robust tool for uncertainty-aware inference in complex stochastic systems.

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

磁性为发现受对称性支配的新兴量子现象提供了丰富的平台。自旋空间群（SSG）理论的最新发展揭示了交替磁体是一类独特的非相对论共线磁体，具有非常规的自旋相关电子结构。在这里，我们证明交变磁对称性对电子和激子激发施加了根本不同的约束。尽管准粒子和二体哈密顿量具有相同的 SSG 对称性，但我们发现电子能带结构中存在的对称保护简并性可以在特定动量的激子谱中本质上被提升。这种分裂仅由激子的多体性质引起，不需要自旋轨道耦合或非共线磁序。将对称分析与多体微扰理论相结合，我们预测了一类新型的自旋分裂激子准粒子，称为交变激子，并揭示了它们在交变磁系统中的特征色散。我们的研究结果将激子确立为隐藏自旋空间对称性的敏感探针，并揭示了共线磁体中对称工程多体现象的新途径。

English Abstract

Magnetism provides a fertile platform for discovering emergent quantum phenomena governed by symmetry. Recent developments of spin-space-group (SSG) theory have revealed altermagnets as a distinct class of nonrelativistic collinear magnets with unconventional spin-dependent electronic structures. Here, we demonstrate that altermagnetic symmetry imposes fundamentally different constraints on electronic and excitonic excitations. Although the quasiparticle and two-body Hamiltonians possess identical SSG symmetries, we find that symmetry-protected degeneracies present in the electronic band structure can be intrinsically lifted in the exciton spectrum at specific momenta. This splitting arises solely from the many-body nature of excitons and does not require spin-orbit coupling or noncollinear magnetic order. Combining symmetry analysis with many-body perturbation theory, we predict a novel class of spin-split excitonic quasiparticles, termed alter-excitons, and reveal their characteristic dispersions in altermagnetic systems. Our results establish excitons as a sensitive probe of hidden spin-space symmetries and uncover a new route toward symmetry-engineered many-body phenomena in collinear magnets.

中文摘要

实现超导材料中的自旋分辨电子特性是一个关键前沿，它为无耗散自旋器件提供了新颖的基础物理和潜力。在这里，我们通过使用近藤型模型和第一性原理计算预测了一系列源自铁基超导体（例如 FeSe、FeTe 和 FeS）的 Janus FeX0.5Y0.5 单层。这些 Janus 结构表现出显着的自旋分裂电子态、大的拓扑带隙 (51.4 meV) 和高尼尔温度 (415 K)。我们进一步揭示，可以通过施加面内应变有效地调节谷极化，并且可以通过改变费米能级来操纵由此产生的谷极化反常霍尔电导率。我们的工作提出了一种基于交变磁性的新策略，用于在超导系统中设计自旋分裂态，并激发了对超导自旋电子学的进一步探索。

English Abstract

Realizing the spin-resolved electronic properties in superconducting materials stands as a critical frontier, offering both novel fundamental physics and potential for dissipationless spin-based devices. Here, we predict a series of Janus FeX0.5Y0.5 monolayers derived from iron-based superconductors (e.g., FeSe, FeTe, and FeS) by using Kondo-type model and first-principles calculations. These Janus structures exhibit significant spin-splittingelectronic states, large topological band gaps (51.4 meV) and high Néel temperatures (415 K). We further reveal that valley polarization can be effectively tuned via applied in-plane strain and the resulting valley-polarized anomalous Hall conductivity can be manipulated by shifting the Fermi level. Our work suggests a new strategy based on altermagnetism for engineering spin-splitting states in superconducting systems and inspires further exploration of superconducting spintronics.

页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

具有非相对论性自旋分裂的共线补偿磁体包括交变磁体 (ALM) 和补偿亚铁磁体 (CFIM)，交替磁体 (ALM) 的旋转或镜像相关的相反自旋子晶格产生在 Γ 处消失的动量选择性分裂，补偿亚铁磁体 (CFIM) 的对称不等价子晶格允许在整个布里渊区（包括在 Γ 处分裂）。在这里，我们证明了多铁性硼酸石 Ni₃B₇O₁₃Cl 的共面非共线磁态在真实空间中不同的 Ni 亚晶格上承载着两个基序。电子结构和自旋织构表现出分别与 ALM 和 CFIM 图案相关的共存的类 d 波和类 s 波自旋极化分量。相反的铁电态表现出反向的带分辨自旋极化，表明磁电耦合以及线性位移和圆形注入电流的符号反转。

English Abstract

Collinear compensated magnets with nonrelativistic spin splitting comprise altermagnets (ALMs), whose rotation- or mirror-related opposite-spin sublattices produce momentum-selective splitting that vanishes at Γ, and compensated ferrimagnets (CFIMs), whose symmetry-inequivalent sublattices allow splitting throughout the Brillouin zone, including at Γ. Here, we show that the coplanar noncollinear magnetic state of multiferroic boracite Ni₃B₇O₁₃Cl hosts both motifs on distinct Ni sublattices in real space. The electronic structure and spin texture exhibit coexisting d-wave-like and s-wave-like spin-polarization components associated with the ALM and CFIM motifs, respectively. Opposite ferroelectric states exhibit reversed band-resolved spin polarizations, indicating magnetoelectric coupling, and sign reversals of linear shift and circular injection currents.

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：phase transition、molecular dynamics、ferroelectric、perovskite。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

当晶体经历二阶结构相变时，例如在铁电体、佩尔斯波和电荷密度波中，有序参数的发散波动导致标准声子准粒子图像的破坏。模拟这些高度非调和的状态是出了名的具有挑战性，因为分子动力学等方法在转变点附近会遭受严重的减速，而调和近似在由量子或热离子涨落稳定的能量景观的鞍点处急剧失效。这项工作引入了一种基于变分自由能原理的新方法，即使在量子离子涨落占主导地位的情况下，也可以预测强非调和系统中的临界长程行为和动态谱。所提出的框架建立在随机自洽谐波近似的基础上，但随着原子数量 N 的增加，计算规模从 O(N⁶) 减少到 O(N²)，内存需求从 O(N⁴) 减少到 O(N)。我们对原型无铅金属卤化物钙钛矿 CsSnI3（一种有前途的光伏工程候选材料）的方法进行了基准测试，模拟了其相稳定性和相变附近的拉曼光谱，其中准粒子图像的分解变得明显。我们通过计算具有 1080 个原子的超级晶胞中的完全自由能 Hessian 矩阵和临界温度来证明该方法的有效性。如果采用传统方法，此类模拟需要数万年的时间。现在，在消费类硬件上只需几个小时即可实现。

English Abstract

When a crystal undergoes a second-order structural phase transition, such as in ferroelectrics, Peierls, and charge-density waves, the diverging fluctuations of the order parameter lead to the break- down of the standard phonon quasiparticle picture. Simulating these highly anharmonic regimes is notoriously challenging, as methods such as molecular dynamics suffer from a critical slowdown near the transition point, while the harmonic approximation fails dramatically at saddle points of the energy landscape stabilized by quantum or thermal ionic fluctuations. This work introduces a new approach, based on the variational free-energy principle, to predict critical long-range behavior and dynamical spectra in strongly anharmonic systems, even when quantum ionic fluctuations dominate. The proposed framework builds upon the stochastic self- consistent harmonic approximation but reduces its computational scaling with the number of atoms, N, from O(N⁶) to O(N²) and the memory requirement from O(N⁴) to O(N). We benchmark the method on the prototypical lead-free metal-halide perovskite CsSnI3, a promising candidate for photovoltaic engineering, simulating its phase stability and Raman spectrum near the phase transition, where the breakdown of the quasiparticle picture becomes evident. We demonstrate the effectiveness of the method by computing the full free-energy Hessian and the critical temperature in a supercell with 1080 atoms. Such simulations would have required tens of thousands of years with the legacy approach; it is now feasible in a few hours on consumer hardware.

中文摘要

交变磁性会产生动量相关的自旋分裂，而无需净磁化。在这里，我们使用角分辨光电子能谱 (ARPES) 来研究沿 (1bar100) 平面劈裂的块体 α-MnTe 单晶，该平面保留了表面补偿磁对称性，并大大减少了表面效应和 k_z 展宽。我们解析了三维体能带结构，光谱清晰度显着增强。由 Γ-K-M 节面中的交变磁序和自旋轨道耦合驱动的 d 波段分裂与理论预测完美匹配。我们进一步确定价带最大值出现在 A 点附近，将相应的空穴袋识别为控制输运特性的状态。空间分辨测量揭示了沿 Γ-K 和 A-L 的能带色散的系统变化，与表面 Néel 矢量的不同钉扎相关。我们的结果建立了一种表面敏感途径来解析 MnTe 的体相变磁电子结构，并为依赖于尼尔矢量的能带分裂提供了直接的光谱证据。

English Abstract

Altermagnetism gives rise to momentum-dependent spin splitting without net magnetization. Here, we use angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy (ARPES) to investigate bulk α-MnTe single crystals cleaved along the (1bar100) plane - an orientation that preserves the compensated magnetic symmetry at the surface and substantially reduces surface effects and k_z broadening. We resolve the three-dimensional bulk band structure with markedly enhanced spectral clarity. The d-wave band splitting, driven by the altermagnetic order and spin-orbit coupling in the Γ-K-M nodal plane, shows a perfect match to theoretical predictions. We further establish that the valence-band maximum occurs near the A point, identifying the corresponding hole pockets as the states governing the transport properties. Spatially resolved measurements reveal systematic variations of the band dispersion along Γ-K and A-L, associated with different pinning of the Néel vector at the surface. Our results establish a surface-sensitive route to resolving the bulk altermagnetic electronic structure of MnTe and provide direct spectroscopic evidence for Néel-vector-dependent band splitting.

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：first-principles calculations、electronic structure、magnetism。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

最近合成的 A₃V₉Te₁₃（A = Cs，Rb）提供了一个独特的平台，用于探索传统 Kagome 系统之外的几何挫败、磁性和相关电子态之间的相互作用。基于第一性原理计算，我们证明了受挫 V 子晶格自然地稳定了鲁棒涡旋或反涡旋反铁磁基态，其总能量远低于所有其他配置。此外，海森堡模型分析显示对基态磁构型的强烈约束，使得1个三角形的固定自旋构型传播并固定所有其他三角形。涡旋或反涡旋相的电子结构让人想起戈薇金属，包括狄拉克式能带交叉、范霍夫奇点和费米能级附近的平带。由于非共线自旋结构和准二维磁特性，尼尔温度对外部磁场的大小和方向表现出极弱的依赖性，这与实验观察结果一致。对于Cs基化合物，基态能带结构是金属性的，BZ中有6个Weyl点；而对于铷基化合物，由于 C₃ 对称性的提升，自旋轨道耦合在环境压力下打开了大约 27 meV 的间隙。我们的结果表明 A₃V₉Te₁₃ 是一个有前途的平台，用于研究挫败驱动的手性磁性及其与拓扑电子态的相互作用。

English Abstract

Recently synthesized A₃V₉Te₁₃ (A = Cs, Rb) provides a unique platform for exploring the interplay between geometric frustration, magnetism, and correlated electronic states beyond conventional kagome systems. Based on first-principles calculations, we demonstrate that the frustrated V sublattice naturally stabilizes a robust vortex or anti-vortex antiferromagnetic ground state, whose total energy is substantially lower than all other configurations. In addition, the Heisenberg model analysis show strong constraints on the ground state magnetic configuration, such that fixing spin configuration of 1 triangle propagates and fixes all others. The vortex or anti-vortex phase hosts electronic structures reminiscent of kagome metals, including Dirac-like band crossings, van Hove singularities, and flat bands near the Fermi level. Owing to the noncollinear spin texture and quasi-two-dimensional magnetic character, the Néel temperature exhibits an extremely weak dependence on both the magnitude and direction of external magnetic fields, consistent with experimental observations. For Cs-based compound, the ground state band structure is metallic with 6 Weyl points in the BZ; while for the Rb-based compound, spin--orbit coupling opens an approximately 27-meV gap at ambient pressure due to the lifted C₃ symmetry. Our results establish A₃V₉Te₁₃ as a promising platform for studying frustration-driven chiral magnetism and its interplay with topological electronic states.

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：magnetism、electronic structure。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

拓扑手性超导已经从动量空间中的电子结构的角度进行了广泛的研究，而它在强相互作用系统中的真实空间过程中的出现仍然很大程度上未被探索。在这里，我们研究了具有三个子晶格和每晶胞 2π 通量的强排斥无自旋费米子模型中的掺杂电荷转移绝缘体。结合微扰分析、精确对角化和密度矩阵重正化群模拟，我们建立了具有 p+ip 对称性的拓扑超导基态。在大电荷转移间隙限制下，手性电荷转移激发产生相关跳跃过程，为电子配对及其手性结构提供微观机制。手性超导性持续到较小能隙区域，其中可以出现手性激子双粒子束缚态。我们的结果建立了通过掺杂具有轨道磁性的强相互作用电荷转移绝缘体来实现拓扑手性超导的具体真实空间路线。

English Abstract

Topological chiral superconductivity has been extensively studied from the perspective of electronic structures in momentum space, while its emergence from real-space processes in strongly interacting systems remains largely unexplored. Here, we study a doped charge-transfer insulator in a strongly repulsive spinless-fermion model with three sublattices and 2π flux per unit cell. Combining perturbative analysis, exact diagonalization, and density matrix renormalization group simulations, we establish a topological superconducting ground state with p+ip symmetry. In the large charge-transfer gap limit, chiral charge-transfer excitations generate a correlated-hopping process that provides a microscopic mechanism for electron pairing and its chiral structure. The chiral superconductivity persists into the smaller-gap regime, where chiral excitonic two-particle bound state can emerge. Our results establish a concrete real-space route to topological chiral superconductivity upon doping strongly interacting charge-transfer insulators with orbital magnetism.

页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

我们分析了极化和屏蔽电荷之间耦合产生的铁电畴壁运动、形状和内部结构。通用情况按电边界条件、电荷守恒和动力学以及极化自由能进行组织。我们考虑暴露的表面、介电层和顶部电极下方的界面以及与阻挡混合离子电子导体的界面。筛选通过局部交换、横向运输交换或严格保守的再分配进行。分析推导和有限深度计算确定了筛选阻力、速度分支和空间不稳定的条件。对于连接不同补偿域的隔离壁，严格的表面守恒和零远场电流排除了屏蔽轮廓的稳定平移，同时允许瞬态位移、有限样本停止和电荷平衡结构变化。保护还限制了相邻导体中移动连接点周围的长距离运输。顶部电极几何形状的大场模拟揭示了非线性波纹、向更长波长的传输以及耦合偏振旋转。一阶极化能量学允许两个相间前沿包围顺电核心；多轴极化允许壁旋转和竞争中间铁电域。驱动结构筛选减少支持稳定的周期性行为。减变量图总结了运动和结构状态，而 BaTiO3 和 BiFeO3 示例将它们与材料热力学联系起来。小信号分析区分位移、极化和静电响应函数。这些结果为不同驱动和筛选条件下的壁动力学和结构转变提供了框架。

English Abstract

We analyze ferroelectric domain wall motion, shape, and internal structure arising from coupling between polarization and screening charge. Universal cases are organized by electrical boundary conditions, charge conservation and dynamics, and polarization free energy. We consider exposed surfaces, interfaces beneath a dielectric layer and top electrode, and interfaces with a blocking mixed ionic-electronic conductor. Screening occurs through local exchange, exchange with lateral transport, or strictly conserved redistribution. Analytical derivations and finite-depth calculations establish screening drag, velocity branches, and conditions for spatial instability. For an isolated wall joining differently compensated domains, strict surface conservation and zero far-field current exclude steady translation of the screening profile, while allowing transient displacement, finite-sample arrest, and charge-balanced structural changes. Conservation also constrains long-distance transport around a moving junction in an adjoining conductor. Large-field simulations in the top-electrode geometry reveal nonlinear corrugation, transfer toward longer wavelengths, and coupled polarization rotation. First-order polarization energetics allow two interphase fronts enclosing a paraelectric core; multiaxial polarization permits wall rotation and competing intermediate ferroelectric domains. A driven structural-screening reduction supports stable periodic behavior. Reduced-variable diagrams summarize motion and structural regimes, while BaTiO3 and BiFeO3 examples connect them to material thermodynamics. Small-signal analysis distinguishes displacement, polarization, and electrostatic response functions. These results provide a framework for wall dynamics and structural transitions under different driving and screening conditions.

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

极性斯格明子（磁性斯格明子的电类似物）在 PbTiO3/SrTiO3 超晶格中形成阵列，已知这些阵列仅是短程有序的，但这种无序系综如何在三个维度上组织，以及其微观结构如何构成这种组织，仍然在很大程度上难以理解。在这里，利用 X 射线漫散射的三维差分对分布函数 (3D-ΔPDF) 分析，我们表明极性斯格明子气泡形成三维晶格气体：它们占据离散位置，但它们的有序度仅延伸到几个邻居，并且在 SrTiO3 间隔物上的有序度比在层内的有序度强两到三倍。这种层间耦合与延伸到间隔物中的气泡的偏振一致。在每个气泡内，极化主要以尼尔型方式旋转，并且 Pb 和 Ti 以相当的皮米位移一起旋转，表明 A 位和 B 位阳离子都参与极性畸变。

English Abstract

Polar skyrmions, the electric analogues of magnetic skyrmions, form arrays in PbTiO3/SrTiO3 superlattices that are known to be only short-range ordered, but how this disordered ensemble is organized in three dimensions, and how its microscopic structure underlies this organization, have remained largely inaccessible. Here, using three-dimensional difference pair distribution function (3D-ΔPDF) analysis of X-ray diffuse scattering, we show that the polar skyrmion bubbles form a three-dimensional lattice gas: they occupy discrete sites, but their order extends over only a few neighbours and is two to three times stronger across the SrTiO3 spacers than within a layer. This interlayer coupling is consistent with the polarization of the bubbles extending into the spacers. Within each bubble, the polarization rotates in a predominantly Néel-type manner, and Pb and Ti rotate together with comparable picometre displacements, demonstrating that both A- and B-site cations take part in the polar distortion.

页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

了解有机材料如何调节磁性结构是有机自旋电子学的一个核心挑战。在这里，我们证明了 C₆₀ 分子薄膜对基于 Pt/CoB/Ir 的多层系统中的埋藏磁自旋织构产生了显着且可量化的影响，该系统具有界面 Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya 相互作用（iDMI）。使用洛伦兹透射电子显微镜，测量无场和原位场环境中的磁畴行为。在菲涅尔模式下，我们揭示了 C₆₀ 沉积时磁畴周期性的减少。然后，使用像素化 4D-STEM 微分相衬模式，我们确定这种修改是由于有效磁各向异性和界面 DMI 强度的大幅增强而产生的。结合体磁力测量和微磁模拟，我们提出了一种基于 C₆₀/Ir 界面处的自旋轨道耦合的机制，该机制修改了 iDMI 并驱动整个多层堆叠中自旋纹理密度的收缩。这些发现扩展了有机分子覆盖膜的影响，作为将自旋纹理工程设计到掩埋磁性多层系统的有效且可访问的途径，对下一代自旋电子和神经形态器件的设计具有直接影响。

English Abstract

Understanding how organic materials modulate magnetic structure is a central challenge in organic spintronics. Here, we demonstrate that C₆₀ molecular films exert a pronounced and quantifiable influence on the buried magnetic spin texture in Pt/CoB/Ir based multilayer systems hosting interfacial Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interaction (iDMI). Using Lorentz transmission electron microscopy, the magnetic domain behaviour in field-free and in-situ field environments is measured. In Fresnel mode, we reveal a reduction in magnetic domain periodicity upon C₆₀ deposition. Then, using pixelated 4D-STEM differential phase contrast mode, we establish that this modification arises from a substantial enhancement of effective magnetic anisotropy and interfacial DMI strength. Coupled with bulk magnetometry and micromagnetic simulations, we propose a mechanism based on spin-orbit coupling at the C₆₀/Ir interface which modifies the iDMI and drives a contraction of spin texture density throughout the multilayer stack. These findings extend the impact of organic molecular capping films as effective and accessible routes for engineering spin textures to buried magnetic multilayer systems, with direct implications for the design of next-generation spintronic and neuromorphic devices.

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：high-throughput calculations、first-principles calculations、magnetic materials、symmetry analysis。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

磁性结构（磁矩的空间排列）支撑着磁性材料的多种功能，但它们仅针对一小部分已知化合物进行了实验确定。传统的第一原理预测需要候选磁配置作为输入，但可能配置的数量原则上是无限的。尽管经典的基于朗道理论的对称性分析被广泛用于磁有序分类，但由于不可避免的残余自由度，它们在生成输入磁配置方面的直接应用仍然是一个主要挑战。在这里，我们通过两步对称辅助策略来解决这些自由度，从而将这种分类语言转化为实用的预测框架：不兼容的最大磁子群选择，然后是物理动机的高对称方向构造。这种高效的方案在新开发的面向自旋空间群框架中是合理的。以MAGNDATA中的1043个Landau兼容实验结构为基准，我们的候选集在四个高对称晶体系统中重现了90%的实验报告磁性结构，平均每种材料只有约51个候选，随后使用材料特定的哈伯德U参数进行的第一原理计算以83%的准确度恢复实验基态。将经过验证的工作流程应用于19,808种过渡金属化合物，我们构建了一个包含5,000多种磁性材料的数据库-几乎是实验MAGNDATA大小的两倍。该数据库揭示了只有大规模才能获得的统计规律，包括磁性的发生率、相互竞争的磁态的普遍性，并使数百种磁性拓扑材料、变磁体和其他新兴磁相和功能的识别成为可能。

English Abstract

Magnetic structures-the spatial arrangement of magnetic moments-underpin the diverse functionalities of magnetic materials, yet they have been experimentally determined for only a small fraction of known compounds. Conventional first-principles prediction requires candidate magnetic configurations as input, but the number of possible configurations is in principle infinite. Although the classic Landau-theory based symmetry analysis is widely used to classify magnetic ordering, their direct application to generate input magnetic configurations remains a major challenge due to unavoidable residual degrees of freedom. Here we turn this classification language into a practical predictive framework by resolving these freedoms through a two-step symmetry-aided strategy: an irrep-compatible maximal-magnetic-subgroup selection, followed by the physically motivated high-symmetry-direction construction. This highly efficient scheme is justified within the newly developed oriented spin space group framework. Benchmarked against 1,043 Landau-compatible experimental structures in MAGNDATA, our candidate sets reproduce 90% of the experimentally reported magnetic structures in the four high-symmetry crystal systems with only ~51 candidates per material on average, and subsequent first-principles calculations with material-specific Hubbard U parameters recover the experimental ground state with 83% accuracy. Applying the validated workflow to 19,808 transition-metal compounds, we construct a database of more than 5,000 magnetic materials-nearly twice the size of the experimental MAGNDATA. The database reveals statistical regularities accessible only at scale, including the occurrence rate of magnetism, the prevalence of competing magnetic states, and enables the identification of hundreds of magnetic topological materials, altermagnets, and other emerging magnetic phases and functionalities.

中文摘要

交变磁学作为一种新颖的磁范式，不仅具有零净磁矩，而且表现出显着的非相对论性自旋分裂效应。目前，人们迫切希望开发一种简单且实验可行的交流磁体设计方法。此外，对于交流磁体来说，沿着价带或导带的某些方向实现 100% 自旋极化仍然具有挑战性。为了解决这些问题，基于紧束缚模型，我们提出了一种可行的化学策略，通过使用二齿配体构建有机金属交替磁体。此外，在这些有机金属交变磁体中，通过交变自旋分裂色散带和平带的共存，可以诱导能带边缘沿[110]方向的方向依赖性100%自旋极化，其中平带抑制一个自旋通道中的电子传输，而色散带能够在另一个自旋通道中传导。然后，通过第一性原理计算，筛选了 90 个基于二维双齿配体的方形有机金属晶格，识别出三种互磁材料，即 Fe(taz)2Cl2 (taz = 1,2,4-三嗪)、Co(app)2Cl2 和 Fe(app)2Cl2 (app = 1,4-氮杂膦)，所有这些材料都具有 100% 沿[110]价带方向，以及显着的压磁行为。这项研究为在交变磁材料中实现方向依赖的完全自旋极化铺平了道路。

English Abstract

Altermagnetism, as a novel magnetic paradigm, not only possesses a zero net magnetic moment, but also exhibits a substantial nonrelativistic spin-splitting effect. Currently, developing a simple and experimentally feasible method for designing altermagnets is eagerly pursued. Moreover, achieving 100% spin polarization along certain directions in valence or conduction bands for altermagnets remains challenging. To address these issues, based on a tight-binding model, we propose a viable chemical strategy for constructing organometallic altermagnets by using ambidentate ligands. Furthermore, in these organometallic altermagnets, direction-dependent 100% spin polarization along the [110] direction in band edges can be induced via the coexistence of altermagnetically spin-split dispersive and flat bands, where the flat band suppresses electron transport in one spin channel and the dispersive band enables conduction in the other spin channel. Then, via first-principles calculations, a screening of 90 two-dimensional ambidentate-ligand based square organometallic lattices leads to the identification of three altermagnetic materials, namely Fe(taz)2Cl2 (taz = 1,2,4- triazine), Co(app)2Cl2, and Fe(app)2Cl2 (app = 1,4-azaphosphinine), all of which possess 100% spin polarization along the [110] direction in valence bands, as well as notable piezomagnetic behavior. This study paves the way for achieving direction-dependent fully spin-polarization in altermagnetic materials.

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

P波磁体在动量空间中表现出奇宇称自旋极化，同时保持零净磁化强度，为非相对论自旋电子学提供了一条有前途的途径。此前报道的基于时间反转和晶格平移对称性 Tt 组合的 p 波磁体迄今为止表现出低于液氮范围的有序温度。在这里，我们展示了一种获得原始 p 波磁性的独特途径，该途径不需要 T t 对称性，而是基于不相称的自旋螺旋顺序。具体而言，多铁性BiFeO3（BFO）的摆线磁结构本质上实现了室温下p波磁性与铁电极化共存。结合对称性分析、密度泛函理论计算和圆形光电效应 (CPGE) 测量，我们发现与摆线级相关的大约 100 meV 的大非相对论自旋分裂。由此产生的 p 波自旋极化产生特征 CPGE 响应，其符号可以通过电控制摆线手性来可逆地切换。我们的研究结果将铁电 BiFeO3 确立为可电切换 p 波磁性的室温平台，并为基于宇称破缺磁序的压控自旋电子器件提供了一条途径。

English Abstract

P -wave magnets exhibit odd-parity spin polarization in momentum space while retaining zero net magnetization, offering a promising route toward nonrelativistic spintronics. Previously reported p-wave magnets based on combined time-reversal and lattice-translation symmetry, Tt, have thus far exhibited ordering temperatures below the liquid-nitrogen range. Here, we demonstrate a distinct route to a pristine p-wave magnetism that does not require T t symmetry, and instead is based on incommensurate spin-spiral order. Specifically, the cycloidal magnetic structure of the multiferroic BiFeO3 (BFO) intrinsically realizes p-wave magnetism coexisting with ferroelectric polarization at room temperature. Combining symmetry analysis, density-functional-theory calculations, and circular photogalvanic-effect (CPGE) measurements, we find a large nonrelativistic spin splitting of approximately 100 meV associated with the cycloidal order. The resulting p-wave spin polarization gives rise to a characteristic CPGE response whose sign can be reversibly switched by electrically controlling the cycloid chirality. Our results establish ferroelectric BiFeO3 as a room-temperature platform for electrically switchable p-wave magnetism and provide a route toward voltage-controlled spintronic devices based on parity-breaking magnetic order.

铁磁超流体中斯格明子的表皮相变#

原题：Skin phase transition of a skyrmion in a ferromagnetic superfluid

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：phase transition、skyrmion、domain wall。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

与标量玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚 (BEC) 相比，自旋 BEC 由于其内部自旋自由度而支持更丰富的现象，包括拓扑孤子，例如畴壁或斯格明子。在这里，我们从理论上揭示了三种不同的轴对称孤子结构，它们形成为 spin-1 BEC 中的圆形畴壁，并在固定的绕数内连接。我们将这些结构称为破轴对称（BA）表皮、反铁磁（AF）表皮和正常核心相。在没有线性塞曼系数 p=0 的情况下，它们的出现由二维无量纲参数控制：q/μ（二次塞曼系数与化学势的比值）和 cₛ/cₙ（自旋相互作用与密度相互作用的比值）。 BA-表皮和AF-表皮相分别表现出局部易平面磁序和局部易平面自旋向列序，而法向核心相类似于双量子化涡旋，类似于中心密度耗尽的标量BEC。我们通过分析 Bogoliubov-de Gennes 方程获得了分离这些相的选定过渡边界的半解析估计，并通过求解 Gross-Pitaevskii 方程对它们进行了数值确认。我们使用流体静力近似估计斯格明子半径，并将结果与数值解进行比较。我们设想我们对 spin-1 BEC 中斯格明子的研究结果可以引发对磁性超流体中新型孤子结构的进一步研究。

English Abstract

Compared with a scalar Bose-Einstein condensate (BEC), a spinor BEC supports richer phenomena because of its internal spin degrees of freedom, including topological solitons such as domain walls or skyrmions. Here, we theoretically reveal three distinct axisymmetric soliton structures formed as a circular domain wall in a spin-1 BEC, connected within fixed winding numbers. We refer to these structures as the broken-axisymmetry (BA)-skin, antiferromagnetic (AF)-skin and normal-core phases. Their occurrence is controlled by the two dimensionless parameters in the absence of linear Zeeman coefficient p=0: q/μ, the ratio of the quadratic Zeeman coefficient to the chemical potential, and cₛ/cₙ, the ratio of spin interaction to the density interaction. The BA-skin and AF-skin phases exhibit local easy-plane magnetic order and local easy-plane spin-nematic order, respectively, while the normal-core phase is similar to a doubly quantized vortex analogous to that of a scalar BEC with a density depletion at the center. We obtain semi-analytical estimates of selected transition boundaries separating these phases by analyzing the Bogoliubov-de Gennes equation and also numerically confirm them by solving the Gross-Pitaevskii equation. We estimate the skyrmion radius using a hydrostatic approximation and compare the result with numerical solutions. We envision our results on skyrmions in a spin-1 BEC can trigger further investigation on novel soliton structures in a magnetic superfluid.

柔电和柔磁#

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

与均匀应变不同，应变梯度在空间反转下是奇数，并解锁母晶体中严格禁止的耦合。在纳米尺度上，特别是在弯曲的低维系统中，这种梯度很容易获得，而且通常是不可避免的，因此曲率充当写入晶格本身的对称破缺场。本章回顾了由此产生的电极化和磁性耦合。在建立了应变梯度和曲率之间的几何关系后，我们将挠曲电作为更成熟的参考案例进行回顾：体积、表面和动态的贡献使其定义、其微观机制、其在二维材料中的表现以及现在可用于其计算的第一性原理方法变得复杂。然后，我们以磁电效应为概念桥梁，发展了广义上的柔性磁学，涵盖磁性和应变梯度之间的任何耦合现象，并具体讨论了曲率和曲率引起的 Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya 相互作用的线性磁化响应，超出了已建立的曲线处理。最后，我们简要描述了在柔磁这一广泛保护伞下的一些附加效应，包括非线性效应、非均匀磁电和拉什巴型现象，以及与交变磁的联系。

English Abstract

Strain gradients, unlike homogeneous strain, are odd under space inversion and unlock couplings that are strictly forbidden in the parent crystal. At the nanoscale, and in curved low-dimensional systems in particular, such gradients are readily attainable and often unavoidable, so that curvature acts as a symmetry-breaking field written into the lattice itself. This chapter reviews the resulting couplings to electric polarization and magnetism. After establishing the geometric relation between strain gradients and curvature, we review flexoelectricity as the better-established reference case: the bulk, surface and dynamic contributions that complicate its definition, its microscopic mechanisms, its manifestation in two-dimensional materials, and the first-principles methodology now available for its calculation. Using the magnetoelectric effect as a conceptual bridge, we then develop flexomagnetism in a broad sense, covering any coupling phenomena between magnetism and strain gradients, and specifically discuss the linear magnetization response to curvature and curvature-induced Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interactions, beyond the established curvilinear treatment. Finally, we briefly describe some additional effects fitting under this broad umbrella of flexomagnetism, including nonlinear ones, inhomogeneous magnetoelectric and Rashba-type phenomena, and connections to altermagnetism.

菱形多层石墨烯超晶格中的跨维陈绝缘体#

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：superlattices、magnetism。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

最近在“跨维度”状态下进行的轻电子掺杂菱面体多层石墨烯（RMG）实验揭示了垂直磁场和面内磁场下的异常霍尔磁滞，表明在相互作用驱动的“费米月形”金属中出现了跨维度轨道磁性。在这里，我们证明，通过与莫尔势耦合，这种跨维轨道铁磁金属可以演化成扭曲 RMG/hBN 莫尔超晶格中的量子化陈绝缘体相。使用无限制的 Hartree-Fock 计算，我们发现相互作用驱动的重建隔离了自旋谷极化 |C| = 1 陈能带，导致“跨维陈绝缘体”(TDCI) 状态，表现出面内和面外轨道磁化强度。引入最小二能带模型来描述 TDCI 的独特性质，揭示了非互易速度与面内轨道磁化强度之间的密切联系。我们进一步分析了 TDCI 的集体激发，并确定了在某些情况下可能导致密度波不稳定的软电荷和自旋模式。面内磁场可以硬化软密度波模式并且可以驱动从密度波状态到TDCI的转变。最后，当 |C| = 1 能带被分数填充，多体基态可能是具有破缺旋转和镜像对称性的分数陈绝缘体。

English Abstract

Recent experiments on lightly electron-doped rhombohedral multilayer graphene (RMG) in the "transdimensional" regime revealed anomalous Hall hysteresis under both perpendicular and in-plane magnetic fields, pointing to the emergence of transdimensional orbital magnetism in an interaction-driven "Fermi-lune" metal. Here we demonstrate that, by coupling to the moiré potential, this transdimensional orbital ferromagnetic metal can evolve into quantized Chern insulator phases in twisted RMG/hBN moiré superlattices. Using unrestricted Hartree-Fock calculations, we find that interaction-driven reconstruction isolates a spin-valley-polarized |C| = 1 Chern band, leading to a "transdimensional Chern insulator" (TDCI) state exhibiting both in-plane and out-of-plane orbital magnetizations. A minimal two-band model is introduced to describe the unique properties of TDCI, which unveils the intimate connection between non-reciprocal velocity and in-plane orbital magnetization. We further analyze the collective excitations of TDCIs, and identify soft charge and spin modes that may lead to density wave instabilities in certain cases. An in-plane magnetic field can harden the soft density-wave mode and may drive a transition from density-wave state to TDCI. Finally, when the |C| = 1 band is fractionally filled, the many-body ground state may be a fractional Chern insulator with broken rotation and mirror symmetries.

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

层状范德华 CuInP2S6 晶体表现出丰富的温度和压力驱动的相变序列，产生具有不同物理性质的多个结构相。尽管进行了大量的实验和理论工作，但由于它们的结构相似性和复杂的振动响应，明确识别这些相仍然具有挑战性。在这项工作中，我们采用第一性原理密度泛函理论计算来系统地研究参考顺电C2/c和P-31c相及其相应的动态稳定铁电Cc和P31c相以及反铁电P-1和P-3相的振动特性。根据计算的声子光谱和对称性分析，我们确定了每个候选结构的拉曼和红外活性模式，并识别了特征振动指纹，从而能够通过振动光谱确定其相位。此外，使用波尔图表示法，我们为不同的反向散射几何形状建立了偏振和晶体取向相关的拉曼选择规则，并确定了在特定实验配置下预期出现或消失的模式。因此，我们的结果提供了一个全面的理论框架，用于解释 CuInP2S6 的拉曼和红外测量，并可靠地识别其在温度和压力驱动的相变中的竞争结构相、铁电相和反铁电相。

English Abstract

Layered van der Waals CuInP2S6 crystals exhibit a rich sequence of temperature- and pressure-driven phase transitions, giving rise to multiple structural phases with distinct physical properties. Despite extensive experimental and theoretical efforts, unambiguous identification of these phases remains challenging because of their structural similarities and complex vibrational responses. In this work, we employ first-principles density functional theory calculations to systematically investigate the vibrational properties of the reference paraelectric C2/c and P-31c phases and their corresponding dynamically stable ferroelectric Cc and P31c phases, as well as the antiferroelectric P-1 and P-3 phases. From calculated phonon spectra and symmetry analysis, we determine the Raman- and infrared-active modes of each candidate structure and identify characteristic vibrational fingerprints that enable their phase determination through vibrational spectroscopy. Furthermore, using Porto notation, we establish polarization- and crystal-orientation-dependent Raman selection rules for different backscattering geometries and identify the modes that are expected to emerge or vanish under specific experimental configurations. Thus, our results provide a comprehensive theoretical framework for interpreting Raman and infrared measurements of CuInP2S6 and for reliably identifying its competing structural, ferroelectric, and antiferroelectric phases across temperature- and pressure-driven phase transitions.

缺氧双钙钛矿氧化物 NdBaCo2O₅₊δ (δ sim 0.65) 的 X 射线吸收光谱研究#

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

A位掺杂的层状双钙钛矿氧化物中氧空位的程度可以导致阴离子空位有序化，从而产生独特的超结构。我们利用 X 射线吸收近边结构 (XANES) 光谱，辅以多重散射和基于密度泛函理论 (DFT+U) 的第一性原理计算，研究了缺氧双钙钛矿氧化物 NdBaCo2O₅₊δ (δ sim 0.65)。目前的结果建立了层状钴酸盐中氧空位分布、磁不均匀性和电荷传输之间的直接相关性。我们对 Co K、Ba 和 Nd LIII 边缘的研究表明，根据光谱特征的数量和形状，近边缘光谱表明所研究化合物的 δ 值更接近 0.75。 Ba LIII 边缘光谱的分析证实了 Ba 离子的 +2 氧化态。 Co K 边缘近边缘光谱表明，除了顶端位点之外，在基部位点也可能出现氧空位。这与氧空位通常发生在顶端位置（即这些化合物中的镧系元素 (Nd) 层）的普遍理解是违反直觉的。这种氧空位的分布自然地解释了格里菲斯相和在化合物中观察到的可变范围跳跃的传导机制。当空位分布处于最佳水平时，还可以带来更容易的氧离子迁移率以及更灵敏和更快的氧传感器。我们相信，我们的结果将有助于调整氧离子迁移率并增强氧传感性能。

English Abstract

The degree of oxygen vacancy in A-site-doped layered double perovskite oxides can lead to anion vacancy ordering, which subsequently results in distinct superstructures. We have studied oxygen deficient double perovskite oxide, NdBaCo2O₅₊δ (δ sim 0.65), utilising X-ray absorption near edge structure (XANES) spectroscopy complemented by multiple scattering and first-principles calculations based on density functional theory (DFT+U). The present results establish a direct correlation between oxygen-vacancy distribution, magnetic inhomogeneity, and charge transport in layered cobaltates. Our investigations on the Co K, Ba, and Nd LIII edges suggest that the near-edge spectra, based on the number and shape of the spectral features, indicate the value of δ for the compound under study lies closer to 0.75. The analysis of the Ba LIII edge spectrum confirms the +2 oxidation state for Ba ions. The Co K-edge near-edge spectra suggest the possibility of oxygen vacancies arising at the basal sites in addition to the apical sites. This is counterintuitive to the common understanding that oxygen vacancies usually occur at the apical sites, i.e., in the lanthanide (Nd) layer in these compounds. Such a distribution of oxygen vacancies naturally explains the Griffith phase and the conduction mechanism of variable range hopping observed in the compound. The vacancy distribution, when at the optimum level, can also bring about easier oxygen ion mobility and a more sensitive and faster oxygen sensor. We believe that our results will help in tailoring oxygen-ion mobility and enhancing oxygen-sensing performance.

Polarons in Lead-Halide Perovskites: What the Structural Evidence Does and Does Not Establish#

作者：Inhee Maeng、Young Mi Lee、Jinwoo Park、Seung Jae Oh、Min-Cherl Jung。arXiv · cond-mat.supr-con，2026-10-14
⚡ 磁性/铁电
AI×科学交叉2方向匹配8

📄 摘要：Photogenerated carriers in lead-halide perovskites are widely believed to move as large polarons, whose accompanying lattice distortion is thought to screen the carrier from charged defects. Three recent findings call th…

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：perovskite、polaron、charged defects。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
English Abstract

Photogenerated carriers in lead-halide perovskites are widely believed to move as large polarons, whose accompanying lattice distortion is thought to screen the carrier from charged defects. Three recent findings call this picture into question. First, time-resolved structural probes now measure the photoinduced lattice distortion directly; yet calculations show that thermal motion of the lattice alone can raise the carrier effective mass-an increase previously taken as evidence for polarons-so that it remains unclear whether the lattice distortion around a carrier is really a polaron. Second, the carrier-lattice coupling strengthens as the material is thinned from 3D to 2D. In the two main families of 2D perovskites, Ruddlesden-Popper and Dion-Jacobson, the carrier couples to the lattice through different channels, and their contrasting behavior is set by which coupling channel dominates rather than by lattice stiffness alone. Third, the first direct test of defect tolerance does not point to polaron screening; its authors attribute defect tolerance to the soft, disordered lattice. New transport data show that polaron screening reduces scattering between carriers, but not that it protects carriers from defects. Polaron formation, even if established, therefore does not establish polarons as the cause of defect tolerance. Separating the two requires measuring the lattice distortion as a function of excitation density, over a range spanning the low densities of a solar cell under sunlight and the much higher densities used in most ultrafast experiments. On this basis, we propose experiments that can clarify how photogenerated carriers behave in lead-halide perovskites.

页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

将二维Ti3C2Tx MXenes集成到卤化物钙钛矿（ HaP ）光电子学中已显示出增强器件性能的巨大潜力，但前体油墨（决定最终材料状态的关键阶段）中的基本化学相互作用在很大程度上仍未得到探索。本研究对Ti3C2Tx MXenes暴露于模型甲基碘化铅（ MAPbI3 ）溶液的单个和组合组分时的化学稳定性和表面演变进行了全面研究。通过将详细的X射线光电子能谱（ XPS ）分析与密度泛函理论（ DFT ）计算相结合，我们解耦了MXenes与油墨成分之间的复杂相互作用。我们证明元素碘（ I2 ）和多碘化物（ In- ）充当侵蚀性氧化剂，引发MXene薄片的快速降解和溶解。相反，碘化铅（ PbI2 ）诱导较慢的氧化过程，其驱动因素是Pb2 +离子强烈吸附到氧封端位点上。与此形成鲜明对比的是，甲基碘化铵（ MAI ）表现出稳定作用：它通过氢键吸附在MXene表面，有效地钝化薄片并阻止降解。此外，我们的研究结果表明，前体添加序列控制着MXene的表面状态，需要格外谨慎，但也实现了进一步的可调性。这些发现为如何看待MXene与材料的集成，特别是与HaP的集成提供了批判性视角，为它们的合理工程提供了基础。

English Abstract

The integration of two-dimensional Ti3C2Tx MXenes into Halide Perovskite (HaP) optoelectronics has shown immense potential for enhancing device performance, yet the fundamental chemical interactions within the precursor ink (a critical stage determining the final material state) remain largely unexplored. This study presents a comprehensive investigation into the chemical stability and surface evolution of Ti3C2Tx MXenes when exposed to the individual and combined components of a model methylammonium lead iodide (MAPbI3) solution. By combining detailed X-ray Photoelectron Spectroscopy (XPS) analysis with Density Functional Theory (DFT) calculations, we decouple the complex interactions between MXenes and the ink constituents. We demonstrate that elemental iodine (I2) and polyiodides (In-) act as aggressive oxidizers, triggering rapid degradation and dissolution of the MXene flakes. Conversely, lead iodide (PbI2) induces a slower oxidation process driven by the strong adsorption of Pb2+ ions onto oxygen terminated sites. In stark contrast, methylammonium iodide (MAI) exhibits a stabilizing effect: it adsorbs onto the MXene surface via hydrogen bonding, effectively passivating the flakes and retarding degradation. In addition, our results reveal that the sequence of precursor addition governs the MXene surface state, requiring extra caution, but also enabling further tunability. These findings provide a critical view on how MXene integration with materials, in general, and HaP in particular, should be perceived, providing a fundamental for their rational engineering.

Hidden reaction pathways inform additive engineering for perovskite LEDs#

English Abstract

Perovskite light-emitting diodes (PeLEDs) have emerged as a promising class of solution-processed light-emitting devices, combining high efficiency with facile fabrication1,2,3,4,5. Amino-acid-based molecular additives are widely used to regulate crystallization and suppress defects in perovskite thin films6,7,8, yet these molecules are chemically reactive in precursor solutions, in which their transformations and products remain poorly understood. Here we resolve and control the hidden solution-phase chemistry of a ubiquitous additive, 5-aminovaleric acid (5AVA), whose solution-phase reactivity has remained unclear6,9,10,11,12,13,14,15. We show that 5AVA undergoes competing in situ reactions during precursor preconditioning, yielding two chemically and functionally distinct products: a beneficial organoamidinium zwitterion and a detrimental Lewis-base lactam. By explaining this reaction dichotomy and establishing targeted reaction-pathway control, we selectively use the beneficial species. Direct synthesis and incorporation of the purified zwitterion decouple its intrinsic passivation function from confounding by-products, yielding perovskite films with a near-unity mean peak photoluminescence quantum yield (PLQY) of 97.2% and PeLEDs with a champion external quantum efficiency (EQE) of 37.1%, alongside a mean peak EQE of 32.8%. Finally, we demonstrate that this reaction-pathway control concept can be extended to further additive chemistries and emission colours, highlighting its potential as a chemistry-driven strategy for rational additive design in PeLEDs.

原题：Chip-mediated manipulation of photonic quantum skyrmions
作者：Haoqi Zhao、Yichi Zhang、Isaac Nape、Shuang Wu、Yaoyang Ji、Chenjie Zhang 等 9 人。Nature Physics，2026-10-12

中文摘要

尽管光子量子斯格明子可以生成纠缠态或单光子态，但量子信息处理需要一种在两者之间切换的方法。现在，纠缠的斯格明子及其在芯片上的操纵已经实现。

English Abstract

Quantum skyrmions are attracting much interest owing to their topological robustness, which offers a route to address noise and loss in quantum systems. The toolkit for their creation has advanced recently, enabling quantum skyrmions to be generated in bulk systems as entangled states and on chip as single-photon states. However, methods for generating on-chip entangled skyrmions, as well as for their reconfigurable manipulation and controlled interfacing with single-photon skyrmions, remain elusive. Here we demonstrate entangled skyrmions and their manipulation on chip with an integrated silicon nitride nanophotonic platform. The skyrmion topology is coherently encoded in a local single-photon spin-textured quantum state, which is subsequently transferred into a non-local skyrmion topology shared between two entangled photons. Throughout the transfer process, the topology remains preserved under background noise and different types of disturbance, even after the entanglement vanishes. This establishes a quantum-topology-enabled transfer scheme beyond entanglement, which reveals a fundamentally enhanced resilience that outperforms conventional entanglement-assisted quantum state transfer. This approach serves as a step towards building robust quantum interconnects and advancing distributed quantum information technology mediated by topology.

栅极诱导的层滑动驱动四层石墨烯中磁性的非易失性切换#

原题：Gate-induced layer sliding drives non-volatile switching of magnetism in tetralayer graphene
作者：Daniel Brandon、Tixuan Tan、Yiwen Ai、Kyle Nadel、Peter Golemis、Akshat Gandhi 等 13 人。Nature Nanotechnology，2026-10-14

📄 摘要（中文翻译）：栅极电压扫描驱动边缘扭曲双双层石墨烯中伯纳尔主导和菱形-伯纳尔混合堆叠之间的一阶滑动过渡，从而允许低温铁磁相的非易失性开/关切换。

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：magnetism、phase transition。

页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

栅极电压扫描驱动边缘扭曲双双层石墨烯中伯纳尔主导和菱形-伯纳尔混合堆叠之间的一阶滑动过渡，从而允许低温铁磁相的非易失性开/关切换。

English Abstract

Layer stacking in two-dimensional materials can strongly impact electronic phases. In graphene, for instance, the metastable rhombohedral stacking arrangement hosts correlated and topological electronic phases, which are absent in conventional Bernal stacking. Here we use the sliding-induced first-order structural phase transition between Bernal and rhombohedral tetralayer graphene driven by gate voltages to manipulate the magnetic phase of the sample. Through transport measurement on marginally twisted double-bilayer graphene samples, we observe bistable switching between a Bernal-dominant state and a rhombohedral–Bernal mixed state across a wide space of the gate-voltage phase diagram. The structural phase transition induces a non-volatile switching between a non-magnetic and a ferromagnetic phase. We characterize the latter phase via low-temperature electric transport measurements and observe an anomalous Hall effect. The sign reversal of the anomalous Hall signal under opposite displacement fields suggests that the Hall signal may originate from domain boundaries between Bernal and rhombohedral regions. Our experiments showcase how gating-induced sliding phase transitions of two-dimensional materials can provide on-demand toggle switching between complex phases and offer insights on the effect of stacking domain boundaries on the global response of the system.

English Abstract

The promises of halide perovskites for chiroptical and spintronic applications hinge largely on how long their spin coherence can last. Recent demonstrations of spin injection from chiral single quantum wells to achiral semiconductors have enabled a variety of spin functionalities. However, their spin polarization at the device level remains limited due to rapid interfacial and bulk spin losses. Here we demonstrate a spin cascade strategy by constructing oriented perovskite chiral multiple quantum wells to extend the spin lifetime. In chiral multiple quantum wells, quantum wells with varying well widths exhibit intrinsic and continuous chirality across all quantum-well phases with an average circular dichroism dissymmetry factor of 1.5 × 10−2. In this system, slowly energy-varying and spatially coupled chiral multiple quantum wells establish consecutive intermediate polarization states for spin cascade, thereby extending the spin coherence lifetime to ~5 ns at room temperature. The long-lived spin coherence yields circularly polarized luminescence with a luminescence dissymmetry factor (|glum|) of 1.4 × 10−2and spin-dependent charge transport with a polarization degree of over 80%, enabling efficient X-ray-triggered dissymmetric photopolymerization and high-performance circularly polarized light photodetectors. This work opens the way to long-lived spin coherence in halide perovskites, promoting their use in chiroptical and spintronic devices.

中文摘要

通过使用三烷基胺的气相分子钝化方法来缓解钙钛矿太阳能电池中的界面重组，从而破坏C60填充。这种方法在保持稳定性的同时，可实现高达27.2%的功率转换效率。

English Abstract

Interfacial non-radiative recombination, particularly at the C60electron-selective contact, remains a major bottleneck limiting the performance of inverted perovskite solar cells. While previous efforts have primarily focused on passivating trap states at the perovskite surface, the underlying mechanism by which fullerene-based electron transport layers induce interfacial recombination has remained unclear. Here we reveal that C60lowest unoccupied molecular orbital (LUMO)-derived band-like states arising from strong intermolecular coupling within the C60film drive recombination at the perovskite/C60interface. To mitigate this issue, we develop a vapour-phase molecular passivation strategy using trialkylamines to disrupt C60molecular packing, substantially weakening the C60LUMO-derived band-like states and suppressing interfacial non-radiative losses. The trialkylamine treatment minimizes the quasi-Fermi-level splitting loss after C60deposition from 89 meV to 41 meV, and to 17 meV in conjunction with standard surface passivation. This interfacial modulation enables inverted perovskite solar cells with a certified power conversion efficiency of 27.2% (stabilized 26.6%) on a 1.02 cm2active area. The devices also retain over 98% of their initial efficiency after 1,000 h of continuous illumination under ambient air conditions. Our work resolves the long-standing puzzle of why C60induces severe interfacial recombination and guides the design of comprehensive interface passivation strategies towards efficient perovskite photovoltaics.

页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

在基塔耶夫磁体中，挫折来自于键依赖性自旋各向异性相互作用，这有可能实现备受追捧的量子自旋液相。然而，以基塔耶夫交换为主的材料实现很少，并且扩展基塔耶夫模型的广泛参数空间在很大程度上仍未通过实验进行探索。在这里，我们使用密度泛函理论、簇多体、自旋波和密度矩阵重正化计算来表明，最近发现的蜂窝状铑酸盐Ag₃LiRh2O₆具有非对角线交换Ђ'，其数量级仅次于铁磁Kitaev交换K₁。 K₁ 和 Ђ' 的这种组合解释了关键的实验观察结果，即环境压力下异常高的磁有序温度 94 K，以及当 Ag3LiRh2O6 在蜂窝平面内从铁磁性演化为锯齿形有序时，其在静水压力下迅速降低。我们的自旋波计算进一步预测了由蜂窝层自旋哈密顿量中的 Γ₁' 项引起的不寻常的磁振子带拓扑。我们证明，t2g 态的大三角分裂增强了 Γ₁'（基塔耶夫磁体中迄今为止未被充分重视的术语），并为扩展基塔耶夫模型的新的有趣状态开辟了道路。

English Abstract

In Kitaev magnets, frustration arises from bond-dependent spin anisotropic interactions, which can potentially realize sought-after quantum spin liquid phases. However, material realizations with dominating Kitaev exchange are scarce and the broad parameter space of extended Kitaev models remains largely unexplored experimentally. Here, we use density-functional theory, cluster many-body, spin-wave, and density-matrix renormalization calculations to show that the recently discovered honeycomb rhodate Ag₃LiRh₂O₆ hosts an off-diagonal exchange Γ₁' that is second in magnitude only to the ferromagnetic Kitaev exchange K₁. This combination of K₁ and Γ₁' explains key experimental observations, an unusually high magnetic ordering temperature of 94 K at ambient pressure and its rapid decrease under hydrostatic pressure as Ag₃LiRh₂O₆ evolves from ferromagnetic toward zigzag order within the honeycomb planes. Our spin-wave calculations further predict an unusual topology of the magnon bands arising from the Γ₁' term in the spin Hamiltonian of the honeycomb layer. We show that the large trigonal splitting of t₂g states enhances Γ₁', the hitherto underappreciated term in Kitaev magnets, and opens the way to new interesting regimes of the extended Kitaev model.

Using resonant X-ray scattering and neutron diffraction, we measure temperature-dependent spin-charge coupling in GdRu2Si2. A minimal Landau model with one critical exponent and two ordering temperatures reproduces all six observed order parameters. Spin-density wave components of the multi-Q ground state drive charge ordering, but the onset of a magnetic helix suppresses it via destructive interference.

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：perovskite。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

双钙钛矿（DP）为光催化和光电子学提供了一个组成多样的平台，但其软晶格有利于强激子-声子耦合和自陷激子（STE），从而产生广泛的红移光致发光（PL）。在这里，我们结合光谱学和从头计算来研究无铅、Sb 掺杂的 Cs2BInCl6 纳米晶体，其 B 位合金化从 Na 到 K。合金化将 PL 能量从约 2.48 eV 调整到 2.70 eV，而 3.8 eV 附近的吸收特征几乎保持不变。计算表明，Sb 掺杂能够实现明亮的辐射复合通道并使激子波函数离域。我们确定了导致大 PL 红移的 STE，该红移与掺杂剂杂化带内态的向上移动相关。 Na-K 取代选择性地改变 STE 晶格畸变，解释了为什么 PL 随成分变化很大，而吸收则不然。理论进一步预测显着的 PL 偏振各向异性反映了 STE 波函数的空间结构并取决于 B 位组成。时间分辨各向异性测量与这些预测非常吻合，提供了激子波函数及其随合金化演化的实验指纹。总之，这些结果阐明了无铅 DP 中掺杂和合金化的作用，并建立了偏振分辨光谱作为 STE 波函数的探针。

English Abstract

Double perovskites (DPs) offer a compositionally versatile platform for photocatalysis and optoelectronics, but their soft lattices favor strong exciton-phonon coupling and self-trapped excitons (STEs), which produce broad, red-shifted photoluminescence (PL). Here, we combine optical spectroscopy and ab initio calculations to study lead-free, Sb-doped Cs2BInCl6 nanocrystals with B-site alloying from Na to K. Alloying tunes the PL energy from about 2.48 to 2.70 eV, while absorption features near 3.8 eV remain nearly unchanged. Calculations reveal that Sb doping enables bright radiative recombination channels and delocalizes the excitonic wavefunction. We identify STEs responsible for the large PL red shift, associated with an upward shift of dopant-hybridized in-gap states. Na-to-K substitution selectively changes the STE lattice distortion, explaining why PL varies strongly with composition while absorption does not. Theory further predicts pronounced PL polarization anisotropy that reflects the STE wavefunction's spatial structure and depends on B-site composition. Time-resolved anisotropy measurements closely agree with these predictions, providing experimental fingerprints of the excitonic wavefunction and its evolution with alloying. Together, these results disentangle the roles of doping and alloying in lead-free DPs and establish polarization-resolved spectroscopy as a probe of STE wavefunctions.

俘获离子量子模拟器中的可编程极化子模型和断层扫描#

原题：Programmable Polaron Models and Tomography in Trapped-Ion Quantum Simulators

页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

移动电荷载流子或激子与晶格振动之间的强耦合产生了极化子：由声子激发云装饰的电荷载流子形成的准粒子。极化子的形成决定了从过渡金属氧化物和有机半导体到光捕获复合物等系统的传输特性。在允许硬核玻色子描述的激子系统中，将激发映射到赝自旋为研究极化子物理提供了一个自然的框架，包括跳跃幅度、声子能量和耦合强度具有可比性的非微扰状态。在这里，我们提出了一个捕获离子蓝图，用于实现自旋声子哈密顿量，具有激发密度和声子位移之间的可编程空间耦合，包括局部荷斯坦和远程弗罗利希型相互作用。为了获得裸光谱权重被抑制的强装饰准粒子，我们引入了通过自旋相关声子位移实现的位移格林函数。它们的光谱响应揭示了极化子带谱，包括结合能和声子激发边带，而空间分辨测量则探测声子敷料云的结构，为极化子断层扫描提供了一条途径。我们通过自旋声子相关性的直接和辅助辅助测量进一步补充了这种光谱学，这些测量跟踪激发及其环境的耦合动力学。这些方法将出现的准粒子光谱与其梳妆云的微观积累联系起来，从而能够对耦合的空间范围如何影响极化子的形成和迁移率进行受控研究。

English Abstract

Strong coupling between mobile charge carriers or excitons and lattice vibrations gives rise to polarons: quasiparticles formed by charge carriers dressed by a cloud of phonon excitations. Polaron formation determines transport properties of systems ranging from transition-metal oxides and organic semiconductors to light-harvesting complexes. In excitonic systems that admit a hard-core boson description, mapping the excitations to pseudospins provides a natural framework for studying polaron physics, including the nonperturbative regime in which hopping amplitudes, phonon energies, and coupling strengths are comparable. Here, we propose a trapped-ion blueprint for realizing spin-phonon Hamiltonians with programmable spatial coupling between excitation density and phonon displacements, encompassing local Holstein and long-range Fröhlich-type interactions. To access strongly dressed quasiparticles whose bare spectral weight is suppressed, we introduce displaced Green's functions implemented through spin-dependent phonon displacements. Their spectral response reveals the polaron band spectrum, including binding energies and phonon-excitation sidebands, while spatially resolved measurements probe the structure of the phonon dressing cloud, providing a route to polaron tomography. We further complement this spectroscopy with direct and ancilla-assisted measurements of spin-phonon correlations that track the coupled dynamics of the excitation and its environment. These approaches connect the emergent quasiparticle spectrum to the microscopic buildup of its dressing cloud, enabling controlled studies of how the spatial range of coupling shapes polaron formation and mobility.

页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

萤石结构铁电体，例如氧化铪锆（Hf₁₋ₓZrₓO2），是开发下一代低功耗非易失性存储器最有前途的材料之一。模拟铁电存储器的实际操作场景需要执行与访问速率相关的耐久性测试。切换偏振的依赖于访问速率的减少不能被描述为疲劳和印记效应的单纯叠加，这分别在传统的耐久性和保持测试中观察到。这些退化机制之间的相互作用及其与编程脉冲之间的延迟时间的相关性仍不清楚。将通过电压脉冲进行的电气测试与从头算和漂移扩散模拟相结合，可以全面了解基于产品相关的 5 nm 厚 Hf₀.₅Zr₀.₅O2 薄膜的铁电电容器开关极化与访问速率相关的减少的微观起源。考虑到改变缺陷电荷状态的电子转移过程，阐明了氧空位缺陷在电极-铁电界面处累积的延迟时间在减少可切换域分数中的作用。基于这一理论理解，我们开发了一个基于物理的模型来推断长期访问速率相关的耐久性能，并针对高达 135 摄氏度的温度和高达 2 毫秒的延迟时间进行了实验验证。

English Abstract

Fluorite-structured ferroelectrics, such as hafnium zirconium oxide (Hf₁₋ₓZrₓO₂), are among the most promising materials for developing the next generation of low-power nonvolatile memories. Emulating realistic operating scenarios for ferroelectric memories requires performing access rate-dependent endurance tests. The access rate-dependent reduction of the switched polarization cannot be described as a mere superposition of fatigue and imprint effects, which are observed in conventional endurance and retention tests, respectively. The interplay between these degradation mechanisms and its correlation with the delay time in between the programming pulses remains unclear. Combining electrical testing through voltage pulses with ab initio and drift-diffusion simulations provides a comprehensive perspective on the microscopic origin of the access rate-dependent reduction of the switched polarization of ferroelectric capacitors based on product-relevant 5 nm thick Hf₀.₅Zr₀.₅O₂ films. The role of oxygen vacancy defects accumulating at the electrode-ferroelectric interface with delay time in reducing the fraction of switchable domains is elucidated considering electron transfer processes which change the defect charge state. Building upon this theoretical understanding, a physics-based model to extrapolate the long-term access rate-dependent endurance performance is developed and experimentally verified for temperatures up to 135degC and delay times up to 2 ms.

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

通过应变工程控制铁电相对称性对于定制氧化物器件中的极化拓扑和功能特性至关重要。在这里，我们证明薄的导电缓冲层可以克服 MgO 上的 BaTiO₃ (BTO) 薄膜中基底引起的应变。虽然 MgO(001) 的晶格参数预计会引起拉伸失配应变，但在 500 nm BaTiO₃ 薄膜中，热膨胀失配占主导地位，导致冷却时产生有效压应力。插入 50 nm La₀.₆₇Sr₀.₃₃MnO₃ (LSMO) 缓冲层可将有效应变状态从压缩状态反转为拉伸状态。使用温度相关的偏振拉曼光谱、X射线衍射和压响应力显微镜，我们揭示了冷却时的非常规相序：顺电→斜方（aa相，sim407 K）→单斜（r相，sim300-345 K），表征面内和倾斜偏振分量。 LSMO 层不仅充当底部电极，而且还可以改变应变状态，而界面电荷重新分布会产生内置电场，这一点可以通过自极化和不对称 PFM 磁滞来证明。这些发现强调了导电缓冲层在控制铁电对称性和为低功率自旋电子学、可调谐微波器件和神经形态计算提供具有定制偏振纹理的氧化物异质结构方面的关键作用。

English Abstract

Controlling ferroelectric phase symmetry through strain engineering is crucial for tailoring polarization topology and functional properties in oxide-based devices. Here, we show that a thin conductive buffer layer can override substrate-induced strain in BaTiO₃ (BTO) films on MgO. Although the lattice parameter of MgO(001) would be expected to induce tensile misfit strain, in 500~nm BaTiO₃ films the thermal-expansion mismatch dominates, resulting in effective compressive stress upon cooling. Inserting a 50-nm La₀.₆₇Sr₀.₃₃MnO₃ (LSMO) buffer layer reverses the effective strain state from compressive to tensile. Using temperature-dependent polarized Raman spectroscopy, X-ray diffraction, and piezoresponse force microscopy, we reveal an unconventional phase sequence upon cooling: paraelectric → orthorhombic (aa-phase, sim407 K) → monoclinic (r-phase, sim300-345 K), characterized in-plane and tilted polarization components. The LSMO layer not only serves as a bottom electrode but also modifies the strain state, while interfacial charge redistribution induces a built-in electric field, evidenced by self-polarization and asymmetric PFM hysteresis. These findings highlight the critical role of conductive buffer layers in controlling ferroelectric symmetry and enabling oxide heterostructures with tailored polarization textures for low-power spintronics, tunable microwave devices, and neuromorphic computing.

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：phase transition。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

Mn₃Sn 表现出复杂的磁相图，具有多种对称性破缺现象。 Mn位点的微小电子掺杂打破了Mn₃Sn中的kagome晶格对称性。在这项工作中，我们利用这一点利用 Mn₃₋ₓCoₓSn 单晶中的磁热效应来调节磁相变的顺序。原始 Mn₃Sn 在 278 K 时表现出金属行为，并具有自旋重新取向转变，在 Co 掺杂后转变为 175 K。此外，Co 掺杂还在大约 210 K 附近引起弱铁磁转变。Co 掺杂的效果与传统的磁热效应相反，具有增强的磁熵变。此外，成比例的熵变揭示了Mn 2.75 Co 0.25 Sn晶体中场驱动的从一级到二级的磁相变交叉。基于实验数据，构建了一个新的相图，描绘了各种磁阶的场演化。

English Abstract

Mn₃Sn exhibits a complex magnetic phase diagram with multiple symmetry-breaking phenomena. Minute electron doping at the Mn site breaks the kagome lattice symmetry in Mn₃Sn. In this work, we exploited this to tune the order of the magnetic phase transition using magnetocaloric effect in Mn₃₋ₓCoₓSn single crystals. Pristine Mn₃Sn shows metallic behaviour with a spin-reorientation transition at 278 K, which shifts to 175 K upon Co doping. Additionally, Co doping also induces a weak-ferromagnetic transition near approx 210 K. The effect of Co doping results inverse to conventional magnetocaloric effect, with enhanced magnetic entropy change. Further, the scaled entropy change reveals a field-driven magnetic phase transition crossover from first order to second order in Mn₂.₇₅Co₀.₂₅Sn crystal. Based on experimental data, a new phase diagram is constructed, depicting the field evolution of various magnetic orders.

原题：Flat-band ferrimagnetism in the edge-centered honeycomb antiferromagnet: From harmonic flat magnon to broad continuum
作者：Sicheng He、Zhixuan Zhao、Hanxiang Di、Shuaihua Zhang、Yanling Wu、Jun Li。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-10-16

📄 摘要（中文翻译）：平带最引人注目的方面之一不仅仅是它缺乏色散，而且它将单粒子动能降低到非常小的尺度，使相互作用效应成为低能物理中的主要能量尺度。这张图支撑了平带铁磁性、平带超导性和局域磁振子等概念。然而，一个更根本的问题仍然存在。平带属于单粒子或线性自旋波物理学的语言；它们是否作为受保护的锐激发存在于真正的多体量子磁体中，或者仅仅提供非相互作用图像留下的光谱权重骨架，目前仍不清楚。我们研究边缘中心蜂窝晶格上的自旋 1/2 海森堡反铁磁体来研究这个问题。与…

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：magnetism。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

平带最引人注目的方面之一不仅仅是它缺乏色散，而且它将单粒子动能降低到非常小的尺度，使相互作用效应成为低能物理中的主要能量尺度。这张图支撑了平带铁磁性、平带超导性和局域磁振子等概念。然而，一个更根本的问题仍然存在。平带属于单粒子或线性自旋波物理学的语言；它们是否作为受保护的锐激发存在于真正的多体量子磁体中，或者仅仅提供非相互作用图像留下的光谱权重骨架，目前仍不清楚。我们研究边缘中心蜂窝晶格上的自旋 1/2 海森堡反铁磁体来研究这个问题。与戈薇等几何受挫磁体中的平带相比，该晶格具有 2:3 的子晶格不平衡，使子晶格间耦合矩阵呈矩形，其零空间在单粒子水平上产生平带。同样的不平衡通过利布-马蒂斯定理稳定了亚铁磁基态，并导致线性自旋波理论中的边缘位置局域平面磁振子。结合线性自旋波理论、无符号问题随机级数展开量子蒙特卡罗和随机解析延拓，我们发现量子涨落将这种谐波平坦模式转变为一个宽连续谱，其质心约为1.55J，并且沿着高对称性路径几乎无色散。因此，亚晶格不平衡提供了一条统一平带和亚铁磁性的顺利途径，并表明平带几何结构组织了多体光谱权重，而不是产生受保护的准粒子。

English Abstract

One of the most striking aspects of a flat band is not merely its lack of dispersion, but that it reduces the single-particle kinetic energy to a very small scale, allowing interaction effects to become the dominant energy scale in the low-energy physics. This picture has underpinned concepts such as flat-band ferromagnetism, flat-band superconductivity, and localized magnons. A more fundamental question, however, remains. Flat bands belong to the language of single-particle or linear-spin-wave physics; whether they survive as protected sharp excitations in a genuine many-body quantum magnet, or merely provide a skeleton of spectral weight left by the noninteracting picture, remains unclear. We study the spin-1/2 Heisenberg antiferromagnet on the edge-centered honeycomb lattice to examine this question. In contrast to flat bands in geometrically frustrated magnets such as kagome, this lattice has a 2:3 sublattice imbalance that renders the intersublattice coupling matrix rectangular, whose null space gives rise to a flat band at the single-particle level. The same imbalance stabilizes a ferrimagnetic ground state through the Lieb--Mattis theorem and leads to an edge-site-localized flat magnon in linear spin-wave theory. Combining linear spin-wave theory, sign-problem-free stochastic series expansion quantum Monte Carlo, and stochastic analytic continuation, we find that quantum fluctuations transform this harmonic flat mode into a broad continuum whose centroid is about 1.55J and nearly dispersionless along the high-symmetry path. Sublattice imbalance thus offers an unfrustrated route that unifies flat bands and ferrimagnetism, and suggests that flat-band geometry organizes many-body spectral weight rather than giving rise to a protected quasiparticle.

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：magnetism。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

通过研究几何挫折与三维Aharonov-Bohm通量的相互作用，我们揭示了一个丰富的游乐场，该游乐场表现出拓扑半金属基态，具有轨道磁偶极子，四极子和铁环面阶，类似于经典自旋冰中的自旋模式-我们称之为挫折拓扑轨道磁性的一类现象。我们证明了由非阿贝尔拓扑节点线支撑的基态环面化诱导了可测量的电流诱导霍尔现象。我们进一步发现，不同的2合2出和3合1挫折轨道电流模式可以与拓扑Weyl节点电荷以及相应的各向异性线性和非线性霍尔效应相关联。我们提出了候选材料中不同受挫拓扑轨道磁序的实验验证特征。

English Abstract

By studying the interplay of geometric frustration with Aharonov-Bohm fluxes in three dimensions, we unveil a rich playground exhibiting topological semimetallic ground states with orbital magnetic dipole, quadrupole and ferrotoroidic orders, akin to spin patterns in classical spin ices - a class of phenomena which we dub frustrated topological orbital magnetism. We demonstrate that the ground-state toroidizations underpinned by non-Abelian topological nodal lines induce measurable current-induced Hall phenomena. We further find that distinct 2-in-2-out and 3-in-1-out frustrated orbital current patterns can be associated with topological Weyl node charges, and corresponding anisotropic linear and nonlinear Hall effects. We propose signatures for experimental validation of distinct frustrated topological orbital magnetic orders in candidate materials.

近藤绝缘子中的奇偶表面磁性#

原题：Odd-parity surface magnetism in a Kondo insulator
作者：Maxim Dzero、Brian M. Andersen。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-10-12

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：magnetism、phase transition。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

我们提出了一种自旋极化隧穿理论，该理论是在拓扑Kondo绝缘体的纳米线尖端SmB ”和双共线反铁磁体Fe”之间建立的，其中测量磁对比度变化的隧穿电导在偏置反转时是符号。首先我们表明，没有时间反转的对称尖端，无论其自旋动量锁定有多强，都可以产生实验观察到的磁对比度，这意味着尖端表面的时间反转必须被打破。在SmB表面理论的平均场处理中，我们发现传导d和f电子之间的相互作用驱动了T_c ~ 10 K附近的表面相变，其中近藤杂交振幅的时间反转奇数分量与平面外表面磁化在同一对称通道中聚集，从而弥补了狄拉克表面状态。在跃迁下方，每个间隙表面狄拉克锥的自旋分辨光谱函数在其节点周围的能量为奇数。对于电荷中性顶点，费米能级位于表面Γ和X狄拉克节点之间，因此由该函数在偏置能量处确定的自旋极化电导将其符号反转接近零偏置。因此，这种符号变化是SmB6表面时间反转对称性断裂的光谱特征。

English Abstract

We formulate a theory of spin-polarized tunneling between a nanowire tip of a topological Kondo insulator SmB₆ and the bi-collinear antiferromagnet Fe₁₊ₓTe in which the tunneling conductance which measures magnetic contrast changes sign when the bias is reversed. First we show that no time reversal symmetric tip, however strong its spin-momentum locking is, can produce experimentally observed magnetic contrast, which implies that time reversal must be broken at the tip surface. Within a mean-field treatment of the surface theory of SmB₆ we find that the interaction between conduction d-and f-electrons drives a surface phase transition near T_c ~10 K in which the time reversal odd component of the Kondo hybridization amplitude condenses in the same symmetry channel as an out-of-plane surface magnetization, gapping out the Dirac surface states. Below the transition the spin-resolved spectral function of each gapped surface Dirac cone is odd in energy about its node. For a charge-neutral apex the Fermi level lies between the surface Γ and X Dirac nodes, so the spin-polarized conductance, which is determined by this function at the bias energy, reverses its sign close to zero bias. This sign change is thus a spectroscopic signature of time reversal symmetry breaking on the surface of SmB6.

40

量子斯格明子分子：通过相干磁振子交换结合的两个量子斯格明子#

原题：Quantum skyrmion molecule: two quantum skyrmions bound by coherent magnon exchange
作者：Ricardo L. Silva。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-10-14

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：skyrmion。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

自旋 1/2 受挫磁体中的量子斯格明子是铁磁背景中一些翻转自旋的束缚态。使用具有易轴各向异性 K 的 J₁--J2 三角晶格模型的精确对角化和密度矩阵重正化群计算，我们询问当两个这样的物体放在一起时会发生什么。当 K=0.02--0.04 时，单个斯格明子每次翻转自旋的结合能最小值为 Nf*=7，小于 Nf* 的斯格明子不会成对存在：在任何距离，它们都会合并成一个较大的物体，钉扎井无法阻止它。相比之下，两个自然大小的斯格明子在没有钉扎或外场的情况下形成结合分子，结合能 Usimeq-0.007J₁ 在 K=0.03--0.04 处，平衡分离为 5.5 个晶格间距，不依赖于 K 或模拟域的大小；当 K=0.02 时，单个斯格明子本身是边缘束缚的，而该对在我们的分辨率内是未束缚的。这两种成分保持其同一性（7+7 翻转自旋），并通过磁振子的连贯交换耦合，表现为域的两半之间的数量波动超过两个独立的斯格明子的数量波动，以及两个斯格明子壁之间的面内自旋相关性在间隙中达到 0.16。这些相关性遵循两种纹理的涡旋模式，并锁定它们的相对螺旋度，即经典斯格明子螺旋度相关相互作用的量子对应物。我们将该分子作为单层斯格明子螺旋量子位的自组装双量子位元件进行讨论。

English Abstract

Quantum skyrmions in frustrated spin-1/2 magnets are bound states of a few flipped spins in a ferromagnetic background. Using exact diagonalization and density-matrix renormalization group calculations for the J₁--J₂ triangular-lattice model with easy-axis anisotropy K, we ask what happens when two such objects are brought together. The binding energy per flipped spin of a single skyrmion has a minimum at Nf*=7 for K=0.02--0.04, and skyrmions smaller than Nf* do not exist as pairs: at any distance they coalesce into a single larger object, and pinning wells cannot prevent it. Two skyrmions of natural size, by contrast, form a bound molecule without pinning or external field, with binding energy Usimeq-0.007J₁ at K=0.03--0.04 and an equilibrium separation of 5.5 lattice spacings that does not depend on K or on the size of the simulation domain; at K=0.02, where the single skyrmion is itself marginally bound, the pair is unbound within our resolution. The two constituents keep their identity (7+7 flipped spins) and are coupled by a coherent exchange of magnons, visible as number fluctuations between the two halves of the domain in excess of those of two independent skyrmions and as in-plane spin correlations between the two skyrmion walls that reach 0.16 across the gap. These correlations follow the vortex pattern of the two textures and lock their relative helicity, the quantum counterpart of the helicity-dependent interaction of classical skyrmions. We discuss the molecule as a self-assembled two-qubit element for skyrmion helicity qubits in a single layer.

知识引导构建含能材料反应行为的机器学习潜力#

原题：Knowledge-Guided Construction of Machine Learning Potentials for Reactive Behavior in Energetic Materials
作者：Guanchen Dong、Huanrong Liu、Lin Wang、Yuxuan Chen、Zhengyu Tu、Qing-an Li 等 15 人。arXiv · cond-mat.supr-con，2026-10-14
🤖 AI/机器学习
AI×科学交叉8方向匹配8

📄 摘要（中文翻译）：含能材料涉及多种相互作用，如果没有大量数据集，就很难构建可靠的机器学习潜力 (MLP)。在这里，我们增强了神经网络原子间势（NNAP）使用 sphmulti 描述分子系统的能力，sphmulti 是一种精炼的球谐基组，它将额外的径向基与每个径向通道中元素特定的化学权重相结合。我们进一步将超活跃学习、最远点采样和迭代充分性标准与知识引导的关键事件相结合，使 NNAP 能够保留用于描述材料系统的基本信息。对于 3,3'-二氨基-4,4'-偶…

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：machine learning potential、interatomic potential、molecular dynamics、active learning。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

含能材料涉及多种相互作用，如果没有大量数据集，就很难构建可靠的机器学习潜力 (MLP)。在这里，我们增强了神经网络原子间势（NNAP）使用 sphmulti 描述分子系统的能力，sphmulti 是一种精炼的球谐基组，它将额外的径向基与每个径向通道中元素特定的化学权重相结合。我们进一步将超活跃学习、最远点采样和迭代充分性标准与知识引导的关键事件相结合，使 NNAP 能够保留用于描述材料系统的基本信息。对于 3,3'-二氨基-4,4'-偶氮呋喃，生成的 NNAP 仅在 13,680 个构型上进行训练，但捕获了关键特性，包括异构化势垒、热解活化能和冲击 Hugoniot 行为。 NNAP 驱动的分子动力学模拟进一步揭示，热解通过呋喃环开环进行，随后发生链式反应，而冲击载荷则诱导层间结构变形驱动的各向异性氢转移。这项工作为含能材料实现了高效、可靠的 MLP。

English Abstract

Energetic materials involve multiple interactions, making reliable machine-learning potentials (MLPs) difficult to construct without extensive datasets. Here, we enhance the ability of the neural network interatomic potential (NNAP) to describe molecular systems using sphmulti, a refined spherical-harmonic basis set that combines extra radial basis with element-specific chemical weighting in each radial channel. We further integrate hyperactive learning, farthest-point sampling, and iterative sufficiency criteria with knowledge-guided key events, enabling the NNAP to retain essential information for describing the material system. For 3,3'-diamino-4,4'-azoxyfurazan, the resulting NNAP is trained on only 13,680 configurations yet captures key properties, including the isomerization barrier, pyrolysis activation energy, and shock Hugoniot behavior. NNAP-driven molecular dynamics simulations further reveal that pyrolysis proceeds through furazan-ring opening followed by chain reactions, whereas shock loading induces anisotropic hydrogen transfer driven by interlayer structural deformations. This work enables efficient and reliable MLPs for energetic materials.

03

使用经典力场对机器学习原子间势进行基准测试#

原题：Benchmarking machine-learning interatomic potentials using classical force fields
作者：Johannes Sandberg、Geneviève Dusson、Julien Lam。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-10-14

AI×科学交叉8方向匹配8

📄 摘要（中文翻译）：我们通过测试几种著名的机器学习原子间势（线性原子团簇扩展、Behler-Parrinello 高维神经网络势和基于图神经网络的 MACE 势）对它们拟合经典势（如 Lennard-Jones 和 Stillinger-Weber 势）的性能进行理性比较。由此，我们评估了它们在几个方面的优点和局限性：不同的训练数据集大小、对噪声的鲁棒性、碳足迹，以及重现径向分布函数作为可观察示例的能力。总体而言，MACE 模型能够最准确地再现势能面，几乎…

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：interatomic potential、neural network potential、graph neural network。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

我们通过测试几种著名的机器学习原子间势（线性原子团簇扩展、Behler-Parrinello 高维神经网络势和基于图神经网络的 MACE 势）对它们拟合经典势（如 Lennard-Jones 和 Stillinger-Weber 势）的性能进行理性比较。由此，我们评估了它们在几个方面的优点和局限性：不同的训练数据集大小、对噪声的鲁棒性、碳足迹，以及重现径向分布函数作为可观察示例的能力。总体而言，MACE 模型能够最准确地再现势能面，几乎没有过度拟合的迹象，但计算成本很高，这凸显了计算材料科学中模型选择的重要问题。

English Abstract

We propose a rational comparison between several well-known machine-learning interatomic potentials (linear atomic cluster expansion, the Behler-Parrinello high-dimensional neural network potential, and the graph neural network-based MACE potential) by testing their performance for fitting classical potentials such as the Lennard-Jones and Stillinger-Weber potential. With this we assess their advantages and limitations regarding several aspects: varying training dataset size, robustness to noise, carbon footprint, as well as the ability to reproduce the radial distribution function as an example observable. Overall the MACE models are shown to most accurately reproduce the potential energy surface, with little sign of overfitting, but at a significant computational cost, highlighting important questions regarding the choice of models in computational materials science.

04

使用机器学习分子动力学模拟预测负载 CO 2 的水性胺中的溶剂聚集#

原题：Prediction of solvent aggregation in CO 2 -loaded aqueous amines using machine learning molecular dynamics simulations
The Journal of Chemical Physics，2026-10-14
🤖 AI/机器学习
AI×科学交叉8方向匹配8

📄 摘要（中文翻译）：CO 2 捕获期间形成的离子对的聚集可能控制吸收热力学和动力学，但固定电荷经典力场常常高估它。在这里，我们展示了图神经网络原子间势的前景，它提供了量子级的精度，可直接评估负载 CO 2 的胺水溶液中离子产物的聚集趋势。

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：molecular dynamics、interatomic potential、graph neural network。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

CO 2 捕获期间形成的离子对的聚集可能控制吸收热力学和动力学，但固定电荷经典力场常常高估它。在这里，我们展示了图神经网络原子间势的前景，它提供了量子级的精度，可直接评估负载 CO 2 的胺水溶液中离子产物的聚集趋势。

English Abstract

Aggregation of ion pairs formed during CO 2 capture may govern absorption thermodynamics and kinetics, but it is often overestimated by fixed-charge classical force fields. Here, we demonstrate the promise of graph neural network interatomic potentials, which offer quantum-level accuracy, for directly evaluating the aggregation tendencies of ionic products in CO 2 -loaded aqueous amine solutions.

文献研究：Noise-resilient training of machine learning interatomic potentials#

原题：Noise-resilient training of machine learning interatomic potentials
The Journal of Chemical Physics，2026-10-13
🤖 AI/机器学习
AI×科学交叉8方向匹配8

📄 摘要：The accuracy of machine learning interatomic potentials suffers from reference data that contain numerical noise. Often originating from unconverged or inconsistent electronic-structure calculations, this noise is challe…

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：interatomic potential。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
English Abstract

The accuracy of machine learning interatomic potentials suffers from reference data that contain numerical noise. Often originating from unconverged or inconsistent electronic-structure calculations, this noise is challenging to identify. Existing mitigation strategies, such as manual filtering or iterative refinement of outliers, require either substantial expert effort or multiple expensive retraining cycles, making them difficult to scale to large datasets. Here, we introduce a noise-resilient training method that automatically down-weighs noisy samples, without requiring additional reference calculations. By tracking the loss distribution via an exponential moving average, this unsupervised method identifies outliers throughout a single training run. We show that this approach prevents overfitting and matches the performance of iterative refinement baselines with significantly reduced overhead. The method’s effectiveness is demonstrated by recovering accurate physical observables for liquid water from unconverged reference data, including diffusion coefficients. Furthermore, we validate its scalability by training a foundation model for organic chemistry on the SPICE dataset, where it reduces both energy and force errors. This framework provides a simple, automated solution for training robust models on imperfect datasets across dataset sizes.

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：interatomic potential、polaron。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

当多余的电子通过晶格畸变局部化时形成的小极化子在过渡金属（TM）氧化物的电荷传输、催化和降解过程中发挥着关键作用。缺乏明确电子自由度的通用机器学习原子间势（uMLIP）是否可以描述它们仍不清楚。在这里，我们使用基于 TM-O 键长扩展（Z 分数）的几何描述符来研究 H 诱导的电子在一系列 TM 氧化物中的局域化，以识别极化子畸变。我们发现 uMLIP 表现出不同程度的电子局域化，从离域态到亚稳态小极化子，其行为强烈依赖于训练数据和电子相关性的处理。特别是，与 DFT+U 预测相反，训练数据中缺乏对特定 TM 阳离子的 Hubbard U 校正会导致电子离域。尽管一些 uMLIP 在耦合质子-极化子转移过程中再现了极化子跳跃势垒，但它们都无法稳定空间分离的极化子-供体构型，而是有利于 H 掺杂剂附近的局域化。

English Abstract

Small polarons, formed when excess electrons become localized through lattice distortions, play a critical role in charge transport, catalysis, and degradation processes in transition-metal (TM) oxides. Whether universal machine-learning interatomic potentials (uMLIPs), which lack explicit electronic degrees of freedom, can describe them remains unclear. Here, we investigate H-induced electron localization across a range of TM oxides using a geometric descriptor based on TM-O bond-length expansion (Z-score) to identify polaronic distortions. We find that uMLIPs exhibit varying degrees of electron localization, from delocalized states to metastable small polarons, with their behavior strongly dependent on the training data and treatment of electronic correlations. In particular, the absence of Hubbard U corrections for specific TM cations in the training data leads to electron delocalization, in contrast to DFT+U predictions. Although some uMLIPs reproduce polaron hopping barriers during coupled proton-polaron transfer, all of them fail to stabilize spatially separated polaron-donor configurations, instead favoring localization near the H dopant.

原题：From Algebraic Completeness to Orbit Separation: Recursive Separating Invariants for Global Neural Network Potential Energy Surfaces

📄 摘要（中文翻译）：置换不变多项式 (PIP) 为全局势能面 (PES) 提供了系统可改进的、保持对称性的表示，但其作为神经网络描述符的高计算成本限制了其在大型分子系统中的应用。基本不变量 (FI) 形成代数完备的生成集，大大减少了描述符的数量，并使神经网络 PES 能够用于更大的系统。然而，随着分子尺寸和复杂性的增加，PES 评估的计算成本仍然很高。在这里，我们引入了递归分离不变量（RSI），将描述符设计原则从代数完备性转变为轨道分离。 RSI 是通过相…

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：neural network potential。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

置换不变多项式 (PIP) 为全局势能面 (PES) 提供了系统可改进的、保持对称性的表示，但其作为神经网络描述符的高计算成本限制了其在大型分子系统中的应用。基本不变量 (FI) 形成代数完备的生成集，大大减少了描述符的数量，并使神经网络 PES 能够用于更大的系统。然而，随着分子尺寸和复杂性的增加，PES 评估的计算成本仍然很高。在这里，我们引入了递归分离不变量（RSI），将描述符设计原则从代数完备性转变为轨道分离。 RSI 是通过相关顺序和递归深度系统构建的，提供了计算效率更高的表示。反应性和非反应性系统的基准测试表明，基于 RSI 的神经网络 (RSI-NN) PES 的评估速度比 FI-NN 同类产品快大约一个数量级，并且系统相关的预测精度在多种情况下相当。这些结果确立了轨道分离作为为全局神经网络 PES 开发有效的对称性保持描述符的有前途的原理。

English Abstract

Permutationally invariant polynomials (PIPs) provide a systematically improvable, symmetry-preserving representation for global potential energy surfaces (PESs), but their high computational cost as neural network descriptors limits applications to large molecular systems. Fundamental invariants (FIs), which form an algebraically complete generating set, substantially reduce the number of descriptors and enable neural network PESs for larger systems. However, PES evaluation remains computationally expensive as molecular size and complexity increase. Here, we introduce recursive separating invariants (RSIs), shifting the descriptor design principle from algebraic completeness to orbit separation. RSIs are constructed systematically through correlation order and recursion depth, providing a more computationally efficient representation. Benchmarks on reactive and nonreactive systems demonstrate that RSI-based neural network (RSI-NN) PESs achieve approximately an order-of-magnitude faster evaluation than their FI-NN counterparts, with system-dependent prediction accuracy that is comparable in several cases. These results establish orbit separation as a promising principle for developing efficient symmetry-preserving descriptors for global neural network PESs.
