关联：与你方向的关键词重合：interatomic potential、density functional theory、molecular dynamics。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

大多数机器学习原子间势（MLIP）都是根据气相数据进行训练的，这限制了它们在溶液化学中的应用。在这里，我们引入了 AnySolv，一种可转移的隐式溶剂化势，可为现有气相势提供依赖于溶剂的能量和力校正，而无需重新训练它们。它接受了 140 万个溶剂化能和力的训练，这些溶剂化能和力是通过密度泛函理论 (DFT) 和 SMD 在 36 种溶剂中计算得出的，涵盖带电、开壳层和过渡态结构。由于溶剂由连续的物理描述符表示，因此 AnySolv 支持 179 种溶剂，包括训练中缺少的溶剂。在外部基准测试中，AnySolv 以低三个数量级以上的成本将 DFT-SMD 溶剂化能再现为 2-4 meV 平均绝对误差，并且它与 DFT-SMD 与实验溶剂化自由能的精度相匹配。这使得能够筛选六个阶段中 120 个亲核芳香族取代反应的机制，以及分子动力学模拟，重现实验观察到的丙氨酸二肽的溶剂依赖性构象偏好。通过为现有 MLIP 提供可转移的溶剂化校正，AnySolv 能够对溶剂依赖性化学反应性和分子动力学进行可扩展的模拟。

English Abstract

Most machine-learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs) are trained on gas-phase data, which limits their use for chemistry in solution. Here, we introduce AnySolv, a transferable implicit solvation potential that provides solvent-dependent energy and force corrections to existing gas-phase potentials without retraining them. It is trained on 1.4 million solvation energies and forces computed with density functional theory (DFT) and SMD in 36 solvents, spanning charged, open-shell, and transition-state structures. Because solvents are represented by continuous physical descriptors, AnySolv supports 179 solvents, including those absent from training. On external benchmarks, AnySolv reproduces DFT-SMD solvation energies to 2-4 meV mean absolute error at more than three orders of magnitude lower cost, and it matches the accuracy of DFT-SMD against experimental solvation free energies. This enables screening of the mechanisms of 120 nucleophilic aromatic substitution reactions in six phases as well as molecular dynamics simulations that reproduce the experimentally observed solvent-dependent conformational preferences of alanine dipeptide. By providing transferable solvation corrections to existing MLIPs, AnySolv enables scalable simulations of solvent-dependent chemical reactivity and molecular dynamics.

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：density functional theory、equivariant neural network。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

从材料结构预测密度泛函理论 (DFT) 哈密顿量的深度神经网络提供了高效电子结构计算的途径。然而，将定域原子轨道 (AO) 哈密顿学习扩展到具有混合泛函的平面波 (PW) 计算需要一个紧凑的 AO 表示，以保留非定域算子的影响。在这里，我们提出了一种从一小部分选定的 PW 本征态和特征值中构建非正交 AO 哈密顿量的可投影性引导构造。保留高可投射性状态，并选择更高能量的状态来完成 AO 子空间，避免经验转变及其对学习目标的任意贡献。由于该构造使用 PW 特征状态和特征值，因此它合并了参考计算中存在的非局部贡献，而无需显式重建其 AO 矩阵元素。 MoS2 和钠相的基准显示，使用比真实空间重建更小的 AO 基数可以准确再现 PW 能带。由此产生的 PBE 和 HSE 哈密顿量可以通过等变神经网络来学习，将 PW 参考计算与电子结构预测联系起来。这种方法扩大了可用于 AO 哈密顿学习的 PW DFT 计算范围，并实现紧凑、准确的训练目标，以开发更广泛适用的电子结构模型。

English Abstract

Deep neural networks that predict density functional theory (DFT) Hamiltonians from material structures offer a route to efficient electronic-structure calculations. However, extending localized atomic-orbital (AO) Hamiltonian learning to plane-wave (PW) calculations with hybrid functionals requires a compact AO representation that preserves the effects of nonlocal operators. Here we propose a projectability-guided construction of nonorthogonal AO Hamiltonians from a small, selected set of PW eigenstates and eigenvalues. High-projectability states are retained, and selected higher-energy states complete the AO subspace, avoiding an empirical shift and its arbitrary contribution to the learning targets. Because the construction uses PW eigenstates and eigenvalues, it incorporates nonlocal contributions present in the reference calculation without explicitly reconstructing their AO matrix elements. Benchmarks for MoS2 and sodium phases show accurate reproduction of PW bands with smaller AO bases than real-space reconstruction. The resulting PBE and HSE Hamiltonians can be learned by equivariant neural networks, connecting PW reference calculations with electronic-structure prediction. This approach broadens the range of PW DFT calculations available for AO Hamiltonian learning and enables compact, accurate training targets for developing more widely applicable electronic-structure models.

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：density functional theory。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

掺杂剂可以通过吸附质和过渡态 (TS) 能量的变化来调节氧化锆基 CO2 加氢催化剂的活性。当必须筛选多种掺杂剂、反应中间体和 TS 时，使用密度泛函理论 (DFT) 计算这些量变得昂贵。在这里，我们进一步开发了基于图的机器学习方法 Wasserstein Weisfeiler-Lehman 高斯过程回归 (WWL-GPR)：我们引入了与氧化物表面相关的附加电子结构和局部环境特征，并使用所得模型来预测掺杂 Al3⁺、Cd2⁺、Ga3⁺、In3⁺ 和 Zn2⁺ 的 ZrO2(101) 表面上的吸附和 TS 能量。吸附物表面结构表示为结合了键连接性和特征的属性图。我们将 WWL-GPR 与反应中间体的热力学标度关系 (TSR) 和 TS 的布朗斯台德-埃文斯-波兰尼关系 (BEP) 进行基准测试，然后将预测的能量传播到微动力学 CH₃OH 产率估计。相对于线性标度方法，WWL-GPR 持续改进了反应中间体能量学的预测，并提供了与基于 BEP 的模型相媲美的 TS 估计。当预测的能量通过反应网络传播时，这些改进变得尤其重要。微动力学计算表明，与线性关系工作流程相比，WWL-GPR 衍生的能量使 CH₃OH 产率更接近 DFT 衍生的产率。速率控制程度分析将活度误差与掺杂剂相关的速率控制步骤联系起来，包括 HCOO* 形成、H2COOH* 解离和 H₃CO* 形成。这些结果支持 WWL-GPR 作为一种数据高效的替代品，用于将原子能量学与掺杂氧化物表面上的催化剂筛选可观测值联系起来。

English Abstract

Dopants can tune the activity of zirconia-based CO₂ hydrogenation catalysts through changes in adsorbate and transition-state (TS) energetics. Computing these quantities with density functional theory (DFT) becomes expensive when several dopants, reaction intermediates, and TSs must be screened. Here, we further develop the graph-based machine learning method Wasserstein Weisfeiler-Lehman Gaussian Process Regression (WWL-GPR): we introduce additional electronic-structure and local-environment features relevant for oxide surfaces and use the resulting model to predict adsorption and TS energies on ZrO₂(101) surfaces doped with Al³⁺, Cd²⁺, Ga³⁺, In³⁺, and Zn²⁺. Adsorbate-surface structures are represented as attributed graphs that combine bonds connectivity and features. We benchmark WWL-GPR against thermodynamic scaling relations (TSR) for reaction intermediates and Bronsted-Evans-Polanyi relations (BEP) for TSs, and then propagate the predicted energies to microkinetic CH₃OH-yield estimates. WWL-GPR consistently improves the prediction of reaction intermediate energetics relative to linear scaling approaches and provides TS estimates that are competitive with BEP-based models. These improvements become especially relevant when the predicted energetics are propagated through the reaction network. Microkinetic calculations show that WWL-GPR-derived energies give CH₃OH yields closer to DFT-derived yields than the linear-relation workflow. Degree-of-rate-control analysis links the activity errors to dopant-dependent rate-controlling steps, including HCOO* formation, H₂COOH* dissociation, and H₃CO* formation. These results support WWL-GPR as a data-efficient surrogate for connecting atomistic energetics with catalyst-screening observables on doped oxide surfaces.

原题：Extrapolating Collisional Rate Coefficients for Molecules of Astrophysical Interest Using Neural Networks

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

非弹性分子碰撞的态间速率系数是非局部热力学平衡辐射传递模型的重要输入，但使用量子散射方法进行计算对于大分子和高度激发的旋转态是禁止的。我们研究了人工神经网络在不执行额外散射计算的情况下扩展现有碰撞激发率系数数据集的能力。使用分子量子数、能级能量、能隙和动力学温度作为描述符来训练多层感知器模型，并针对对数速率系数进行优化。该模型在不同的碰撞系统上进行了测试，从简单的双原子分子（CO、SiO）到开壳层物质（SO）、小型不对称顶部（H2O、SO2）以及与 H2 分子或 He 原子碰撞的复杂有机分子（苯甲腈和乙烯基氰）。针对更高的旋转水平、更高的温度以及两者的组合进行了外推。对于大多数系统，涉及先前未见过的旋转状态的速率系数的预测产生的平均误差因子在 1.1 和 1.5 之间，主要速率系数通常比较弱的转变更准确地再现。事实证明，温度外推法特别成功，而在大多数情况下，温度和旋转水平的组合外推法仍然准确到两倍以内。预测质量取决于数据集大小、温度范围、分子复杂性以及明确的碰撞倾向规则的存在之间的相互作用。总体而言，该方法表明，机器学习可以为扩展天体物理应用的碰撞数据集提供实用途径，特别是对于需要额外量子散射计算的大分子。

English Abstract

State-to-state rate coefficients for inelastic molecular collisions are essential inputs for non-local thermodynamic equilibrium radiative transfer models, yet their computation using quantum scattering methods is prohibitive for large molecules and highly excited rotational states. We investigate the ability of artificial neural networks to extend existing datasets of collisional excitation rate coefficients without performing additional scattering calculations. A multilayer perceptron model was trained using molecular quantum numbers, level energies, energy gaps, and kinetic temperature as descriptors, and optimized on logarithmic rate coefficients. The model was tested on diverse collision systems ranging from simple diatomic molecules (CO, SiO) to open-shell species (SO), small asymmetric tops (H₂O, SO₂), and complex organic molecules (benzonitrile and vinyl cyanide) colliding with H₂ molecules or He atoms. Extrapolations were performed toward higher rotational levels, higher temperatures, and combinations of both. For most systems, predictions of rate coefficients involving previously unseen rotational states yielded mean error factors between 1.1 and 1.5, with the dominant rate coefficients generally reproduced more accurately than weaker transitions. Temperature extrapolations proved particularly successful, while combined extrapolations in temperature and rotational levels remained accurate to within a factor of two in most cases. The prediction quality depends on the interplay between dataset size, temperature range, molecular complexity, and the presence of clear collisional propensity rules. Overall, the approach demonstrates that machine learning can provide a practical route to extending collisional datasets for astrophysical applications, especially for large molecules for which additional quantum-scattering calculations are computationally demanding.

PolyFKAN：将 Fourier-KAN 层集成到图神经网络中以进行聚合物性能预测#

原题：PolyFKAN: Integrating Fourier-KAN layers into graph neural networks for polymer property prediction
The Journal of Chemical Physics，2026-10-14

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：graph neural networks。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

图神经网络已成为聚合物性能预测的核心工具，但它们的固定激活函数可以施加低通光谱偏差，从而平滑区分化学相似聚合物的尖锐的多尺度结构性能变化。我们使用 PolyFKAN 解决了这一限制，这是一种多任务图学习架构，它通过 Fourier Kolmogorov-Arnold 网络层增强了 PolyGNN 主干的预测头。 PolyFKAN 将边缘激活表示为截断的傅里叶级数，并通过有界残差门将生成的光谱分支耦合到标准多层感知器 (MLP) 路径，从而在不破坏学习的图嵌入的情况下提高功能表达能力。在涵盖电子、热力学、光学/介电、热和气体渗透性领域的 17 种聚合物特性中，PolyFKAN 在 17 个目标中的 11 个（单侧 t 检验，p < 0.05）上比严格控制的 PolyGNN 基线实现了统计上的显着改进（单侧 t 检验，p < 0.05），另外一个目标显示出边际改进。多尺度特性的收益尤其明显，包括大渗透气体渗透率的平均均方根误差减少超过 15%，光学和介电目标的改善超过 15%。分层消融进一步揭示，最佳傅立叶带宽取决于属性：困难的多尺度目标需要更宽的频谱容量和更强的幅度控制，而高保真目标则受益于较轻的正则化。这些结果将 Fourier-KAN 预测头确立为聚合物信息学的微创且物理对齐的归纳偏差。

English Abstract

Graph neural networks have become a central tool for polymer property prediction, but their fixed activation functions can impose a low-pass spectral bias that smooths over the sharp, multi-scale structure–property variations that distinguish chemically similar polymers. We address this limitation with PolyFKAN, a multitask graph-learning architecture that augments the prediction head of a polyGNN backbone with Fourier Kolmogorov–Arnold network layers. PolyFKAN represents edge-wise activations as truncated Fourier series and couples the resulting spectral branch to the standard multilayer perceptron (MLP) pathway through a bounded residual gate, thereby increasing functional expressivity without disrupting the learned graph embeddings. Across 17 polymer properties spanning electronic, thermodynamic, optical/dielectric, thermal, and gas-permeability domains, PolyFKAN achieves statistically significant improvement over a tightly controlled polyGNN baseline on 11 of 17 targets (one-sided t -test, p < 0.05), with one additional target showing marginal improvement. The gains are especially pronounced for multi-scale properties, including more than 15% average root mean squared error reduction for large-penetrant gas permeabilities and more than 15% improvement across optical and dielectric targets. Hierarchical ablation further reveals that optimal Fourier bandwidth is property-dependent: difficult, multi-scale targets require broader spectral capacity with stronger amplitude control, whereas high-fidelity targets benefit from lighter regularization. These results establish Fourier-KAN prediction heads as a minimally invasive and physically aligned inductive bias for polymer informatics.

Wisevolve 潜在引擎：在单个 GPU 上每秒 1 亿原子步的机器学习分子动力学#

📄 摘要（中文翻译）：通用分子动力学软件支持不同的原子间势和模拟要求。在模拟阶段，随着原子构型的演变，加载的势模型和所需的计算通常保持固定。我们提出了 Wisevolve 势引擎 (WPE)，它使用来自 GPUMD 的信息来选择和重用针对模型和仿真阶段量身定制的 GPU 执行策略。 GPUMD 保留模拟控制和模拟数据的所有权。在单个 NVIDIA RTX 5090 上，支持 WPE 的 GPUMD (GPUMD-WPE) 在 106 个神经进化潜能 (NEP…

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：molecular dynamics、interatomic potential。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

通用分子动力学软件支持不同的原子间势和模拟要求。在模拟阶段，随着原子构型的演变，加载的势模型和所需的计算通常保持固定。我们提出了 Wisevolve 势引擎 (WPE)，它使用来自 GPUMD 的信息来选择和重用针对模型和仿真阶段量身定制的 GPU 执行策略。 GPUMD 保留模拟控制和模拟数据的所有权。在单个 NVIDIA RTX 5090 上，支持 WPE 的 GPUMD (GPUMD-WPE) 在 106 个神经进化潜能 (NEP) 工作负载中实现了 1.09 亿原子步/秒的中位吞吐量。对于 1510 万原子金刚石硅系统，它可以维持 1.42 亿原子步/秒。相对于 GPUMD，这些 NEP 工作负载的几何平均加速为 3.49 倍，使用动态电荷 (qNEP) 的 NEP 的 16 个工作负载的几何平均加速为 3.61 倍，28 个经典势工作负载的几何平均加速为 1.24 倍，56 个路径积分分子动力学 (PIMD) 配置的几何平均加速为 10.5 倍。水结构、晶格热导率和 PIMD 热膨胀的端到端计算与相应的 GPUMD 结果和已发布的模拟结果非常一致。单独的 1250 万原子 Cu 冲击压缩模拟证明了其对大规模非平衡动力学的适用性。这些结果支持特定于模型和阶段的专业化，作为加速通用分子动力学工作流程中 GPU 潜力评估的实用方法。

English Abstract

General-purpose molecular dynamics software supports diverse interatomic potentials and simulation requirements. Within a simulation stage, the loaded potential model and the required calculations typically remain fixed as the atomic configuration evolves. We present the Wisevolve Potential Engine (WPE), which uses this information from GPUMD to select and reuse a GPU execution policy tailored to the model and simulation stage. GPUMD retains simulation control and ownership of the simulation data. On a single NVIDIA RTX 5090, WPE-enabled GPUMD (GPUMD-WPE) achieves a median throughput of 109 million atom-step/s across 106 neuroevolution potential (NEP) workloads. For a 15.1-million-atom diamond Si system, it sustains 142 million atom-step/s. Relative to GPUMD, geometric-mean speedups are 3.49x across these NEP workloads, 3.61x across 16 workloads using NEP with dynamic charges (qNEP), 1.24x across 28 classical-potential workloads, and 10.5x across 56 path-integral molecular dynamics (PIMD) configurations. End-to-end calculations of water structure, lattice thermal conductivity, and PIMD thermal expansion show close agreement with corresponding GPUMD results and published simulation results. A separate 12.5-million-atom Cu shock-compression simulation demonstrates its applicability to large-scale nonequilibrium dynamics. These results support model- and stage-specific specialization as a practical way to accelerate GPU potential evaluation in general-purpose molecular dynamics workflows.

机器学习原子间势的风险控制 DFT 选择#

原题：Risk-Controlled DFT Selection for Machine-Learned Interatomic Potentials

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：interatomic potential、density functional theory。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

自动化材料发现需要评估大量候选配置，其中密度泛函理论 (DFT) 是主要的吞吐量瓶颈。预训练的机器学习原子间势 (MLIP) 可以廉价地近似 DFT，但它们的误差在不同配置之间存在数量级差异，因此很难决定何时信任 MLIP 预测以及何时回退到 DFT。我们将此决策视为风险控制选择问题，并在保形风险控制（CRC）框架内解决它。每个配置都从原子序数和笛卡尔坐标映射到固定的几何描述符，轻量级预测器使用该描述符来估计能量、平均力和最大力的 MLIP-DFT 不一致得分。然后，CRC 将此分数校准为 DFT 标记的选择阈值，以便保留其 MLIP 标记的未选择配置上的 MLIP 误差保持在用户选择的预期误差分数 α 以下。在八个数据集-MLIP 对的相同保证下，我们的预测器在 α=0.3 时将路由到 DFT 的配置比委员会基线分别减少 7% 和 14%。由于委员会的分歧忽略了 MLIP 共有的错误，因此当 DFT 标签和 MLIP 训练数据在 DFT 函数和基础集上不同时，我们的收益最大，而当两者一致时，我们的收益会缩小。如果没有模型访问，纯结构预测器也会对错误以及目标 MLIP 的潜在表示进行排名，并且优于广泛使用的描述符，例如原子位置平滑重叠 (SOAP) 和原子中心对称函数 (ACSF)。

English Abstract

Automated materials discovery requires evaluating large pools of candidate configurations, with density functional theory (DFT) as a major throughput bottleneck. Pretrained machine-learned interatomic potentials (MLIPs) approximate DFT cheaply, but their errors vary by orders of magnitude across configurations, making it difficult to decide when to trust the MLIP predictions and when to fall back to DFT. We frame this decision as a risk-controlled selection problem and solve it within a conformal risk control (CRC) framework. Each configuration is mapped from atomic numbers and Cartesian coordinates to a fixed geometric descriptor, which a lightweight predictor uses to estimate an MLIP-DFT disagreement score in energy, average force, and maximum force. CRC then calibrates this score into a selection threshold for DFT labeling such that the MLIP error on the unselected configurations, which keep their MLIP labels, stays below a user-chosen fraction α of the expected error. Under an identical guarantee across eight dataset-MLIP pairs, our predictor routes 7% and 14% fewer configurations to DFT at α=0.3 than committee baselines. Because committee disagreement overlooks errors the MLIPs share, our gains are largest when the DFT labels and MLIP training data differ in DFT functional and basis set, and narrow when the two agree. Without model access, the structure-only predictor also ranks errors as well as the target MLIP's latent representation and outperforms widely used descriptors such as Smooth Overlap of Atomic Positions (SOAP) and Atom-Centered Symmetry Functions (ACSF).

ActMOF：通过主动学习绘制网状综合#

原题：ActMOF: Mapping Reticular Synthesis through Active Learning

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：active learning。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

网状化学允许从其构建单元设计金属有机框架（MOF），但其合成仍然受到实验数据稀疏、结晶结果嘈杂以及缺乏比较主动学习策略的基准的限制。虽然贝叶斯优化 (BO) 等数据驱动方法可以提高合成成功率，但预先选择合适的优化器来绘制 MOF 结晶图谱在很大程度上仍然是凭经验得出的。在这里，我们提出 ActMOF，一个基于实验的基准，用于比较羧酸铝 MOF 合成中的搜索策略。 ActMOF 是通过实验测量的微波合成来模拟的，每个编码条件都分配了一个基于衍射峰强度和半峰全宽计算的结晶度指数。通过对高斯过程 (GP)、树集成和大型语言模型 (LLM) 指导的政策进行基准测试，ActMOF 将 GP 系列置于稀疏合成环境中主动学习的领先政策之列。然后，它通过引导微波合成六种网状相关的铝 MOF 来证明可转移性。所有六项活动均在 12 次实验的固定预算内提供了晶体产品，并为人工智能驱动的 MOF 合成建立了可重复使用的测试平台。

English Abstract

Reticular chemistry allows metal-organic frameworks (MOFs) to be designed from their building units, yet their synthesis remains constrained by sparse experimental data, noisy crystallization outcomes, and the absence of benchmarks for comparing active-learning strategies. While data-driven approaches such as Bayesian optimization (BO) can improve synthesis success rates, selecting appropriate optimizers beforehand for mapping MOF crystallization landscapes remains largely empirical. Here we present ActMOF, an experiment-grounded benchmark for comparing search strategies in aluminum-carboxylate MOF synthesis. ActMOF is emulated from experimentally measured microwave syntheses with each encoded condition assigned a crystallinity index calculated based on diffraction peak intensity and full width at half-maximum. By benchmarking policies guided by Gaussian processes (GP), tree ensembles and large language models (LLM), ActMOF placed the GP family among the leading policies for active learning in sparse synthesis landscapes. It then demonstrated transferability by guiding the microwave synthesis of six reticularly related aluminum MOFs. All six campaigns gave crystalline products within a fixed budget of 12 experiments each, and established a reusable testbed for AI-driven MOF synthesis.

用于异质材料断裂预测的热力学一致机器学习#

原题：Thermodynamically Consistent Machine Learning for Fracture Prediction in Heterogeneous Materials
作者：Muhamad Alim、Xiaolong He、Mei-Na Tsao、Chi-Hua Yu、J. S. Chen。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-10-16

📄 摘要（中文翻译）：了解断裂行为对于评估结构完整性和寿命至关重要。高度耗时的基于物理的断裂模拟促进了基于机器学习的替代模型断裂演化预测的发展。然而，大多数现有的断裂预测机器学习模型都是作为黑匣子运行的，需要大量的训练数据集才能可靠地概括。在这项工作中，引入参数系统的热力学一致潜在动力学识别（tLaSDI）来预测异质材料的断裂模式和演化。该模型使用参数化通用形式主义通知神经网络 (pGFINN) 来学习低维潜在空间中的热力学状态变量，该网络强制执行关键的热力…

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

了解断裂行为对于评估结构完整性和寿命至关重要。高度耗时的基于物理的断裂模拟促进了基于机器学习的替代模型断裂演化预测的发展。然而，大多数现有的断裂预测机器学习模型都是作为黑匣子运行的，需要大量的训练数据集才能可靠地概括。在这项工作中，引入参数系统的热力学一致潜在动力学识别（tLaSDI）来预测异质材料的断裂模式和演化。该模型使用参数化通用形式主义通知神经网络 (pGFINN) 来学习低维潜在空间中的热力学状态变量，该网络强制执行关键的热力学原理，例如总能量守恒和熵生成。该模型使用本质上符合基本热力学原理的硬约束方法将物理学嵌入到神经网络架构中。所提出的 tLaSDI-pGFINN 通过几何条件自动编码器在学习几何配置方面得到进一步增强，以实现有效的几何引导学习。能量和熵演化历史表明该模型保留了对未见过的测试样本的热力学约束。结果进一步表明，与纯数据驱动的自动编码器长短期记忆 (AE-LSTM) 模型相比，tLaSDI-pGFINN 可以更准确地捕获断裂模式和随后的断裂演化。

English Abstract

Understanding fracture behavior is critical for assessing the structural integrity and longevity. Highly time-consuming physics-based fracture simulations motivate the development of machine-learning-based fracture evolution prediction with surrogate models. However, most existing machine-learning models for fracture prediction operate as black boxes and require large training datasets to generalize reliably. In this work, Thermodynamically Consistent Latent Dynamics Identification (tLaSDI) for parametric systems is introduced to predict fracture patterns and evolution in heterogeneous materials. The model learns the thermodynamic state variable in a lower-dimensional latent space using parametric GENERIC Formalism-Informed Neural Networks (pGFINNs) that enforce key thermodynamic principles, such as total energy conservation and entropy generation. This model embeds physics into the neural network architecture using a hard-constraint method that inherently complies with the underlying thermodynamic principles. The proposed tLaSDI-pGFINNs is further enhanced by a Geometry-Conditioned Autoencoder in learning the geometry configuration for effective geometry-guided learning. The energy and entropy evolution histories demonstrate that the model preserves the thermodynamic constraints on unseen testing samples. The results further show that tLaSDI-pGFINNs can more accurately capture both fracture patterns and the subsequent fracture evolution than the purely data-driven Autoencoder Long Short-Term Memory (AE-LSTM) model.

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

我们提出了一种保对称玻色子神经网络（BINN），用于连续空间中相互作用玻色子基态的变分蒙特卡罗计算。该框架明确区分了系统的两个主要物理尺度。在微观相互作用尺度上，细胞贾斯特罗因子吸收了主要的近接触刚度，而在宏观尺度上，电位感知包络对外部限制进行编码，使神经网络能够捕获更平滑的多体残差。在二维和三维测试中，BINN 在能量和密度方面均以经验 O(N⁻1) 有限尺寸趋势接近 Hartree 和 Gross-Pitaevskii 极限。在三维收缩范围情况下，对于测试的粒子数高达 350，能量与扩散蒙特卡罗一致，误差在 0.06% 以内。在各种捕获场景下进行的应用证明了 BINN 的稳健性能。

English Abstract

We propose a symmetry-preserving Boson-informed neural network (BINN) for the variational Monte Carlo computation of interacting-boson ground states in continuous space. The framework explicitly separates the two dominant physical scales of the system. At the microscopic interaction scale, a cell Jastrow factor absorbs the leading near-contact stiffness, while at the macroscopic scale, a potential-aware envelope encodes the external confinement, leaving the neural network to capture a smoother many-body residual. Across both two- and three-dimensional tests, the BINN approaches the Hartree and Gross-Pitaevskii limits with an empirical O(N⁻¹) finite-size trend in both energy and density. The energies agree with diffusion Monte Carlo to within 0.06% for the particle number tested up to 350 in the three-dimensional shrinking-range case. Applications are made under various trapping scenarios, justifying robust performance of BINN.

利用深度学习从二维红外光谱推断跃迁偶极子#

作者：Hao-Yue Zhang、Rong-Hang Chen、Qing Ai。arXiv · physics，2026-10-15

AI×科学交叉8方向匹配5

📄 摘要（中文翻译）：二维红外 (2D IR) 光谱是一种强大的非线性技术，用于探测振动耦合、非谐性、相干性和分子动力学。在二维红外光谱编码的微观参数中，过渡偶极子是表征分子结构和控制光与物质相互作用的基本量。然而，从光谱数据重建这些量仍然是一个具有挑战性的逆问题。在这里，我们介绍了一种基于物理的卷积神经网络 (CNN) Spec2DipoleNet，它通过非厄米哈密顿量 (NHH) 方法生成的 2D 红外光谱进行训练。这种多任务 CNN 在物理驱动的掩蔽约…

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：molecular dynamics。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

二维红外 (2D IR) 光谱是一种强大的非线性技术，用于探测振动耦合、非谐性、相干性和分子动力学。在二维红外光谱编码的微观参数中，过渡偶极子是表征分子结构和控制光与物质相互作用的基本量。然而，从光谱数据重建这些量仍然是一个具有挑战性的逆问题。在这里，我们介绍了一种基于物理的卷积神经网络 (CNN) Spec2DipoleNet，它通过非厄米哈密顿量 (NHH) 方法生成的 2D 红外光谱进行训练。这种多任务 CNN 在物理驱动的掩蔽约束下重建过渡偶极子矩阵。在验证集上，该模型的均方根误差 (RMSE) 为 0.085，R² 为 0.97，归一化互相关为 0.98。它可靠地恢复与允许的跃迁相关的矩阵元素，而对于名义上禁止的跃迁则观察到较大的偏差。我们的结果表明，可以从二维红外光谱中定量推断跃迁偶极子算子，凸显了基于物理的深度学习从多维光谱数据中提取微观量子参数的潜力。

English Abstract

Two-dimensional infrared (2D IR) spectroscopy is a powerful nonlinear technique for probing vibrational couplings, anharmonicities, coherence, and molecular dynamics. Among the microscopic parameters encoded in 2D IR spectra, transition dipoles are fundamental quantities that characterize molecular structure and govern light-matter interactions. However, reconstructing these quantities from spectroscopic data remains a challenging inverse problem. Here, we introduce a physics-informed convolutional neural network (CNN), Spec2DipoleNet, trained on 2D IR spectra generated via the non-Hermitian Hamiltonian (NHH) method. This multi-task CNN reconstructs the transition dipole matrices under physically-motivated masking constraints. On the validation set, the model achieves a root-mean-square error (RMSE) of 0.085, an R² of 0.97, and a normalized cross-correlation of 0.98. It reliably recovers matrix elements associated with allowed transitions, while larger deviations are observed for nominally forbidden transitions. Our results suggest that transition dipole operators can be quantitatively inferred from 2D IR spectra, highlighting the potential of physics-informed deep learning for extracting microscopic quantum parameters from multidimensional spectroscopic data.

通过神经网络跟踪大型化学反应网络和罕见事件#

作者：Jiayu Weng、Xinyi Zhu、Jing Liu、Linyuan Lü、Pan Zhang、Ying Tang。arXiv · physics，2026-10-15

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

化学反应网络广泛用于模拟生物物理过程中的随机动力学。由于状态空间的指数增长，通过化学主方程进行精确的概率描述对于高维系统来说变得困难。当亚稳态转变和罕见事件主导动态时，这种困难会进一步加剧。在这里，我们提出了一种先进的神经网络方法，可以在统一的框架内有效地处理大型反应网络和罕见事件动力学。基于全联合概率分布的自回归表示，我们通过二阶优化方法（包括自然梯度下降和时间相关变分原理）实现了高效训练。我们利用增强采样策略来准确解决管理罕见转变的低概率区域。我们在多稳态遗传切换开关和丝裂原激活蛋白激酶 (MAPK) 级联上演示了该方法，该级联由 16 个物种和 35 个密集耦合反应组成。我们进一步量化一维和二维晶格中空间扩展 Schlögl 模型中的罕见事件，达到系统尺寸，状态空间极大，高达 3×10⁵。这些结果凸显了神经建模作为研究高维化学反应网络和罕见事件的灵活且可扩展的框架的潜力。

English Abstract

Chemical reaction networks are widely used to model stochastic dynamics in biophysical processes. Their exact probabilistic description via the chemical master equation becomes intractable for high-dimensional systems due to the exponential growth of the state space. This difficulty is further exacerbated when metastable transitions and rare events dominate the dynamics. Here, we present an advanced neural-network approach that effectively tackles large reaction networks and rare-event dynamics within a unified framework. Building on autoregressive representations of the full joint probability distribution, we achieve efficient training through second-order optimization methods, including natural gradient descent and the time-dependent variational principle. We leverage enhanced-sampling strategies that enable accurate resolution of low-probability regions governing rare transitions. We demonstrate the method on a multistable genetic toggle switch and a mitogen-activated protein kinase (MAPK) cascade consisting of 16 species and 35 reactions with dense coupling. We further quantify rare events in spatially extended Schlögl models in both one- and two-dimensional lattices, reaching system sizes with extremely large state spaces up to 3×10¹⁵. These results highlight the potential of neural modeling as a flexible and scalable framework for studying high-dimensional chemical reaction networks and rare events.

迁移学习辅助的人工智能方法可评估经典和量子光子应用材料的光学特性#

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：artificial neural network。

页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

在这项研究中，对不同材料的折射率进行了人工智能辅助评估。折射率的评估对于设计用于各种经典和量子光子应用的光子器件以及从理论上分析实验结果是必要的。折射率是影响光与物质相互作用的关键参数之一。虽然折射率可以通过实验测量，但通过计算手段进行估计具有不需要昂贵仪器、训练有素的研究人员的优点，并且可以避免潜在的基于实验室的不确定性。因此，在本研究中，人工智能被用来估计两种材料（即贵金属和半导体）的光学常数。为此，使用人工神经网络来估计特定材料对于各种光波长的折射率的实部和虚部。然后，利用经过训练的神经网络的知识来启动和训练新的神经网络来估计其他材料的折射率。对于神经网络的训练，可以采用各种方法。然而，培训的应用可能是一个耗时的过程。这里采用迁移学习方法来为所研究的问题提供更快的训练解决方案。与非基于知识迁移的训练相比，迁移学习的实施允许以更高的速度或准确性训练新材料的神经网络。我们的结果表明所提出的技术在准确评估不同材料的折射率的实部和虚部方面的效率。

English Abstract

In this study, an artificial intelligence-assisted evaluation of the refractive indices of different materials is performed. The evaluation of the refractive index is necessary to design photonic devices for various classical and quantum photonic applications, and to theoretically analyse experimental results. The refractive index is one of the crucial parameters that affect the interaction of light with matter. Although the refractive index can be measured experimentally, its estimation via computational means provide the advantage of not requiring costly instrumentation, trained researchers and could avoid potential laboratory-based uncertainties. Therefore, in this study, artificial intelligence is implemented to estimate the optical constants of two types of materials, i.e., noble metals, and semiconductors. To this end, an artificial neural network is used to estimate the real and the imaginary parts of the refractive index of a specific material for various wavelengths of light. Then, the knowledge of the trained neural network is employed to initiate and train new neural networks to estimate the refractive indices of other materials. For the training of a neural network, various methods can be deployed. However, the application of the training can be a time-consuming process. A transfer learning approach is here implemented to provide a faster training solution for the problem under study. The implementation of transfer learning allows training the neural networks for a new material with higher speed or accuracy when compared to non-knowledge transfer-based training. Our results show the efficiency of the proposed technique in accurately evaluating the real and imaginary parts of the refractive index of different materials.

学习用卷积神经网络重建 Germano 差分滤波场以进行等离子体湍流的大涡模拟#

原题：Learning to reconstruct Germano Differential Filtered Fields with Convolutional Neural Networks for Large Eddy Simulation of Plasma Turbulence
作者：Jony Castagna、Josh Williams。arXiv · physics，2026-10-15
🤖 AI/机器学习
AI×科学交叉8方向匹配5

📄 摘要（中文翻译）：我们提出了一种新颖的方法，它将 Germano 微分滤波器的可逆性与卷积网络相结合，以加速等离子体湍流模拟。该方法不是学习从粗网格 LES 字段到细网格 DNS 的映射，而是学习从粗网格 LES 字段到差分过滤 DNS 字段的映射。然后，我们使用 Germano 滤波器的逆来重建精细尺度细节，从而精确计算 LES 方程中控制方程中的非线性项和亚网格尺度项。该方法在 256² 和 512² DNS 分辨率的 2D Hasekawa-Wak…

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

我们提出了一种新颖的方法，它将 Germano 微分滤波器的可逆性与卷积网络相结合，以加速等离子体湍流模拟。该方法不是学习从粗网格 LES 字段到细网格 DNS 的映射，而是学习从粗网格 LES 字段到差分过滤 DNS 字段的映射。然后，我们使用 Germano 滤波器的逆来重建精细尺度细节，从而精确计算 LES 方程中控制方程中的非线性项和亚网格尺度项。该方法在 256² 和 512² DNS 分辨率的 2D Hasekawa-Wakatani (HW) 和改进的 Hasekawa-Wakatani (mHW) 等离子体湍流模型上进行了测试，在每个方向上使用 2、4 和 8 倍更小的 LES 网格。我们将模型与 DNS 结果在能量、熵、光谱、概率分布、极向和径向通量以及计算成本方面进行比较。验证扩展到 HW 的 1000 个模拟时间单位和 mHW 的 100 个模拟时间单位，其更快的动态导致层化。对于 256² 情况，更精细的 LES 网格保留了一些积分和光谱特征，而最粗糙的网格会产生非物理行为。相比之下，对于 512² 情况，即使是最粗糙的 LES 网格也能保留流动的湍流特征，并保持 HW 和 mHW 模型可接受的统计数据。此外，我们还展示了我们的方法如何优于传统的代数数值 LES 模型，传统的代数数值 LES 模型传统上无法捕获重要的设计特征。最后，基准测试显示，与全 CPU 插槽相比，MPI 任务时间减少了 4.82--49.45，最大挂钟加速为 1.55 倍。

English Abstract

We present a novel method which combines the invertibility of the differential filter of Germano, with convolutional networks to accelerate plasma turbulence simulations. Rather than learning the mapping from coarse-grid LES fields to fine-grid DNS, the proposed method learns the mapping from coarse LES fields to the differential-filtered DNS fields. We then use the inverse of Germano's filter to reconstruct the fine-scale details and hence calculate exactly the nonlinear terms in the governing equations and subgrid-scale terms in the LES equations. The method is tested on the 2D Hasegawa-Wakatani (HW) and modified Hasegawa-Wakatani (mHW) plasma turbulence models at 256² and 512² DNS resolutions using 2, 4 and 8 times smaller LES grids in each direction. We compare the model with DNS results on energy, enstrophy, spectra, probability distributions, poloidal and radial fluxes and computational cost. Validation extends to 1000 simulation time units for HW and 100 for mHW, whose faster dynamics lead toward laminarization. For the 256² cases, the finer LES meshes preserve several integral and spectral features, whereas the coarsest mesh yields unphysical behavior. In contrast, for the 512² cases, even the coarsest LES mesh preserves the turbulent character of the flow and maintains acceptable statistics for both the HW and mHW models. Moreover, we show how our methodology is superior to traditional algebraic numerical LES models, which traditionally fail to capture important design features. Finally, benchmarks show an MPI task-time reductions of 4.82--49.45, with a maximum wall-clock speedup of 1.55× when compared to full CPU socket.

分子晶体转变机器学习力场振动特性的准确性#

原题：Accuracy of Vibrational Properties of Machine-Learning Force Fields for Molecular Crystal Transformations
作者：Natalia Goncharova、Oskar Schweighofer、Johannes Hoja。arXiv · physics，2026-10-12

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：density functional theory。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

机器学习力场 (MLFF) 能够计算分子晶体中的转变路径，而其计算成本仅为密度泛函理论 (DFT) 的一小部分。然而，能量和结构的良好一致性并不一定意味着振动特性的准确描述，这对于表征过渡态和确定有限温度下的自由能至关重要。在此，我们评估了在 PBE+MBD 参考数据上训练的系统特定 SO3krates MLFF 的准确性，以描述分子晶体转变的振动特性。作为例子，我们考虑 1,4-二碘苯的多晶型转变和单晶到单晶的狄尔斯-阿尔德反应。使用 MLFF 进行固态微移弹性带 (NEB) 计算获得的过渡态结构表现出与相应变换相关的单个虚 Γ 点频率。与PBE+MBD相比，300 K时的γ点振动频率和相对谐波自由能的平均绝对误差分别约为3 cm-1和1 kJ/mol。此外，我们将 Diels-Alder 反应的集体转化途径与顺序途径进行比较，其中各个二烯-亲双烯体对连续反应，并证明 PBE0+MBD 单点校正可以显着影响预测的反应能量。这些结果表明，系统特定的MLFF不仅可以高精度地再现能量和力，还可以再现势能面的局部曲率，从而能够可靠地计算谐振自由能，为将分子晶体转变的研究扩展到更大的超晶胞提供了基础。

English Abstract

Machine-learning force fields (MLFFs) enable the calculation of transformation pathways in molecular crystals at a fraction of the computational cost of density functional theory (DFT). However, a good agreement in energies and structures does not necessarily imply an accurate description of vibrational properties, which are essential for characterizing transition states and determining free energies at finite temperatures. Herein, we assess the accuracy of system-specific SO3krates MLFFs trained on PBE+MBD reference data for describing vibrational properties of molecular crystal transformations. As examples, we consider a polymorph transition of 1,4-diiodobenzene and a single-crystal-to-single-crystal Diels-Alder reaction. The transition state structures obtained from solid-state nudged-elastic-band (NEB) calculations with the MLFFs exhibit a single imaginary Γ-point frequency associated with the respective transformation. Compared with PBE+MBD, the mean absolute errors of Γ-point vibrational frequencies and relative harmonic free energies at 300 K are about 3 cm⁻¹ and 1 kJ/mol, respectively. Furthermore, we compare for the Diels-Alder reaction a collective transformation pathway with a sequential one, in which individual diene-dienophile pairs react successively, and demonstrate that PBE0+MBD single-point corrections can significantly affect the predicted reaction energetics. These results demonstrate that system-specific MLFFs can reproduce not only the energies and forces but also the local curvature of the potential-energy surface with high accuracy, enabling reliable calculations of harmonic vibrational free energies, providing a basis for extending the studies of molecular crystal transformations to larger supercells.

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

热电技术将废热转化为电能，但寻找新的热电技术需要传输计算，这对于整个数据库来说成本太高。因此，搜索选择从电子态密度（DOS）中计算什么，越来越多地通过机器学习来预测，但这样的选择很少经过测试。我们对 DXMag 数据库中的所有 106,235 个 Heusler 结构进行了测试，筛选了 51,257 个密度泛函光谱和 54,978 个神经网络预测，并通过 3,625 个能带结构和输运计算来判断。源自 DOS 的塞贝克指标将大多数通过每项传输和稳定性测试的化合物排名在其总体中的前 1% 之内，并且 11 种已建立的 half Heusler 热电材料中的 10 种计算通过。预测的光谱失去了大部分带隙，光谱相似性指标没有注意到，但仍然将通过的化合物排名在顶部附近。该屏幕验证了 80 种计算候选者，其中 61 种没有报告测量结果。计算或预测，DOS 可以选择计算什么；仅能带结构表明是否存在能隙。

English Abstract

Thermoelectrics turn waste heat into electricity, but finding new ones requires transport calculations too costly for whole databases. Searches therefore choose what to calculate from the electronic density of states (DOS), increasingly predicted by machine learning, yet such choices are rarely tested. We test them on all 106,235 Heusler structures in the DXMag database, screening 51,257 on density-functional spectra and 54,978 on neural-network predictions, and judging both by 3,625 band-structure and transport calculations. A DOS-derived Seebeck indicator ranks most compounds passing every transport and stability test within the top 1% of their population, and ten of the eleven established half-Heusler thermoelectrics calculated pass. Predicted spectra lose most band gaps, unnoticed by spectral-similarity metrics, yet still rank passing compounds near the top. The screen certifies 80 computational candidates, 61 without reported measurements. Computed or predicted, the DOS can choose what to calculate; only a band structure shows whether a gap exists.

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

准确的日前光伏 (PV) 功率预测需要强大的时间建模并遵守物理光伏系统约束。纯粹的数据驱动模型通常会忽略底层的光伏转换过程，并可能产生超出光伏系统运行范围的预测。在本文中，我们提出了一种基于物理的状态空间框架，它将可微分 PV 转换模型与选择性状态空间主干（PowerMamba）相结合。我们的方法遵循物理残差分解。物理模块提供基于太阳几何、辐照度、温度影响和逆变器限制的结构化基线，而状态空间模型则从 24 小时范围内的数值天气预报 (NWP) 输入中学习残差修正。对一年公用事业规模光伏数据的实验表明，该方法显着提高了预测精度，与纯物理基线相比，RMSE 降低了 38.5%，MAE 降低了 45.8%，并且优于纯数据驱动模型。结果进一步表明，结合系统级物理提供了最大的性能提升，而额外的改进仅提供了边际改进。除了准确性之外，该模型还通过限制对物理可行性的预测来提高鲁棒性，减少在损坏的测量下不切实际的预测。

English Abstract

Accurate day-ahead photovoltaic (PV) power forecasting requires both strong temporal modeling and adherence to physical PV system constraints. Purely data-driven models often ignore the underlying PV conversion process and may produce predictions that are outside the operational range of a PV system. In this paper, we propose a physics-informed state space framework that combines a differentiable PV conversion model with a selective state space backbone (PowerMamba). Our approach follows a physics-residual decomposition. The physics module provides a structured baseline based on solar geometry, irradiance, temperature effects, and inverter limits, while the state space model learns residual corrections from numerical weather prediction (NWP) inputs over a 24-hour horizon. Experiments on one year of utility-scale PV data show that the proposed method significantly improves forecasting accuracy, reducing RMSE by 38.5% and MAE by 45.8% compared to a physics-only baseline, and outperforming a purely data-driven model. Results further show that incorporating system-level physics provides the largest performance gains, while additional refinements offer only marginal improvements. Beyond accuracy, the model improves robustness by constraining predictions toward physical feasibility, reducing unrealistic predictions under corrupted measurements.

用于 EUV 光刻曝光后烘烤模拟的全局实时光谱 Duhamel-Picard 方法和基于物理的神经算子#

原题：Global-in-time Spectral Duhamel-Picard Method for Post-Exposure Bake Simulation and Physics-Informed Neural Operators for EUV Lithography
作者：Doyun Kim、Mircea Dusa、Werner Gilijns、Philippe Leray。arXiv · physics，2026-10-16

📄 摘要（中文翻译）：我们提出了一种用于曝光后烘烤 (PEB) 模拟的具有多项式展开的全局时间光谱 Duhamel-Picard 方法。使用 Duhamel 原理将耦合酸猝灭剂反应扩散方程重新表述为非线性 Volterra 积分方程。完整的轨迹由有限多项式展开表示，时间系数场由安德森加速皮卡德迭代在烘焙间隔内确定，从而避免顺序时间行进。在 3-D PEB 模拟中，我们采用切比雪夫和勒让德时间基，同时使用相应的高斯求积规则评估杜哈梅尔积分。我们证明了所提出的方…

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

我们提出了一种用于曝光后烘烤 (PEB) 模拟的具有多项式展开的全局时间光谱 Duhamel-Picard 方法。使用 Duhamel 原理将耦合酸猝灭剂反应扩散方程重新表述为非线性 Volterra 积分方程。完整的轨迹由有限多项式展开表示，时间系数场由安德森加速皮卡德迭代在烘焙间隔内确定，从而避免顺序时间行进。在 3-D PEB 模拟中，我们采用切比雪夫和勒让德时间基，同时使用相应的高斯求积规则评估杜哈梅尔积分。我们证明了所提出的方法可以在一分钟的时间内模拟 1200×1200×100 体素的大域。该表示进一步被合并到具有因式分解傅里叶神经算子的混合变换变体的物理信息神经算子（PINO）中，该算子在没有预先计算解轨迹的情况下进行训练。积分公式及其 Pino 扩展共同实现了烘烤间隔内任意时间的浓度场的加速、无时间步进预测。

English Abstract

We present a global-in-time spectral Duhamel-Picard method with polynomial expansions for post-exposure bake (PEB) simulation. The coupled acid-quencher reaction-diffusion equations are reformulated as nonlinear Volterra integral equations using the Duhamel principle. Full trajectories are represented by finite polynomial expansions, with temporal coefficient fields determined over the bake interval by Anderson-accelerated Picard iteration, thereby avoiding sequential time marching. In 3-D PEB simulations, we employ Chebyshev and Legendre temporal bases while evaluating the Duhamel integrals using the corresponding Gaussian quadrature rules. We demonstrate that the proposed method simulates a large domain of 1200×1200×100 voxels on the order of one minute. The representation is further incorporated into a physics-informed neural operator (PINO) with a mixed-transform variant of a factorized Fourier neural operator, which is trained without precomputed solution trajectories. Together, the integral formulation and its PINO extension enable accelerated, time-marching-free prediction of concentration fields at arbitrary times within the bake interval.

使大型语言模型代理能够进行高保真多体扰动理论计算#

原题：Enabling large language model agents for high-fidelity many-body perturbation theory calculations
作者：Benran Zhang、Byeongchan Lee、Zien Zhu、Chih-En Hsu、Zhenfa Zheng、Hung-Chung Hsueh 等 9 人。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-10-16

📄 摘要（中文翻译）：多体扰动理论（ MBPT ）方法，包括GW近似、GW加Bethe-Salpeter方程（ GW-BSE ）方法和GW扰动理论（ GWPT ）等，为材料的激发态性质和多粒子相互作用提供了准确的第一原理描述。然而，由于复杂的多软件工作流程、相互依赖的物理参数和大量的理论专业知识，它们的使用仍然有限。大型语言模型（ LLM ）代理将语言模型与软件工具相结合，显示出自动化计算任务的潜力，但MBPT仍然是一个未解决的挑战。我们表明，在我们的工作中…

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

多体扰动理论（ MBPT ）方法，包括GW近似、GW加Bethe-Salpeter方程（ GW-BSE ）方法和GW扰动理论（ GWPT ）等，为材料的激发态性质和多粒子相互作用提供了准确的第一原理描述。然而，由于复杂的多软件工作流程、相互依赖的物理参数和大量的理论专业知识，它们的使用仍然有限。大型语言模型（ LLM ）代理将语言模型与软件工具相结合，显示出自动化计算任务的潜力，但MBPT仍然是一个未解决的挑战。我们表明，在我们的工作中测试的LLM代理（包括Opus 4.7和GPT-5.5等前沿模型）一次无法可靠地构建MBPT工作流程，这表明正确的设置需要专业知识和当前模型缺乏的一致性。在测试的模型-线束组合中， GW、GW-BSE和GWPT工作流程基准的开箱即用代理平均为54%、36%和19% ，具有很强的模型依赖性和较大的运行间差异。错误通常源于跨阶段的不一致性，这些不一致性在引入后仅出现几个阶段。因此，我们开发了AI-Donkey ，这是一个代理框架，将专家策划的知识与确定性执行相结合，以实现高保真GW、GW-BSE和GWPT计算和分析。借助AI-Donkey ，所有经过测试的代理，从前沿专有模型到开放式权重模型，都可以生成完整、可运行的MBPT工作流程，没有观察到运行间差异。我们进一步演示了1D、2D和体材料的自主计算和后处理，包括极性半导体中Fröhlich电子-声子耦合的多体增强。我们的方法为知识工程提供了一个通用框架，以实现计算材料科学中的可靠代理工作流程。

English Abstract

Many-body perturbation theory (MBPT) methods, including the GW approximation, the GW plus Bethe-Salpeter equation (GW-BSE) method, and GW perturbation theory (GWPT), among others, provide accurate first-principles descriptions of excited-state properties and multiparticle interactions of materials. However, their use remains limited due to complex multi-software workflows, interdependent physical parameters, and substantial theoretical expertise. Large language model (LLM) agents, which couple a language model to software tools, show potential in automating computational tasks, but MBPT remains an unaddressed challenge. We show that LLM agents tested in our work, including frontier models such as Opus 4.7 and GPT-5.5, do not reliably construct MBPT workflows in one attempt, suggesting that correct setup requires expert knowledge and consistency that current models lack. Across tested model-harness combinations, out-of-the-box agents average 54%, 36%, and 19% on GW, GW-BSE, and GWPT workflow benchmarks, with strong model dependence and large run-to-run variation. Errors typically originate from cross-stage inconsistencies that surface only several stages after they are introduced. We therefore develop AI-Donkey, an agentic framework that combines expert-curated knowledge with deterministic execution to enable high-fidelity GW, GW-BSE, and GWPT calculations and analyses. With AI-Donkey, all tested agents, from frontier proprietary models to open-weight models, produce complete, runnable MBPT workflows with no observed run-to-run variation. We further demonstrate autonomous calculations and post-processing on 1D, 2D, and bulk materials, including the many-body enhancement of Fröhlich electron-phonon coupling in polar semiconductors. Our approach provides a general framework for knowledge engineering to enable reliable agentic workflows in computational materials science.

MolP2SBench：法学硕士真的能从分子性质推理到结构吗？#

原题：MolP2SBench: Do LLMs Really Reason from Molecular Properties to Structure?
作者：Yuhang Xie、Zhiyi Shi、Zeyi Zhang、Zhisheng Qi、Yushun Dong、Yu Wang 等 7 人。arXiv · physics，2026-10-12
🤖 AI/机器学习
AI×科学交叉8方向匹配0

📄 摘要（中文翻译）：大型语言模型 (LLM) 越来越多地用于分子发现和结构阐明，其中决策取决于物理观察。现有的评估主要集中于端到端的分子重建，其中正确的预测并不一定表明对所提供的属性的依赖。我们介绍 MolP2SBench，这是法学硕士中分子性质到结构 (P2S) 推理的基准。每项任务都会呈现一个具有给定特性的掩蔽分子支架，并要求模型从合理的候选结构中选择缺失的结构。我们对属性进行洗牌或替换，以测试对正确属性-结构配对的依赖以及对反事实属性变化的响应能力。…

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

大型语言模型 (LLM) 越来越多地用于分子发现和结构阐明，其中决策取决于物理观察。现有的评估主要集中于端到端的分子重建，其中正确的预测并不一定表明对所提供的属性的依赖。我们介绍 MolP2SBench，这是法学硕士中分子性质到结构 (P2S) 推理的基准。每项任务都会呈现一个具有给定特性的掩蔽分子支架，并要求模型从合理的候选结构中选择缺失的结构。我们对属性进行洗牌或替换，以测试对正确属性-结构配对的依赖以及对反事实属性变化的响应能力。渠道明智的排行榜使用基于属性的恢复（PGR），对源恢复和反事实恢复给予同等的权重。通过评估七个模型，我们发现法学硕士可以使用物理属性进行结构预测，但不同属性类型的性能差异很大。某个属性的更高准确度并不一定意味着对其的响应能力更强：模型从 1H NMR 中获益可能比从 13C NMR 中获益更多，但当 1H 数据被替换时，模型不太可能改变其预测。法学硕士的表现也不必反映这些特性对人类化学家来说的相对难度。尽管 UV-Vis 光谱提供的直接结构信息比 NMR 光谱少，但 Astra 使用 UV-Vis 数据比使用 NMR 峰实现了更高的准确性。 MolP2SBench 支持研究法学硕士如何提取和使用物理性质信息，揭示可能不同于传统化学直觉的模式，并为分子和量子化学推理的更可靠的基于性质的模型提供基础。代码和数据集：https://github.com/DUAL-Xiao/MolP2SBench。

English Abstract

Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used in molecular discovery and structure elucidation, where decisions depend on physical observations. Existing evaluations primarily focus on end-to-end molecular reconstruction, where correct predictions do not necessarily indicate reliance on the supplied properties. We introduce MolP2SBench, a benchmark for molecular property-to-structure (P2S) reasoning in LLMs. Each task presents a masked molecular scaffold with given properties and asks the model to select the missing structure from plausible candidates. We shuffle or replace properties to test reliance on correct property-structure pairing and responsiveness to counterfactual property changes. A channel-wise leaderboard uses Property-Grounded Recovery (PGR), giving equal weight to source and counterfactual recovery. Evaluating seven models, we find that LLMs can use physical properties for structure prediction, but performance varies substantially across property types. Higher accuracy with a property does not necessarily imply greater responsiveness to it: a model may benefit more from ¹H NMR than from ¹³C NMR, yet be less likely to change its prediction when the ¹H data is replaced. LLM performance also need not reflect the relative difficulty of these properties for human chemists. Although UV-Vis spectra provide less direct structural information than NMR spectra, Astra achieves higher accuracy with UV-Vis data than with NMR peaks. MolP2SBench supports studying how LLMs extract and use physical-property information, revealing patterns that may differ from conventional chemical intuition and providing a foundation for more reliably property-grounded models for molecular and quantum-chemical inference. Code and dataset: https://github.com/DUAL-Xiao/MolP2SBench.

大语言模型辅助异常吸附现象的系统研究#

原题：Large Language Model-Assisted Systematic Study of Anomalous Adsorption Phenomena
作者：Fengxu Yang、Matthew L. Schneider、Weitong Chen、Jack D. Evans。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-10-16
🤖 AI/机器学习

📄 摘要（中文翻译）：软多孔晶体可以经历客体诱导的结构转变，产生非常规的吸附等温线，这对于气体分离和存储应用很有价值。然而，这些转变的证据以叙述形式分散在文献中，并且没有被汇总。在这里，我们使用大型语言模型管道来挖掘异常吸附的文献，提取相变事件以及材料、吸附物、温度和压力元数据，并将每个事件分类为已建立的行为类别。以人类注释为基准，提取阶段的 F1 分数为 0.96。当大规模应用时，该管道会生成一个包含 240 种不同材料的 547 个转变的精选语料库，这些…

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

软多孔晶体可以经历客体诱导的结构转变，产生非常规的吸附等温线，这对于气体分离和存储应用很有价值。然而，这些转变的证据以叙述形式分散在文献中，并且没有被汇总。在这里，我们使用大型语言模型管道来挖掘异常吸附的文献，提取相变事件以及材料、吸附物、温度和压力元数据，并将每个事件分类为已建立的行为类别。以人类注释为基准，提取阶段的 F1 分数为 0.96。当大规模应用时，该管道会生成一个包含 240 种不同材料的 547 个转变的精选语料库，这些语料库取自 4,252 篇经过筛选的出版物中的 200 篇，我们进一步将其与晶体结构联系起来。对该数据库的分析表明，主要的行为类别（开门和呼吸）通过其功能反应来区分。开门框架在比呼吸框架低得多的压力下发生转变 (p = 3.8E-3)。相比之下，在不同类别之间没有检测到孔径、空隙率、连接化学或金属特性的差异。我们的研究结果支持对响应性多孔材料进行功能性而非纯粹结构性的描述。他们还强调需要一个社区数据库来记录这些材料每次报告的转变的客人、温度和压力。

English Abstract

Soft porous crystals can undergo guest-induced structural transitions producing unconventional adsorption isotherms, which are valuable for gas separation and storage applications. However, the evidence for these transitions is scattered across the literature in narrative form and has not been aggregated. Here we use a large language model pipeline to mine the literature for anomalous adsorption, extracting phase-transition events together with material, adsorbate, temperature and pressure metadata and classifying each into an established behavioural class. Benchmarked against human annotation, the extraction stage attains an F1 score of 0.96. When applied at scale, the pipeline yields a curated corpus of 547 transitions across 240 distinct materials, drawn from 200 of 4,252 screened publications, which we further link to crystallographic structures. Analysis of this database shows that the principal behavioural classes, gate-opening and breathing, are distinguished by their functional response. Gate-opening frameworks transform at a significantly lower pressure than breathing frameworks (p = 3.8E-3). In contrast, no difference in pore size, void fraction, linker chemistry or metal identity was detected between the classes. Our findings argue for a functional rather than purely structural description of responsive porous materials. They also highlight the need for a community database that records the guest, temperature and pressure of every reported transition of these materials.

可以训练全晶体管物理学习变压器来执行上下文学习#

原题：An all-transistor physical-learning transformer can be trained to perform in-context learning
作者：Michael C. Abbott、Benjamin B. Machta。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-10-12

📄 摘要（中文翻译）：大多数通过平衡传播或耦合学习训练的系统与多层感知器属于同一类：虽然它们可能是通用函数逼近器，但它们很难学习对于变换器来说很容易的关联任务。平衡传播依赖于输入和输出之间通过物理系统的双向信息流。反向流被更复杂的非线性系统打破，例如那些执行表达点积注意力所需的乘法的系统。在这里，我们通过为此目的将额外的电路元件引入双或二元电路来明确地恢复这种双向流。然后，我们利用这些想法构建了一个利用亚阈值 MOSFET 器件的指数电流定律的注意力机制。我…

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

大多数通过平衡传播或耦合学习训练的系统与多层感知器属于同一类：虽然它们可能是通用函数逼近器，但它们很难学习对于变换器来说很容易的关联任务。平衡传播依赖于输入和输出之间通过物理系统的双向信息流。反向流被更复杂的非线性系统打破，例如那些执行表达点积注意力所需的乘法的系统。在这里，我们通过为此目的将额外的电路元件引入双或二元电路来明确地恢复这种双向流。然后，我们利用这些想法构建了一个利用亚阈值 MOSFET 器件的指数电流定律的注意力机制。我们表明，在模拟中，尽管使用仅传输近似梯度的真实反向组件，但可以训练该电路来执行简单的上下文学习任务。为了使训练成功，我们需要交叉熵损失（不是均方误差）。我们使用一种新颖的浮动电路元件生成适合这种损失的微调，该元件基于用于 softmax 的相同 MOSFET 子电路。我们使用 Lion 优化器训练该电路中的电阻器，可以使用每个参数一个电容器来存储过去梯度估计的本地存储器来实现。

English Abstract

Most systems trained by equilibrium propagation or coupled learning are in the same class as multi-layer perceptrons: while they may be universal function approximators, they struggle to learn associative tasks which are easy for transformers. Equilibrium propagation relies on a bidirectional flow of information through the physical system between input and output. The reverse flow is broken by more complex nonlinear systems, such as those performing multiplication which is needed to express dot-product attention. Here we explicitly restore this bidirectional flow by introducing extra circuit elements for this purpose into a double or dyadic circuit. We then use these ideas to construct an attention mechanism exploiting the exponential current law of sub-threshold MOSFET devices. We show that in simulations this circuit can be trained to perform a simple in-context learning task, despite using realistic reverse components which transmit only an approximate gradient. For this training to succeed we need cross-entropy loss (not mean squared error). We generate nudges appropriate for this loss using a novel floating circuit element, based on the same MOSFET sub-circuit used for softmax. We train the resistors in this circuit using the Lion optimiser, which can be implemented using one capacitor per parameter to store local memory of past gradient estimates.

HIP ：无衍生物的黑森原子间势#

原题：HIP: Hessian Interatomic Potentials without derivatives
作者：Andreas Burger、Luca Thiede、Nikolaj Rønne、Nandita Vijaykumar、Varinia Bernales、Tejs Vegge 等 8 人。Nature Computational Science，2026-10-13
🤖 AI/机器学习
AI×科学交叉6方向匹配8

📄 摘要（中文翻译）：作者引入了直接预测分子Hessian的机器学习原子间势的扩展，与自动微分相比，在过渡态搜索、几何优化和振动分析中提高了准确性和速度。

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：interatomic potential、graph neural network。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

作者引入了直接预测分子Hessian的机器学习原子间势的扩展，与自动微分相比，在过渡态搜索、几何优化和振动分析中提高了准确性和速度。

English Abstract

Molecular Hessians, the second derivatives of the potential energy, are fundamental to many workflows in computational chemistry. Usually, accurate Hessians are computationally expensive to calculate and scale poorly with system size, whether computed using quantum chemistry methods or machine learning interatomic potentials. Here we introduce Hessian Interatomic Potentials (HIPs), a deep learning model that directly predicts Hessians without relying on automatic differentiation or finite differences. To do so, we construct SE(3)-equivariant, symmetric Hessians from irreducible representation (irrep) features up to degreel= 2, computed by a graph neural network. HIP Hessians are one to two orders of magnitude faster, more accurate, more memory efficient, easier to train and exhibit favorable scaling with system size. We validate our predictions across a wide range of downstream tasks, demonstrating consistently superior performance in transition state search, geometry optimization, zero-point energy corrections and vibrational analysis. We open source the HIP code and model weights.

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：interatomic potential、molecular dynamics。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

机器学习原子间势 (MLIP) 通过学习控制原子运动的能量和力来加速分子动力学 (MD) 模拟。最近的工作表明，放松物理归纳偏差可以提高计算效率和预测准确性，从而提出了仅能量和力匹配是否足以确保准确采样的问题。我们表明，对于无约束的假设类，预测误差和采样精度之间的关系在维度上呈指数病态。特别是，随着维度呈指数衰减的预测误差可以与学习分布和目标分布之间接近最大总变异距离共存。我们确定了两种故障机制。第一个是动力学捕获，即使从能量和力完全匹配的训练配置初始化模拟，松弛到准确的学习平衡也需要指数级的长时间。第二个是平衡不匹配，即学习到的平稳分布本身与目标相差甚远。在明确的假设下，我们表明，即使在局部对称性保持模型中，平衡失配也可能持续存在，其中每个原子的小误差与随着系统规模的增加而接近其最大值的分布差异共存。最后，我们建立了全局能量控制条件，以保证平衡精度并确定快速松弛所需的附加条件。

English Abstract

Machine-learned interatomic potentials (MLIPs) accelerate molecular dynamics (MD) simulations by learning the energies and forces that govern atomic motion. Recent work has shown that relaxing physical inductive biases can improve computational efficiency and predictive accuracy, raising the question of whether energy-and-force matching alone is sufficient to ensure accurate sampling. We show that, for unconstrained hypothesis classes, the relationship between prediction error and sampling accuracy is exponentially ill-conditioned in dimension. In particular, prediction errors that decay exponentially with dimension can coexist with nearly maximal total variation distance between the learned and target distributions. We identify two failure mechanisms. The first is kinetic trapping, where relaxation to an accurate learned equilibrium requires exponentially long times, even when simulations are initialized from training configurations at which energies and forces are matched exactly. The second is equilibrium mismatch, where the learned stationary distribution itself remains far from the target. Under explicit assumptions, we show that equilibrium mismatch can persist even in local, symmetry-preserving models, where small per-atom errors coexist with a distributional discrepancy that approaches its maximum as system size increases. Finally, we establish global energy-control conditions that guarantee equilibrium accuracy and identify additional conditions required for rapid relaxation.

Half-Heusler 热电的通用机器学习框架#

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

机器学习 (ML) 已成为加速热电 (TE) 材料发现的强大方法，但许多模型的通用性有限。在这里，我们提出了一个强大的机器学习框架，用于预测 half-Heusler (hH) 化合物的热电品质因数 (zT)，强调预测以前未见过的成分。引入基于主成分分析（PCA）的训练/测试分割策略来生成化学空间的代表性分区。贝叶斯超参数优化与随机森林、XGBoost 和神经网络模型的基于相关性的 k 最佳特征选择以及可解释的 SISSO 符号回归方法相结合。相对于全套 118 个输入特征，优化后的特征子集将测试 RMSE 降低了 16-27%。 SHAP 和 SISSO 分析将温度、A 位掺杂剂浓度和 A 位汽化热确定为 zT 最重要的描述符。该框架通过预测先前未探索的成分、恢复文献报道的已建立 hH 系统的最佳 zT 值以及新化合物 CeCoBi 和 CeIrBi 的第一原理计算来验证，这些化合物表现出良好的电子传输特性。该框架进一步应用于掺杂 hH 化学空间的高通量筛选，覆盖了稳定性约束下的 631 个热力学筛选母体中约 246 万个元素取代组合。这种筛选产生了一组简明的有前途的候选者供未来研究。总体而言，这项工作为可靠的数据驱动热电材料发现建立了一个通用的工作流程。

English Abstract

Machine learning (ML) has emerged as a powerful approach for accelerating the discovery of thermoelectric (TE) materials, yet many models suffer from limited generalizability. Here we present a robust ML framework for predicting the thermoelectric figure of merit (zT) in half-Heusler (hH) compounds, emphasizing prediction of previously unseen compositions. A principal component analysis (PCA)-based train/test splitting strategy is introduced to generate representative partitions of chemical space. Bayesian hyperparameter optimization is combined with correlation-based k-best feature selection for Random Forest, XGBoost, and Neural Network models, together with the interpretable SISSO symbolic-regression method. Relative to the full set of 118 input features, the optimized feature subsets reduce the test RMSE by 16-27%. SHAP and SISSO analyses identify temperature, A-site dopant concentration, and A-site heat of vaporization as the most important descriptors of zT. The framework is validated through prediction of previously unexplored compositions, recovery of literature-reported optimal zT values for established hH systems, and first-principles calculations for the new compounds CeCoBi and CeIrBi, which exhibit favorable electronic transport properties. The framework is further applied to high-throughput screening of the doped hH chemical space, covering about 2.46 million elemental substitution combinations across 631 thermodynamically screened parent matrices under stability constraints. This screening yields a concise set of promising candidates for future study. Overall, the work establishes a generalizable workflow for reliable data-driven thermoelectric materials discovery.

过渡金属硫族化物纳米线：稳定性、电子结构和机械响应的第一性原理研究#

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：electronic structure、density functional theory、molecular dynamics。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

亚纳米过渡金属硫族化物纳米线提供了一个一维平台，可以通过化学成分调节电子功能和机械响应。在这里，密度泛函理论计算与机器学习力场 (MLFF) 分子动力学相结合，研究 MoS、MoSe、MoTe、WS、WSe 和 WTe 纳米线。优化的结构在谐波近似内是动态稳定的，并且它们的电子特性在硫属元素系列中系统地演变：MoS、MoSe、WS和WSe是金属性的，而MoTe和WTe是半导体性的，HSE06带隙分别为0.52和0.20 eV。有效轴向刚度从硫化物到硒化物和碲化物逐渐减小，这与金属-硫族键长的增加一致；使用圆形有效截面，相应的杨氏模量范围为 392 至 718 GPa。相对于所选元素参考相，所有六种纳米线均具有负形成能。 MLFF 轨迹在 300 K 下延伸至 2.5 ns，保留了一维形态和局部原子序，为模拟时间尺度上的有限温度动态稳定性提供了证据。相应的 MLFF 分子动力学能量迹线如图所示。我们进一步研究了纳米线对轴向应变和曲率的响应，包括碲化物中应变引起的带隙调制。这些结果将成分和机械变形确定为调节超薄过渡金属硫族化物纳米线的电子和机械性能的补充途径，并支持它们作为柔性纳米电子系统组件的进一步研究。

English Abstract

Subnanometer transition-metal chalcogenide nanowires provide a one-dimensional platform in which electronic functionality and mechanical response can be tuned through chemical composition. Here, density functional theory calculations are combined with machine-learning force-field (MLFF) molecular dynamics to investigate MoS, MoSe, MoTe, WS, WSe, and WTe nanowires. The optimized structures are dynamically stable within the harmonic approximation, and their electronic character evolves systematically across the chalcogen series: MoS, MoSe, WS, and WSe are metallic, whereas MoTe and WTe are semiconducting, with HSE06 band gaps of 0.52 and 0.20 eV, respectively. The effective axial stiffness decreases from sulfides to selenides and tellurides, consistent with the increase in metal--chalcogen bond length; using a circular effective cross section, the corresponding Young moduli range from 392 to 718 GPa. All six nanowires have negative formation energies relative to the selected elemental reference phases. MLFF trajectories extending to 2.5 ns at 300 K preserve the one-dimensional morphology and local atomic order, providing evidence of finite-temperature dynamical stability on the simulated time scale. The corresponding MLFF molecular-dynamics energy traces are shown in. We further examine the response of the nanowires to axial strain and curvature, including strain-induced band-gap modulation in the tellurides. These results establish composition and mechanical deformation as complementary routes for tuning the electronic and mechanical properties of ultrathin transition-metal chalcogenide nanowires and support their further investigation as components of flexible nanoelectronic systems.

通过神经网络预测状态间碰撞率系数#

原题：Predicted state-to-state collisional rate coefficients from neural networks
作者：Lisán David Cabrera-González、Carlos Cárdenas、Otoniel Denis-Alpizar。arXiv · physics，2026-10-12

📄 摘要（中文翻译）：在星际介质中观察到的分子光谱通常使用非局域热力学平衡模型进行分析，这需要精确的状态间碰撞速率系数。对于从高激发旋转态的转变，紧耦合计算变得极其昂贵，因此可用的数据集通常在较低的初始状态和温度下被截断；而且由于每个新系统还需要精确的从头算势能表面，因此许多检测到的分子没有碰撞数据。我们采用两种机器学习策略来预测训练集中包含的分子的旋转状态速率系数，超出迄今为止计算的初始状态和温度范围，并预测训练和验证集中不存在的分子的旋转状态速率系数。前…

页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

在星际介质中观察到的分子光谱通常使用非局域热力学平衡模型进行分析，这需要精确的状态间碰撞速率系数。对于从高激发旋转态的转变，紧耦合计算变得极其昂贵，因此可用的数据集通常在较低的初始状态和温度下被截断；而且由于每个新系统还需要精确的从头算势能表面，因此许多检测到的分子没有碰撞数据。我们采用两种机器学习策略来预测训练集中包含的分子的旋转状态速率系数，超出迄今为止计算的初始状态和温度范围，并预测训练和验证集中不存在的分子的旋转状态速率系数。前馈神经网络根据与对氢碰撞的 24 个线性分子的状态到状态去激发速率系数进行训练。方案 1 包括训练+验证集中的所有系统，具有 70%、80% 和 90% 初始状态边界以及 90% 温度边界。方案 2 在仅通过转变和分子特性描述的 22 个系统上进行训练，并预测两个排除的系统 CS 和 CF+，其天体物理影响通过非 LTE 计算进行探索。对于 90% 边界，方案 1 预测 99.4% 的测试转换在 2 倍以内，并且没有预测与参考值的偏差超过 4.7 倍。该模型用于完成 18 个数据集。方案 2 将两个排除系统的速率系数再现为 3 倍，其中超过 92% 的转变。两种方案都为紧密耦合计算提供了近乎瞬时的补充：方案 1 以高精度扩展了训练集中已包含的分子的范围，而方案 2 则对尚未研究的分子给出了合理的初步估计。

English Abstract

Molecular spectra observed in the interstellar medium are commonly analyzed using non-local thermodynamic equilibrium models, which requires accurate state-to-state collisional rate coefficients. Close-coupling calculations become prohibitively expensive for transitions from highly excited rotational states, therefore available datasets are often truncated at low initial states and temperatures; and since each new system also requires an accurate ab initio potential energy surface, many detected molecules have no collisional data. We employ two machine learning strategies to predict rotational state-to-state rate coefficients of molecules included in the train set beyond the range of initial states and temperatures computed so far, and predicting those of a molecule absent from training and validation sets. Feed-forward neural networks are trained on state-to-state de-excitation rate coefficients of 24 linear molecules colliding with para-H2. Scheme 1 includes all systems in the train+validation set, with 70%, 80%, and 90% initial-state boundaries and a 90% temperature boundary. Scheme 2 is trained on 22 systems described only by transition and molecular properties, and predicts two excluded systems, CS and CF+, whose astrophysical impact is explored with non-LTE calculations. For the 90% boundary, Scheme 1 predicts 99.4% of the test transitions within a factor of 2, and no prediction deviates from the reference value by more than a factor of 4.7. This model was used to complete 18 datasets. Scheme 2 reproduces the rate coefficients of two excluded systems within a factor of 3 for more than 92% of their transitions. Both schemes provide near-instantaneous complements to close-coupling calculations: Scheme 1 extends the range of a molecule already included in the training set with high accuracy, while Scheme 2 gives a reasonable first estimate for a molecule that has not been studied.

真实空间电荷密度和泛化的神经网络解决方案#

原题：Neural-Network Solutions to Real-Space Charge Density and Generalization
作者：Yuxuan Zeng、Tong Xie、Taoyuze Lv、Zhicheng Zhong。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-10-15

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：electronic structure calculation、density functional theory。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

Hohenberg-Kohn 定理规定，原则上，基态 (GS) 电荷密度包含多电子系统的所有 GS 信息，因此所有 GS 可观测量都可以表示为 GS 电荷密度的函数。传统的 Kohn-Sham 密度泛函理论需要以大量计算成本迭代求解自洽场方程，这推动了电子结构计算深度学习替代方法的开发，进而加速了计算机辅助材料设计。在这里，我们提出了 AIDEN，一种用于求解真实空间电荷密度的原子相互作用密度等变网络。 AIDEN 将元素相关的单中心密度与环境引起的密度重新分布分开，并通过互补的原子和边缘中心张量相关性来表示后者。然后，连续低秩高斯解码器重建任意空间坐标处的密度，同时独立于评估网格重新使用原子编码。 AIDEN 在周期性晶体基准上实现了最先进的精度，同时在分子系统方面保持竞争力，并进一步证明了在几个结构不同的分布外案例研究中的零样本可转移性。此外，AIDEN 提供比基线模型和完整 SCF 计算快得多的推理速度，从而实现大规模电子结构计算的高效电荷密度重建。

English Abstract

The Hohenberg-Kohn theorem establishes that, in principle, the ground state (GS) charge density contains all GS information of a many-electron system, such that all GS observables can be expressed as functionals of the GS charge density. Conventional Kohn-Sham density functional theory requires iterative solution of the self-consistent-field equations at substantial computational cost, motivating the development of deep learning surrogates for electronic structure calculations and, in turn, accelerating computer-aided materials design. Here, we propose AIDEN, an Atomic-Interaction Density Equivariant Network for solving real-space charge density. AIDEN separates the element-dependent one-center density from environment-induced density redistribution and represents the latter through complementary atom- and edge-centered tensor correlations. A continuous low-rank Gaussian decoder then reconstructs the density at arbitrary spatial coordinates while reusing atomic encodings independently of the evaluation grid. AIDEN achieves state-of-the-art accuracy on periodic crystal benchmarks while remaining competitive for molecular systems, and further demonstrates zero-shot transferability across several structurally distinct out-of-distribution case studies. Furthermore, AIDEN provides substantially faster inference than both baseline models and full SCF calculations, enabling efficient charge density reconstruction for large-scale electronic structure calculations.
